[31m+ // ── Shared KPI cache — one fetch for all KPI strips ──────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ let _kpiCache: { data: Record<string, unknown>; ts: number } | null = null;[39m
[31m+ const KPI_CACHE_TTL = 60_000; // 1 minute[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function fetchKpisV2(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {[39m
[31m+   if (_kpiCache && Date.now() - _kpiCache.ts < KPI_CACHE_TTL) return _kpiCache.data;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const res = await fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2", { credentials: "include" });[39m
[31m+     if (!res.ok) return _kpiCache?.data ?? {};[39m
[31m+     const data = await res.json();[39m
[31m+     _kpiCache = { data, ts: Date.now() };[39m
[31m+     return data;[39m
[31m+   } catch {[39m
[31m+     return _kpiCache?.data ?? {};[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── InlineKPIStrip — compact KPI bar embedded in Resumen ─────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function InlineKPIStrip() {[39m
[31m+   const [kpis, setKpis] = useState<{[39m
[31m+     ventasHoy: number; ticketPromedio: number; margen: number; clientesHoy: number; fiadoPendiente: number; rotacion: number;[39m
[31m+   } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       const d = await fetchKpisV2();[39m
[31m+       if (!d || Object.keys(d).length === 0) return;[39m
[31m+       setKpis({[39m
[31m+         ventasHoy: Number(d.ingresosHoy ?? d.ventasHoy ?? d.salesDay ?? 0) || 0,[39m
[31m+         ticketPromedio: Number(d.ticketPromedio ?? d.avgTicket ?? 0) || 0,[39m
[31m+         margen: Number(d.margenOperativo ?? d.marginPct ?? 0) || 0,[39m
[31m+         clientesHoy: Number(d.clientesActivos ?? d.clientesHoy ?? d.customersToday ?? 0) || 0,[39m
[31m+         fiadoPendiente: Number(d.fiadoPendiente ?? d.fiadoTotal ?? 0) || 0,[39m
[31m+         rotacion: Number(d.rotacionInventario ?? d.inventoryTurnover ?? 0) || 0,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!kpis) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-2 mb-4">[39m
[31m+         {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={i} className="h-16 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const items = [[39m
[31m+     { label: "Ventas hoy", value: `S/ ${kpis.ventasHoy.toFixed(0)}`, color: "text-emerald-600" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Ticket prom", value: `S/ ${kpis.ticketPromedio.toFixed(0)}`, color: "text-emerald-600" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Margen", value: `${kpis.margen.toFixed(1)}%`, color: kpis.margen >= 20 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-amber-600" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Clientes hoy", value: String(kpis.clientesHoy), color: "text-purple-600" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Fiado pend.", value: `S/ ${kpis.fiadoPendiente.toFixed(0)}`, color: kpis.fiadoPendiente > 0 ? "text-red-500" : "text-gray-500" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Rotación", value: `${kpis.rotacion.toFixed(1)}x`, color: "text-cyan-600" },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m

[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           key={item.label}[39m
[31m+           className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5  text-center"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 mb-0.5">{item.label}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-lg font-extrabold tabular-nums", item.color)} style={{ fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>{item.value}</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ))}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m

[31m+ // ── TabbedCard — card con tabs internas para unificar componentes ─────────────[39m
[31m+ function TabbedCard({ title, subtitle, icon, tabs, className }: {[39m
[31m+   title: string;[39m
[31m+   subtitle?: string;[39m
[31m+   icon?: typeof BarChart3;[39m

[31m+         <div className="flex gap-1 border-b border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+           {tabs.map((t) => ([39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               key={t.id}[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => setActiveTab(t.id)}[39m
[31m+               className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-t-lg transition-colors",[39m
[31m+                 activeTab === t.id[39m
[31m+                   ? "bg-primary/10 text-primary border-b-2 border-primary"[39m
[31m+                   : "text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50"[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {t.label}[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div className="w-full">[39m
[31m+         {tabs.find(t => t.id === activeTab)?.content}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Top 10 Clientes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function Top10Clientes({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {[39m
[31m+   const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Array<{[39m
[31m+     id: string; nombre: string; gastoTotal: number; compras: number; ticketPromedio: number; tendencia: number;[39m
[31m+   }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const res = await fetch("/api/customers?sort=totalSpent&limit=10", { credentials: "include" });[39m
[31m+         if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+           const data = await res.json();[39m
[31m+           const list = (Array.isArray(data) ? data : data.customers ?? data.data ?? []).slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+           setCustomers(list.map((c: Record<string, unknown>) => ({[39m
[31m+             id: String(c.id ?? ""),[39m
[31m+             nombre: String(c.name ?? c.nombre ?? c.customerName ?? "Cliente"),[39m
[31m+             gastoTotal: Number(c.totalSpent ?? c.gastoTotal ?? c.total ?? 0),[39m
[31m+             compras: Number(c.orderCount ?? c.compras ?? c.totalOrders ?? 0),[39m
[31m+             ticketPromedio: Number(c.avgTicket ?? c.ticketPromedio ?? 0) || (Number(c.totalSpent ?? 0) / Math.max(1, Number(c.orderCount ?? 1))),[39m
[31m+             tendencia: Number(c.trend ?? c.tendencia ?? 0),[39m
[31m+           })));[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch { /* silent */ }[39m
[31m+       finally { setLoading(false); }[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+   }, [refreshKey]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+         {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={i} className="h-12 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const medals = ["\u{1F947}", "\u{1F948}", "\u{1F949}"];[39m
[31m+   const formatCurrency = (n: number) => `S/${n.toFixed(2)}`;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+       {customers.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-8">No hay datos de clientes disponibles</p>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <thead>[39m
[31m+             <tr className="border-b border-gray-200 text-left">[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-500 text-xs">#</th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-500 text-xs">Cliente</th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-500 text-xs text-right">Gasto total</th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-500 text-xs text-right hidden sm:table-cell">Compras</th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-500 text-xs text-right hidden md:table-cell">Ticket prom</th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-500 text-xs text-center">Tendencia</th>[39m
[31m+             </tr>[39m
[31m+           </thead>[39m
[31m+           <tbody>[39m
[31m+             {customers.map((c, i) => ([39m
[31m+               <tr key={c.id} className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                 <td className="px-3 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                   {i < 3 ? <span className="text-lg">{medals[i]}</span> : <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">{i + 1}</span>}[39m
[31m+                 </td>[39m
[31m+                 <td className="px-3 py-3">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="font-medium text-gray-900 truncate max-w-50">{c.nombre}</p>[39m
[31m+                 </td>[39m
[31m+                 <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right font-bold text-primary">{formatCurrency(c.gastoTotal)}</td>[39m
[31m+                 <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">{c.compras} compras</td>[39m
[31m+                 <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right text-gray-500 hidden md:table-cell">{formatCurrency(c.ticketPromedio)}</td>[39m
[31m+                 <td className="px-3 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                   {c.tendencia > 5 ? ([39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-emerald-500 text-xs font-bold">&#8593; +{c.tendencia.toFixed(0)}%</span>[39m
[31m+                   ) : c.tendencia < -5 ? ([39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-red-500 text-xs font-bold">&#8595; {c.tendencia.toFixed(0)}%</span>[39m
[31m+                   ) : ([39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-bold">&#8594; Estable</span>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 </td>[39m
[31m+               </tr>[39m
[31m+             ))}[39m
[31m+           </tbody>[39m
[31m+         </table>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Star Product of the Week ─────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function StarProductCard({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {[39m
[31m+   const [star, setStar] = useState<{[39m
[31m+     name: string; qty: number; revenue: number; trend: number; imageUrl?: string;[39m
[31m+   } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const res = await fetch("/api/sales?limit=500", { credentials: "include" });[39m
[31m+         if (!res.ok) return;[39m
[31m+         const raw = await res.json();[39m
[31m+         const sales = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw.sales ?? [];[39m
[31m+         const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+         const weekAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+         const twoWeekAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 14 * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const thisWeek: Record<string, { name: string; qty: number; revenue: number; imageUrl?: string }> = {};[39m
[31m+         const lastWeek: Record<string, { qty: number }> = {};[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         for (const s of sales) {[39m
[31m+           const d = (s.createdAt ?? "").slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+           const items = s.items ?? [];[39m
[31m+           for (const i of items) {[39m
[31m+             const pid = String(i.productId ?? i.name ?? "?");[39m
[31m+             if (d >= weekAgo) {[39m
[31m+               if (!thisWeek[pid]) thisWeek[pid] = { name: i.name ?? "Producto", qty: 0, revenue: 0, imageUrl: i.imageUrl };[39m
[31m+               thisWeek[pid].qty += i.quantity ?? 1;[39m
[31m+               thisWeek[pid].revenue += (i.price ?? 0) * (i.quantity ?? 1);[39m
[31m+             } else if (d >= twoWeekAgo && d < weekAgo) {[39m
[31m+               if (!lastWeek[pid]) lastWeek[pid] = { qty: 0 };[39m
[31m+               lastWeek[pid].qty += i.quantity ?? 1;[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const top = Object.entries(thisWeek).sort((a, b) => b[1].qty - a[1].qty)[0];[39m
[31m+         if (top) {[39m
[31m+           const [pid, data] = top;[39m
[31m+           const lastQty = lastWeek[pid]?.qty ?? 0;[39m
[31m+           const trend = lastQty > 0 ? ((data.qty - lastQty) / lastQty) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+           setStar({ name: data.name, qty: data.qty, revenue: data.revenue, trend, imageUrl: data.imageUrl });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch { /* silent */ }[39m
[31m+       finally { setLoading(false); }[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+   }, [refreshKey]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-5 w-40 bg-gray-200 rounded mb-4" />[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-8 w-56 bg-gray-200 rounded" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!star) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+           <Flame className="h-4 w-4 text-secondary" /> Producto Estrella[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">Aun no hay ventas esta semana</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="rounded-xl bg-linear-to-br from-secondary/5 to-secondary/10 p-4 border border-secondary/20">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-start gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {star.imageUrl && ([39m
[31m+           <div className="relative w-16 h-16 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-white shrink-0 border border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+             <Image src={star.imageUrl} alt={star.name} fill className="object-cover" sizes="64px" />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">[39m
[31m+             <span className="text-xs font-bold text-secondary bg-secondary/15 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">[39m
[31m+               Mas vendido[39m
[31m+             </span>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900 truncate">{star.name}</p>[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1 flex-wrap">[39m
[31m+             <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">{star.qty} unidades esta semana</span>[39m
[31m+             <span className="text-sm font-bold text-primary">S/{star.revenue.toFixed(0)}</span>[39m
[31m+             {star.trend !== 0 && ([39m
[31m+               <span className={`text-xs font-bold ${star.trend > 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-500"}`}>[39m
[31m+                 {star.trend > 0 ? "\u2191" : "\u2193"} {star.trend > 0 ? "+" : ""}{star.trend.toFixed(0)}% vs sem. pasada[39m
[31m+               </span>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── AnomalyDetectorWrapper — fetches data for AnomalyDetector ────────────────[39m
[31m+ function AnomalyDetectorWrapper({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {[39m
[31m+   const [sales, setSales] = useState<Sale[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Array<{ id: string; status: string; total: number; createdAt: string; items?: Array<{ productId?: string | number; name?: string; quantity: number; price: number }> }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const [salesRaw, ordersRaw] = await Promise.all([[39m
[31m+         fetch("/api/sales?limit=500", { credentials: "include" }).then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []).catch(() => []),[39m
[31m+         fetch("/api/orders?limit=500", { credentials: "include" }).then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []).catch(() => []),[39m
[31m+       ]);[39m
[31m+       setSales(Array.isArray(salesRaw) ? salesRaw : salesRaw.sales ?? []);[39m
[31m+       setOrders(Array.isArray(ordersRaw) ? ordersRaw : ordersRaw.orders ?? []);[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchData(); }, [fetchData, refreshKey]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <S />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (sales.length === 0 && orders.length === 0) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="text-center py-8">[39m
[31m+         <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">[39m
[31m+           <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>[39m
[31m+             <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />[39m
[31m+           </svg>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Sin anomalias — Todo marcha bien</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <Suspense fallback={<S />}>[39m
[31m+       <AnomalyDetector sales={sales} orders={orders} />[39m
[31m+     </Suspense>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── RFMWrapper — fetches data for RFMSegmentationPanel ──────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function RFMWrapper({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {[39m
[31m+   const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Customer[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [sales, setSales] = useState<Sale[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const [customersRaw, salesRaw] = await Promise.all([[39m
[31m+         fetch("/api/customers?limit=500", { credentials: "include" }).then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []).catch(() => []),[39m
[31m+         fetch("/api/sales?limit=1000", { credentials: "include" }).then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []).catch(() => []),[39m
[31m+       ]);[39m
[31m+       setCustomers(Array.isArray(customersRaw) ? customersRaw : customersRaw.customers ?? customersRaw.data ?? []);[39m
[31m+       setSales(Array.isArray(salesRaw) ? salesRaw : salesRaw.sales ?? []);[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchData(); }, [fetchData, refreshKey]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <S />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <Suspense fallback={<S />}>[39m
[31m+       <RFMSegmentationPanel customers={customers} sales={sales} />[39m
[31m+     </Suspense>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════[39m

[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+       // SECCION 2: VENTAS[39m
[31m+       // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+       case "ventas": {[39m
[31m+         const ventasCharts: ChartDefinition[] = [[39m
[31m+           {[39m
[31m+             id: "ventas-kpis",[39m
[31m+             label: "KPIs de Ventas",[39m
[31m+             description: "Métricas clave de ventas",[39m
[31m+             component: <SectionKPIStrip section="ventas" />,[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+           {[39m
[31m+             id: "ventas-trend",[39m
[31m+             label: "Tendencia de Ventas",[39m
[31m+             description: "Ventas diarias con media movil y prediccion",[39m
[31m+             component: ([39m
[31m+               <AnalyticsCard title="Tendencia de Ventas" subtitle="Ventas diarias con media movil y prediccion" icon={TrendingUp}>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="min-h-80">[39m
[31m+                   <Suspense fallback={<S />}><SalesTrendChart /></Suspense>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </AnalyticsCard>[39m
[31m+             ),[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+           {[39m
[31m+             id: "ventas-patterns",[39m
[31m+             label: "Patrones de Venta",[39m
[31m+             description: "Mapa de calor y distribucion horaria",[39m
[31m+             component: ([39m

[31m+ function ProductsDashboard() {[39m
[31m+   const [products, setProducts] = useState<ProductRecord[]>([]);[39m

[31m+     if (p.category && typeof p.category === "object" && p.category.name) return p.category.name;[39m
[31m+     return p.categoryName || "Otros";[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── KPIs ── */[39m
[31m+   const kpis = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const total = products.length;[39m
[31m+     const conStock = products.filter((p) => (p.stock || 0) > 0).length;[39m
[31m+     const sinStock = products.filter((p) => (p.stock || 0) === 0).length;[39m
[31m+     const stockBajo = products.filter((p) => (p.stock || 0) > 0 && (p.stock || 0) <= (p.minStock || 5)).length;[39m
[31m+     const precioAvg = total > 0 ? products.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.price || 0), 0) / total : 0;[39m
[31m+     const conCosto = products.filter((p) => (p.costPrice ?? 0) > 0);[39m
[31m+     const margenAvg = conCosto.length > 0[39m
[31m+       ? conCosto.reduce((s, p) => {[39m
[31m+           const price = p.price || 0;[39m
[31m+           const costPrice = p.costPrice || 0;[39m
[31m+           return s + (price > 0 ? ((price - costPrice) / price) * 100 : 0);[39m
[31m+         }, 0) / conCosto.length[39m
[31m+       : 0;[39m
[31m+     const cats = new Set(products.map((p) => categoryName(p))).size;[39m
[31m+     const sinImagen = products.filter((p) => !p.image).length;[39m
[31m+     const sinCosto = products.filter((p) => !p.costPrice || p.costPrice === 0).length;[39m
[31m+     return { total, conStock, sinStock, stockBajo, precioAvg, margenAvg, cats, sinImagen, sinCosto };[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Datos por categoría ── */[39m
[31m+   const categoryData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const g: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+     products.forEach((p) => {[39m
[31m+       const cat = categoryName(p);[39m
[31m+       g[cat] = (g[cat] || 0) + 1;[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.entries(g).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value })).sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Valor inventario por categoría ── */[39m
[31m+   const inventoryByCat = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const g: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+     products.forEach((p) => {[39m
[31m+       const cat = categoryName(p);[39m
[31m+       g[cat] = (g[cat] || 0) + (p.stock || 0) * (p.costPrice || p.price || 0);[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.entries(g).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value: Math.round(value) })).sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const totalInventoryValue = useMemo(() => inventoryByCat.reduce((s, c) => s + c.value, 0), [inventoryByCat]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Radial data for category distribution ── */[39m
[31m+   const radialData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return categoryData.slice(0, 7).map((c, i) => ({[39m
[31m+       name: c.name,[39m
[31m+       value: c.value,[39m
[31m+       fill: CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length],[39m
[31m+     }));[39m
[31m+   }, [categoryData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Histograma de precios ── */[39m
[31m+   const priceHistogram = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const ranges = [[39m
[31m+       { label: "S/0-5", min: 0, max: 5 },[39m
[31m+       { label: "S/5-10", min: 5, max: 10 },[39m
[31m+       { label: "S/10-20", min: 10, max: 20 },[39m
[31m+       { label: "S/20-50", min: 20, max: 50 },[39m
[31m+       { label: "S/50-100", min: 50, max: 100 },[39m
[31m+       { label: "S/100+", min: 100, max: Infinity },[39m
[31m+     ];[39m
[31m+     return ranges.map((r, i) => ({[39m
[31m+       rango: r.label,[39m
[31m+       count: products.filter((p) => (p.price || 0) >= r.min && (p.price || 0) < r.max).length,[39m
[31m+       fill: CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length],[39m
[31m+     }));[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Top 10 por valor en stock ── */[39m
[31m+   const top10Stock = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return [...products][39m
[31m+       .map((p) => {[39m
[31m+         const price = p.price || 0;[39m
[31m+         const costPrice = p.costPrice || 0;[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           name: (p.name || "").substring(0, 22),[39m
[31m+           valor: Math.round((p.stock || 0) * (costPrice || price)),[39m
[31m+           margen: costPrice > 0 && price > 0 ? Math.round(((price - costPrice) / price) * 100) : 0,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .sort((a, b) => b.valor - a.valor)[39m
[31m+       .slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Scatter data (margen vs precio) ── */[39m
[31m+   const scatterData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return products[39m
[31m+       .filter((p) => (p.costPrice ?? 0) > 0 && (p.price ?? 0) > 0)[39m
[31m+       .map((p) => {[39m
[31m+         const price = p.price || 0;[39m
[31m+         const costPrice = p.costPrice || 0;[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           name: (p.name || "").substring(0, 25),[39m
[31m+           precio: price,[39m
[31m+           margen: price > 0 ? Math.round(((price - costPrice) / price) * 100) : 0,[39m
[31m+           stock: p.stock || 1,[39m
[31m+           costo: costPrice,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Estado stock (gauge) ── */[39m
[31m+   const stockGauge = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return [[39m
[31m+       { name: "Con stock", value: kpis.conStock - kpis.stockBajo, fill: "var(--color-primary)" },[39m
[31m+       { name: "Stock bajo", value: kpis.stockBajo, fill: "#f97316" },[39m
[31m+       { name: "Sin stock", value: kpis.sinStock, fill: "#e63946" },[39m
[31m+     ].filter((s) => s.value > 0);[39m
[31m+   }, [kpis]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Loading skeleton ── */[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <DashboardSkeleton kpis={8} />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const kpiCards = [[39m
[31m+     { label: "Total SKUs", value: kpis.total, icon: Package, color: "var(--color-primary)", alert: false },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Con stock", value: kpis.conStock, icon: PackageCheck, color: "#457b9d", alert: false },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Sin stock", value: kpis.sinStock, icon: PackageX, color: "#e63946", alert: kpis.sinStock > 0 },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Precio prom.", value: `S/ ${kpis.precioAvg.toFixed(1)}`, icon: DollarSign, color: "#9b5de5", alert: false },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Margen prom.", value: `${kpis.margenAvg.toFixed(1)}%`, icon: TrendingUp, color: "#f97316", alert: kpis.margenAvg < 15 },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Categorias", value: kpis.cats, icon: Layers, color: "#2dd4bf", alert: false },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Sin imagen", value: kpis.sinImagen, icon: ImageOff, color: "#e63946", alert: kpis.sinImagen > 0 },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Sin costo", value: kpis.sinCosto, icon: Calculator, color: "#6b705c", alert: kpis.sinCosto > 0 },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Controls: Period + Refresh + Export === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">[39m
[31m+         <PeriodSelector value={period} onChange={setPeriod} />[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+           <AutoRefreshControl secondsLeft={autoRefresh.secondsLeft} paused={autoRefresh.paused} isActive={autoRefresh.isActive} onTogglePause={autoRefresh.togglePause} onRefreshNow={autoRefresh.refreshNow} />[39m
[31m+           <ExportButton />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 1: KPIs Premium === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {kpiCards.map((k, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <KpiCard key={i} label={k.label} value={k.value} icon={k.icon} color={k.color} change={kpiMockChanges[i] ?? 0} alert={k.alert} />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 2: Distribucion (RadialBarChart + PieChart inventario) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4"><FavStar id="distribucion-cat" favs={catFavs} /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Distribucion por categoria</h3></div>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+             <RadialBarChart cx="50%" cy="50%" innerRadius="20%" outerRadius="90%" data={radialData} startAngle={180} endAngle={-180}>[39m
[31m+               <RadialBar dataKey="value" background={{ fill: "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)" }} cornerRadius={6} />[39m
[31m+               <Legend iconSize={10} formatter={(value) => <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">{value}</span>} />[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+             </RadialBarChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">[39m
[31m+             <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Valor del inventario por categoria</h3>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               {pieFilter && ([39m
[31m+                 <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-bold">[39m
[31m+                   {pieFilter}[39m
[31m+                   <button onClick={() => setPieFilter(null)} className="hover:bg-primary/20 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors"><XIcon className="h-3 w-3" /></button>[39m
[31m+                 </span>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setExpandedChart("inv-cat")} className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded transition-colors" title="Expandir"><Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-gray-400" /></button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+             <PieChart>[39m
[31m+               <Pie data={inventoryByCat.slice(0, 8)} innerRadius={55} outerRadius={90} dataKey="value" paddingAngle={2} className="cursor-pointer"[39m
[31m+                 label={({ name, percent }: { name?: string; percent?: number }) => `${(name || "").substring(0, 12)} ${((percent ?? 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`}[39m
[31m+                 onClick={(_: unknown, idx: number) => setPieFilter(prev => prev === inventoryByCat[idx]?.name ? null : inventoryByCat[idx]?.name ?? null)}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {inventoryByCat.slice(0, 8).map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+                   <Cell key={i} fill={CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length]} />[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </Pie>[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+               <Legend />[39m
[31m+               {/* Centro label */}[39m
[31m+               <text x="50%" y="48%" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central" className="fill-gray-900 text-lg font-bold font-mono">[39m
[31m+                 S/ {totalInventoryValue.toLocaleString()}[39m
[31m+               </text>[39m
[31m+               <text x="50%" y="57%" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central" className="fill-gray-400 text-[10px]">[39m
[31m+                 Total inv.[39m
[31m+               </text>[39m
[31m+             </PieChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 3: Histograma de precios === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Rango de precios</h3>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>[39m
[31m+           <BarChart data={priceHistogram}>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="rango" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={({ active, payload, label }) => {[39m
[31m+               if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;[39m
[31m+               const total = products.length || 1;[39m
[31m+               const rawVal = payload[0]?.value;[39m
[31m+               const val = typeof rawVal === "number" ? rawVal : 0;[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-900">{String(label ?? "")}</p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">{val} productos ({((val / total) * 100).toFixed(0)}%)</p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             }} />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="count" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} name="Productos">[39m
[31m+               {priceHistogram.map((entry, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <Cell key={i} fill={CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length]} />[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </Bar>[39m
[31m+           </BarChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 4: Top 10 por valor en stock (ComposedChart) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Top 10 productos por valor en stock</h3>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>[39m
[31m+           <ComposedChart data={top10Stock} layout="vertical">[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" horizontal={false} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `S/${v.toLocaleString()}`} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis dataKey="name" type="category" width={130} tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} className="fill-gray-600" />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+             <Legend />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="valor" fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]} name="Valor stock (S/)" barSize={16} />[39m
[31m+             <Line dataKey="margen" stroke="#f97316" strokeWidth={2} dot={{ r: 4, fill: "#f97316" }} name="Margen %" />[39m
[31m+           </ComposedChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 5: Scatter de margenes === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">Analisis de margenes</h3>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mb-4">Precio venta vs margen % — tamano = stock</p>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>[39m
[31m+           <ScatterChart>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="precio" name="Precio" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `S/${v}`} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis dataKey="margen" name="Margen %" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `${v}%`} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <ZAxis dataKey="stock" range={[30, 300]} name="Stock" />[39m
[31m+             <ReferenceLine y={20} stroke="#f97316" strokeDasharray="6 3" label={{ value: "Objetivo 20%", position: "right", fontSize: 10, fill: "#f97316" }} />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={({ active, payload }) => {[39m
[31m+               if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;[39m
[31m+               const d = payload[0]?.payload as[39m
[31m+                 | { name?: string; precio?: number; costo?: number; margen?: number; stock?: number }[39m
[31m+                 | undefined;[39m
[31m+               if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+               const margen = d.margen ?? 0;[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="min-w-45 bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">{d.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Precio: <span className="font-mono font-medium text-gray-800">S/ {d.precio?.toFixed(2)}</span></p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Costo: <span className="font-mono font-medium text-gray-800">S/ {d.costo?.toFixed(2)}</span></p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Margen: <span className={cn("font-mono font-medium", margen >= 25 ? "text-green-600" : margen >= 10 ? "text-amber-600" : "text-red-600")}>{margen}%</span></p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Stock: <span className="font-mono font-medium text-gray-800">{d.stock}</span></p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             }} />[39m
[31m+             <Scatter data={scatterData} shape={(props: unknown) => {[39m
[31m+               const p = props as { cx?: number; cy?: number; payload?: { margen?: number; stock?: number } };[39m
[31m+               const { cx = 0, cy = 0, payload } = p;[39m
[31m+               const m = payload?.margen ?? 0;[39m
[31m+               const color = m >= 25 ? "var(--color-primary)" : m >= 10 ? "#f97316" : "#e63946";[39m
[31m+               const r = Math.min(Math.max((payload?.stock || 1) * 0.4, 4), 14);[39m
[31m+               return <circle cx={cx} cy={cy} r={r} fill={color} fillOpacity={0.7} stroke={color} strokeWidth={1.5} />;[39m
[31m+             }} />[39m
[31m+           </ScatterChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mt-3 justify-center">[39m
[31m+           <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-gray-500"><span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary" /> {">"}25% margen</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-gray-500"><span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#f97316]" /> 10-25%</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-gray-500"><span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#e63946]" /> {"<"}10%</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 6: Estado de stock (Gauge donut) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Productos por estado de stock</h3>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={200}>[39m
[31m+           <PieChart>[39m
[31m+             <Pie data={stockGauge} innerRadius={55} outerRadius={80} startAngle={180} endAngle={0} dataKey="value" paddingAngle={2}>[39m
[31m+               {stockGauge.map((entry, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <Cell key={i} fill={entry.fill} />[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </Pie>[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+             <Legend iconType="circle" formatter={(value) => <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">{value}</span>} />[39m
[31m+             <text x="50%" y="70%" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central" className="fill-gray-900 text-2xl font-bold font-mono">[39m
[31m+               {kpis.total}[39m
[31m+             </text>[39m
[31m+             <text x="50%" y="82%" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central" className="fill-gray-400 text-[10px]">[39m
[31m+               total[39m
[31m+             </text>[39m
[31m+           </PieChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === SECCION 7: Alertas del catalogo === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">[39m
[31m+         {kpis.sinImagen > 0 && ([39m
[31m+           <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3">[39m
[31m+             <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full bg-amber-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">[39m

[31m+ // ── Dashboard de Compras ────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function ComprasDashboard() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<ComprasData>({ purchases: [], suppliers: [], payables: [] });[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 12: Click-to-filter PieChart proveedores[39m
[31m+   const [pieFilter, setPieFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 13: Expand chart[39m
[31m+   const [expandedChart, setExpandedChart] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 1: Period selector[39m
[31m+   const [period, setPeriod] = useState<"today" | "7d" | "30d" | "month">("30d");[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 3: Auto-refresh[39m
[31m+   // Snapshot "now" once per mount — React Compiler rejects Date.now() during render/useMemo[39m
[31m+   const [nowTs] = useState(() => Date.now());[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 5: Favoritos[39m
[31m+   const compFavs = useFavoriteCharts("compras");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchData = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.allSettled([[39m
[31m+       fetch('/api/purchases').then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+       fetch('/api/suppliers').then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+       fetch('/api/payables').then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+     ]).then(([pRes, sRes, paRes]) => {[39m
[31m+       setData({[39m
[31m+         purchases: pRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(pRes.value) ? pRes.value : pRes.value?.purchases || pRes.value?.data || []) : [],[39m
[31m+         suppliers: sRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(sRes.value) ? sRes.value : sRes.value?.suppliers || sRes.value?.data || []) : [],[39m
[31m+         payables: paRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(paRes.value) ? paRes.value : paRes.value?.payables || paRes.value?.data || []) : [],[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchData(); }, [fetchData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const autoRefresh = useAutoRefresh({ intervalSeconds: 300, onRefresh: fetchData });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── KPIs ── */[39m
[31m+   const kpis = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const totalOC = data.purchases.length;[39m
[31m+     const ocPendientes = data.purchases.filter((p) => p.status !== 'completed' && p.status !== 'recibido' && p.status !== 'cancelado').length;[39m
[31m+     const totalGastado = data.purchases.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.total || p.grandTotal || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+     const totalProveedores = data.suppliers.length;[39m
[31m+     const deudaTotal = data.payables.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.amount || p.balance || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+     const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+     const deudaVencida = data.payables[39m
[31m+       .filter((p) => p.status !== 'pagado' && p.status !== 'paid' && p.dueDate && new Date(p.dueDate) < now)[39m
[31m+       .reduce((s, p) => s + (p.amount || p.balance || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+     return { totalOC, ocPendientes, totalGastado, totalProveedores, deudaTotal, deudaVencida };[39m
[31m+   }, [data]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Compras por mes ── */[39m
[31m+   const purchasesByMonth = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const months: Record<string, { total: number; count: number }> = {};[39m
[31m+     const monthNames = ['Ene', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Abr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Ago', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dic'];[39m
[31m+     // Initialize last 6 months[39m
[31m+     const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+     for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+       const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);[39m
[31m+       const key = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;[39m
[31m+       months[key] = { total: 0, count: 0 };[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     data.purchases.forEach((p) => {[39m
[31m+       const d = new Date(p.createdAt || p.date || p.orderDate || nowTs);[39m
[31m+       const key = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;[39m
[31m+       if (months[key]) {[39m
[31m+         months[key].total += (p.total || p.grandTotal || 0);[39m
[31m+         months[key].count += 1;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.entries(months).map(([key, val]) => {[39m
[31m+       const [, m] = key.split('-');[39m
[31m+       return { mes: monthNames[parseInt(m) - 1], total: Math.round(val.total), count: val.count };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   }, [data.purchases, nowTs]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Gasto por proveedor ── */[39m
[31m+   const supplierSpend = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const g: Record<string, { name: string; total: number; count: number }> = {};[39m
[31m+     data.purchases.forEach((p) => {[39m
[31m+       const supplierField = p.supplier;[39m
[31m+       const name = p.supplierName[39m
[31m+         || (typeof supplierField === "object" && supplierField !== null ? supplierField.name : undefined)[39m
[31m+         || (typeof supplierField === "string" ? supplierField : undefined)[39m
[31m+         || 'Sin proveedor';[39m
[31m+       const id = name;[39m
[31m+       if (!g[id]) g[id] = { name, total: 0, count: 0 };[39m
[31m+       g[id].total += (p.total || p.grandTotal || 0);[39m
[31m+       g[id].count += 1;[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.values(g).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total).slice(0, 8);[39m
[31m+   }, [data.purchases]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const totalSpend = useMemo(() => supplierSpend.reduce((s, v) => s + v.total, 0), [supplierSpend]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Estado de OC por mes (stacked) ── */[39m
[31m+   const statusByMonth = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const months: Record<string, Record<string, number>> = {};[39m
[31m+     const monthNames = ['Ene', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Abr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Ago', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dic'];[39m
[31m+     const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+     for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+       const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);[39m
[31m+       const key = `${monthNames[d.getMonth()]}`;[39m
[31m+       months[key] = { Pendiente: 0, 'En proceso': 0, Recibido: 0, Cancelado: 0 };[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     data.purchases.forEach((p) => {[39m
[31m+       const d = new Date(p.createdAt || p.date || p.orderDate || nowTs);[39m
[31m+       const key = monthNames[d.getMonth()];[39m
[31m+       if (!months[key]) return;[39m
[31m+       const st = p.status || 'pendiente';[39m
[31m+       if (st === 'pendiente' || st === 'pending' || st === 'draft') months[key].Pendiente += 1;[39m
[31m+       else if (st === 'en_proceso' || st === 'processing' || st === 'ordered' || st === 'enviado') months[key]['En proceso'] += 1;[39m
[31m+       else if (st === 'completed' || st === 'recibido' || st === 'received') months[key].Recibido += 1;[39m
[31m+       else if (st === 'cancelado' || st === 'cancelled' || st === 'canceled') months[key].Cancelado += 1;[39m
[31m+       else months[key].Pendiente += 1;[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.entries(months).map(([mes, vals]) => ({ mes, ...vals }));[39m
[31m+   }, [data.purchases, nowTs]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Deuda por proveedor ── */[39m
[31m+   const debtBySupplier = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const g: Record<string, { name: string; total: number; vencida: number; dueDate: string }> = {};[39m
[31m+     const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+     data.payables.forEach((p) => {[39m
[31m+       const supplierField = p.supplier;[39m
[31m+       const name = p.supplierName[39m
[31m+         || (typeof supplierField === "object" && supplierField !== null ? supplierField.name : undefined)[39m
[31m+         || (typeof supplierField === "string" ? supplierField : undefined)[39m
[31m+         || 'Sin nombre';[39m
[31m+       if (!g[name]) g[name] = { name, total: 0, vencida: 0, dueDate: '' };[39m
[31m+       const amt = p.amount || p.balance || 0;[39m
[31m+       g[name].total += amt;[39m
[31m+       if (p.dueDate && new Date(p.dueDate) < now && p.status !== 'pagado' && p.status !== 'paid') {[39m
[31m+         g[name].vencida += amt;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (!g[name].dueDate || (p.dueDate && p.dueDate > g[name].dueDate)) {[39m
[31m+         g[name].dueDate = p.dueDate || '';[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.values(g).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total).slice(0, 8);[39m
[31m+   }, [data.payables]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Proximos pagos ── */[39m
[31m+   const nextPayments = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+     return [...data.payables][39m
[31m+       .filter((p) => p.status !== 'pagado' && p.status !== 'paid')[39m
[31m+       .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.dueDate || 0).getTime() - new Date(b.dueDate || 0).getTime())[39m
[31m+       .slice(0, 5)[39m
[31m+       .map((p) => {[39m
[31m+         const due = new Date(p.dueDate || nowTs);[39m
[31m+         const diffDays = Math.ceil((due.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));[39m
[31m+         const supplierField = p.supplier;[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           name: p.supplierName[39m
[31m+             || (typeof supplierField === "object" && supplierField !== null ? supplierField.name : undefined)[39m
[31m+             || (typeof supplierField === "string" ? supplierField : undefined)[39m
[31m+             || 'Proveedor',[39m
[31m+           amount: p.amount || p.balance || 0,[39m
[31m+           dueDate: p.dueDate,[39m
[31m+           days: diffDays,[39m
[31m+           id: p.id,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+   }, [data.payables, nowTs]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Tendencia con promedio movil ── */[39m
[31m+   const trendData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return purchasesByMonth.map((m, i, arr) => {[39m
[31m+       const windowSize = Math.min(3, i + 1);[39m
[31m+       const avg = arr.slice(Math.max(0, i - windowSize + 1), i + 1).reduce((s, v) => s + v.total, 0) / windowSize;[39m
[31m+       return { ...m, promedio: Math.round(avg) };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   }, [purchasesByMonth]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Cambio real mes anterior vs mes actual ── */[39m
[31m+   const kpiChanges = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (purchasesByMonth.length < 2) return { gastado: undefined, oc: undefined };[39m
[31m+     const current = purchasesByMonth[purchasesByMonth.length - 1];[39m
[31m+     const previous = purchasesByMonth[purchasesByMonth.length - 2];[39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       gastado: previous.total > 0 ? ((current.total - previous.total) / previous.total) * 100 : undefined,[39m
[31m+       oc: previous.count > 0 ? ((current.count - previous.count) / previous.count) * 100 : undefined,[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   }, [purchasesByMonth]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* ── Skeleton loading ── */[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <DashboardSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const kpiCards = [[39m
[31m+     { label: "OC totales", value: kpis.totalOC, icon: ShoppingCart, color: "#457b9d", change: kpiChanges.oc != null ? Math.round(kpiChanges.oc * 10) / 10 : undefined },[39m
[31m+     { label: "OC pendientes", value: kpis.ocPendientes, icon: Clock, color: "#f97316" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Total gastado", value: `S/ ${kpis.totalGastado.toLocaleString()}`, icon: DollarSign, color: "var(--color-primary)", change: kpiChanges.gastado != null ? Math.round(kpiChanges.gastado * 10) / 10 : undefined },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Proveedores", value: kpis.totalProveedores, icon: Building2, color: "#9b5de5" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Deuda total", value: `S/ ${kpis.deudaTotal.toLocaleString()}`, icon: CreditCard, color: "#f97316" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Deuda vencida", value: `S/ ${kpis.deudaVencida.toLocaleString()}`, icon: AlertTriangle, color: "#e63946", alert: kpis.deudaVencida > 0 },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Controls: Period + Refresh + Export === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">[39m
[31m+         <PeriodSelector value={period} onChange={setPeriod} />[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+           <AutoRefreshControl secondsLeft={autoRefresh.secondsLeft} paused={autoRefresh.paused} isActive={autoRefresh.isActive} onTogglePause={autoRefresh.togglePause} onRefreshNow={autoRefresh.refreshNow} />[39m
[31m+           <ExportButton />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Alertas Compras === */}[39m
[31m+       {(kpis.ocPendientes > 0 || kpis.deudaVencida > 0) && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+           {kpis.ocPendientes > 0 && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"><AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> {kpis.ocPendientes} ordenes pendientes</span>}[39m
[31m+           {kpis.deudaVencida > 0 && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-700"><AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> S/ {kpis.deudaVencida.toLocaleString()} deuda vencida</span>}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === KPIs (6 cards) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {kpiCards.map((k, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <KpiCard key={i} label={k.label} value={k.value} icon={k.icon} color={k.color} change={k.change} alert={k.alert} />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Compras por Mes (AreaChart) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4"><FavStar id="compras-mes" favs={compFavs} /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Compras por mes (ultimos 6 meses)</h3></div>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+           <AreaChart data={purchasesByMonth}>[39m
[31m+             <defs>[39m
[31m+               <linearGradient id="areaGradient" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">[39m
[31m+                 <stop offset="5%" stopColor="var(--color-primary)" stopOpacity={0.3} />[39m
[31m+                 <stop offset="95%" stopColor="var(--color-primary)" stopOpacity={0.02} />[39m
[31m+               </linearGradient>[39m
[31m+             </defs>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="mes" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `S/${v.toLocaleString()}`} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={({ active, payload, label }) => {[39m
[31m+               if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;[39m
[31m+               const rawVal = payload[0]?.value;[39m
[31m+               const val = typeof rawVal === "number" ? rawVal : 0;[39m
[31m+               const count = (payload[0]?.payload as { count?: number } | undefined)?.count || 0;[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-900">{String(label ?? "")}</p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">[39m
[31m+                     S/ {val.toLocaleString()} ({count} OCs)[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             }} />[39m
[31m+             <Area type="monotone" dataKey="total" stroke="var(--color-primary)" strokeWidth={2.5} fill="url(#areaGradient)" name="Total compras" />[39m
[31m+           </AreaChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Distribucion por Proveedor (PieChart + tabla) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">[39m
[31m+             <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Gasto por proveedor</h3>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               {pieFilter && ([39m
[31m+                 <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-bold">[39m
[31m+                   {pieFilter}[39m
[31m+                   <button onClick={() => setPieFilter(null)} className="hover:bg-primary/20 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors"><XIcon className="h-3 w-3" /></button>[39m
[31m+                 </span>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setExpandedChart("proveedor")} className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded transition-colors" title="Expandir"><Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-gray-400" /></button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+             <PieChart>[39m
[31m+               <Pie data={supplierSpend} innerRadius={55} outerRadius={90} dataKey="total" paddingAngle={2} className="cursor-pointer"[39m
[31m+                 label={({ name, percent }: { name?: string; percent?: number }) => `${(name || "").substring(0, 10)} ${((percent ?? 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`}[39m
[31m+                 onClick={(_: unknown, idx: number) => setPieFilter(prev => prev === supplierSpend[idx]?.name ? null : supplierSpend[idx]?.name ?? null)}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {supplierSpend.map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+                   <Cell key={i} fill={CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length]} />[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </Pie>[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+               <Legend />[39m
[31m+             </PieChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Ranking de proveedores</h3>[39m
[31m+           <div className="max-h-70 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto">[39m
[31m+             {supplierSpend.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+               <EmptyState title="Sin datos de proveedores" className="py-8" />[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               supplierSpend.map((s, i) => {[39m
[31m+                 const pct = totalSpend > 0 ? (s.total / totalSpend) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold w-5 text-gray-400 shrink-0">#{i + 1}</span>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                       <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">[39m
[31m+                         <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-800 truncate">{s.name}</span>[39m
[31m+                         <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-gray-900 shrink-0 ml-2">S/ {s.total.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                         <div className="flex-1 h-1.5 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+                           <div className="h-full rounded-full transition-all" style={{ width: `${pct}%`, backgroundColor: CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length] }} />[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                         <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 shrink-0 w-10 text-right">{pct.toFixed(0)}%</span>[39m
[31m+                         <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 shrink-0">{s.count} OCs</span>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               })[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Estado de OC (BarChart stacked) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Estado de ordenes por mes</h3>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={250}>[39m
[31m+           <BarChart data={statusByMonth}>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="mes" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+             <Legend iconType="circle" formatter={(value) => <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">{value}</span>} />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="Pendiente" stackId="a" fill="#f97316" radius={[0, 0, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="En proceso" stackId="a" fill="#457b9d" />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="Recibido" stackId="a" fill="var(--color-primary)" />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="Cancelado" stackId="a" fill="#e63946" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+           </BarChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Deuda por Proveedor (horizontal) + Proximos Pagos === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Deuda por proveedor</h3>[39m
[31m+           {debtBySupplier.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <EmptyState title="Sin deudas registradas" description="No hay deudas pendientes con proveedores" />[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>[39m
[31m+               <BarChart data={debtBySupplier} layout="vertical">[39m
[31m+                 <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" horizontal={false} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+                 <XAxis type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `S/${v.toLocaleString()}`} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+                 <YAxis dataKey="name" type="category" width={100} tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} className="fill-gray-600" />[39m
[31m+                 <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+                 <Bar dataKey="total" name="Deuda total" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]} barSize={14}>[39m
[31m+                   {debtBySupplier.map((d, i) => ([39m
[31m+                     <Cell key={i} fill={d.vencida > 0 ? "#e63946" : d.total > 500 ? "#f97316" : "var(--color-primary)"} />[39m
[31m+                   ))}[39m
[31m+                 </Bar>[39m
[31m+               </BarChart>[39m
[31m+             </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Proximos pagos</h3>[39m
[31m+           {nextPayments.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <EmptyState title="Sin pagos pendientes" description="No hay pagos próximos registrados" />[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+               {nextPayments.map((p, i) => {[39m
[31m+                 const isOverdue = p.days < 0;[39m
[31m+                 const isUrgent = p.days >= 0 && p.days <= 7;[39m
[31m+                 const badgeColor = isOverdue[39m
[31m+                   ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700"[39m
[31m+                   : isUrgent[39m
[31m+                     ? "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"[39m
[31m+                     : "bg-green-100 text-green-700";[39m
[31m+                 const dotColor = isOverdue ? "bg-red-500" : isUrgent ? "bg-amber-500" : "bg-green-500";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={p.id || i} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 bg-gray-50/50">[39m
[31m+                     <div className={cn("h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0", dotColor)} />[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-800 truncate">{p.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">S/ {p.amount.toLocaleString()}</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1 shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                       <span className={cn("text-[10px] font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full", badgeColor)}>[39m
[31m+                         {isOverdue ? `Vencido ${Math.abs(p.days)}d` : p.days === 0 ? "Hoy" : `En ${p.days}d`}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <button className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-primary hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                         <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" /> Pagado[39m
[31m+                       </button>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               })}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* === Tendencia de Gastos vs Promedio Movil (ComposedChart) === */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">Tendencia de gastos</h3>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mb-4">Gasto real vs promedio movil 3 meses</p>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>[39m
[31m+           <ComposedChart data={trendData}>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="mes" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `S/${v.toLocaleString()}`} className="fill-gray-500" />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
