[31m+ function PartnerModal({[39m
[31m+   partner,[39m
[31m+   onClose,[39m
[31m+   onSave,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   partner: Partial<DeliveryPartner> | null;[39m
[31m+   onClose: () => void;[39m
[31m+   onSave: (data: Omit<DeliveryPartner, "id" | "createdAt">) => Promise<void>;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const [form, setForm] = useState<Omit<DeliveryPartner, "id" | "createdAt">>([39m
[31m+     partner[39m
[31m+       ? {[39m
[31m+           name: partner.name ?? "",[39m
[31m+           phone: partner.phone ?? "",[39m
[31m+           zone: partner.zone ?? "Centro",[39m
[31m+           vehicleType: partner.vehicleType ?? "Moto",[39m
[31m+           fee: partner.fee ?? 5,[39m
[31m+           rating: partner.rating ?? 5,[39m
[31m+           isActive: partner.isActive ?? true,[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       : { ...EMPTY_PARTNER }[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {[39m
[31m+     e.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     if (!form.name.trim()) { setError("El nombre es obligatorio."); return; }[39m
[31m+     setSaving(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       await onSave(form);[39m
[31m+       onClose();[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al guardar el repartidor. Intenta nuevamente.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setSaving(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div[39m
[31m+       className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50"[39m
[31m+       onClick={onClose}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="bg-white rounded-xl w-full max-w-md"[39m
[31m+         onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-gray-100">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+             {partner?.id ? "Editar repartidor" : "Nuevo repartidor"}[39m
[31m+           </h3>[39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={onClose}[39m
[31m+             className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <X className="h-5 w-5" />[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-5 space-y-4">[39m
[31m+           {error && ([39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+               <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" /> {error}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">[39m
[31m+             <div className="col-span-2 space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Nombre completo *</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.name}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, name: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Juan Pérez"[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+                 autoFocus[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Teléfono</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="tel"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.phone}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, phone: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="987654321"[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Tarifa base (S/)</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="number"[39m
[31m+                 min={0}[39m
[31m+                 step={0.5}[39m
[31m+                 value={form.fee}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, fee: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0 }))}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Zona</label>[39m
[31m+               <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+                 <select[39m
[31m+                   value={form.zone}[39m
[31m+                   onChange={(e) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, zone: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                   className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary appearance-none transition-all"[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   {ZONAS.map((z) => <option key={z} value={z}>{z}</option>)}[39m
[31m+                 </select>[39m
[31m+                 <ChevronDown className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none" />[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Vehículo</label>[39m
[31m+               <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+                 <select[39m
[31m+                   value={form.vehicleType}[39m
[31m+                   onChange={(e) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, vehicleType: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                   className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary appearance-none transition-all"[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   {VEHICLE_TYPES.map((v) => <option key={v} value={v}>{v}</option>)}[39m
[31m+                 </select>[39m
[31m+                 <ChevronDown className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none" />[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Activo toggle */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Activo</p>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               type="button"[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, isActive: !p.isActive }))}[39m
[31m+               className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 items-center rounded-full transition-colors",[39m
[31m+                 form.isActive ? "bg-primary" : "bg-gray-300"[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <span className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "inline-block h-4 w-4 transform rounded-full bg-white  transition-transform",[39m
[31m+                 form.isActive ? "translate-x-6" : "translate-x-1"[39m
[31m+               )} />[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               type="button"[39m
[31m+               onClick={onClose}[39m
[31m+               className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Cancelar[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               type="submit"[39m
[31m+               disabled={saving}[39m
[31m+               className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-white bg-primary hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {saving ? ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="h-4 w-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+               ) : ([39m
[31m+                 <Save className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+               {saving ? "Guardando..." : "Guardar"}[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </form>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Repartidores[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function RepartidoresTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [partners, setPartners] = useState<DeliveryPartner[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading]   = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError]       = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [modal, setModal]       = useState<{ open: boolean; partner: Partial<DeliveryPartner> | null }>({ open: false, partner: null });[39m
[31m+   const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const load = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/delivery/partners")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject()))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => setPartners(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []))[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setError("No se pudieron cargar los repartidores."))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSave = async (data: Omit<DeliveryPartner, "id" | "createdAt">) => {[39m
[31m+     const id = modal.partner?.id;[39m
[31m+     const res = await fetch(id ? `/api/delivery/partners/${id}` : "/api/delivery/partners", {[39m
[31m+       method: id ? "PUT" : "POST",[39m
[31m+       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+       body: JSON.stringify(data),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+     const saved = await res.json();[39m
[31m+     if (id) {[39m
[31m+       setPartners((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === id ? saved : p)));[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       setPartners((prev) => [...prev, saved]);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {[39m
[31m+     setDeleting(id);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/delivery/partners/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+       setPartners((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id));[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al eliminar el repartidor.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setDeleting(null);[39m
[31m+       setConfirmDelete(null);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {error && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+           <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {error}[39m
[31m+           <button onClick={() => setError(null)} className="ml-auto"><X className="h-4 w-4" /></button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">{partners.length} repartidor{partners.length !== 1 ? "es" : ""}</p>[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setModal({ open: true, partner: null })}[39m
[31m+           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-sm font-bold hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           Nuevo repartidor[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {partners.length === 0 && !error ? ([39m
[31m+         <EmptyState icon={Truck} title="Sin repartidores registrados" description="Agrega tu primer repartidor para empezar a gestionar entregas." />[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl  overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+             <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+               <thead className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+                 <tr>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Repartidor</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">Zona / Vehículo</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Tarifa</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-center px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Rating</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-center px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Estado</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Acciones</th>[39m
[31m+                 </tr>[39m
[31m+               </thead>[39m
[31m+               <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">[39m
[31m+                 {partners.map((p) => ([39m
[31m+                   <tr key={p.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{p.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       {p.phone && ([39m
[31m+                         <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">[39m
[31m+                           <Phone className="h-3 w-3" /> {p.phone}[39m
[31m+                         </p>[39m
[31m+                       )}[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 hidden sm:table-cell">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                         <MapPin className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" /> {p.zone}[39m
[31m+                       </p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{p.vehicleType}</p>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                       S/{p.fee.toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-amber-600">[39m
[31m+                         <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-amber-400" />[39m
[31m+                         {p.rating.toFixed(1)}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                       <span className={cn([39m
[31m+                         "inline-flex px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold",[39m
[31m+                         p.isActive[39m
[31m+                           ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"[39m
[31m+                           : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-500"[39m
[31m+                       )}>[39m
[31m+                         {p.isActive ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">[39m
[31m+                       <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">[39m
[31m+                         <button[39m
[31m+                           onClick={() => setModal({ open: true, partner: p })}[39m
[31m+                           className="p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                           title="Editar"[39m
[31m+                         >[39m
[31m+                           <Edit2 className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                         </button>[39m
[31m+                         <button[39m
[31m+                           onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(p.id)}[39m
[31m+                           className="p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                           title="Eliminar"[39m
[31m+                         >[39m
[31m+                           <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                         </button>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                   </tr>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </tbody>[39m
[31m+             </table>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Modal crear/editar */}[39m
[31m+       {modal.open && ([39m
[31m+         <PartnerModal[39m
[31m+           partner={modal.partner}[39m
[31m+           onClose={() => setModal({ open: false, partner: null })}[39m
[31m+           onSave={handleSave}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Confirm delete */}[39m
[31m+       {confirmDelete && ([39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50"[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(null)}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className="bg-white rounded-xl w-full max-w-sm p-6"[39m
[31m+             onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">[39m
[31m+               <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-red-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                 <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5 text-red-500" />[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               <div>[39m
[31m+                 <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">¿Eliminar repartidor?</h3>[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Esta acción no se puede deshacer.</p>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex gap-3">[39m
[31m+               <button[39m
[31m+                 onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(null)}[39m
[31m+                 className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 Cancelar[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+               <button[39m
[31m+                 onClick={() => handleDelete(confirmDelete)}[39m
[31m+                 disabled={!!deleting}[39m
[31m+                 className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {deleting ? "Eliminando..." : "Sí, eliminar"}[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Asignaciones[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function AsignacionesTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<DeliveryAssignment[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [partners, setPartners]       = useState<DeliveryPartner[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError]             = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [assignModal, setAssignModal] = useState<{ open: boolean; orderId?: string }>({ open: false });[39m
[31m+   const [selectedPartner, setSelectedPartner] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [assigning, setAssigning]     = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const load = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/delivery/assignments").then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject())),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/delivery/partners").then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject())),[39m
[31m+     ])[39m
[31m+       .then(([a, p]) => {[39m
[31m+         setAssignments(Array.isArray(a) ? a : []);[39m
[31m+         setPartners(Array.isArray(p) ? p.filter((x: DeliveryPartner) => x.isActive) : []);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setError("No se pudieron cargar las asignaciones."))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleAssign = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!selectedPartner || !assignModal.orderId) return;[39m
[31m+     setAssigning(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/delivery/assignments", {[39m
[31m+         method: "POST",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ orderId: assignModal.orderId, partnerId: selectedPartner }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+       const saved = await res.json();[39m
[31m+       setAssignments((prev) => [saved, ...prev]);[39m
[31m+       setAssignModal({ open: false });[39m
[31m+       setSelectedPartner("");[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al crear la asignación.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setAssigning(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {error && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+           <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {error}[39m
[31m+           <button onClick={() => setError(null)} className="ml-auto"><X className="h-4 w-4" /></button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">{assignments.length} asignación{assignments.length !== 1 ? "es" : ""}</p>[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setAssignModal({ open: true })}[39m
[31m+           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-sm font-bold hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           Asignar[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {assignments.length === 0 && !error ? ([39m
[31m+         <EmptyState icon={ClipboardList} title="Sin asignaciones registradas" description="Asigna repartidores a ordenes pendientes." />[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl  overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+             <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+               <thead className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+                 <tr>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Orden</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Repartidor</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Tarifa</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-center px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Estado</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">Asignado</th>[39m
[31m+                 </tr>[39m
[31m+               </thead>[39m
[31m+               <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">[39m
[31m+                 {assignments.map((a) => {[39m
[31m+                   const sc = ASSIGNMENT_STATUS_CONFIG[a.status] ?? {[39m
[31m+                     label: a.status, className: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600",[39m
[31m+                   };[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <tr key={a.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                         #{a.orderId.slice(-8).toUpperCase()}[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                         {a.partnerName}[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-gray-600">[39m
[31m+                         S/{a.fee.toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                         <span className={cn("inline-flex px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold", sc.className)}>[39m
[31m+                           {sc.label}[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-xs text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">[39m
[31m+                         <span className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">[39m
[31m+                           <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                           {new Date(a.assignedAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" })}[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                     </tr>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 })}[39m
[31m+               </tbody>[39m
[31m+             </table>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Modal asignar */}[39m
[31m+       {assignModal.open && ([39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50"[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setAssignModal({ open: false })}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className="bg-white rounded-xl w-full max-w-sm p-5 space-y-4"[39m
[31m+             onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+               <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">Asignar repartidor</h3>[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setAssignModal({ open: false })} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                 <X className="h-5 w-5" />[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">ID de orden (opcional)</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                 value={assignModal.orderId ?? ""}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setAssignModal((p) => ({ ...p, orderId: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Ej: ORD-12345"[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Repartidor *</label>[39m
[31m+               <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+                 <select[39m
[31m+                   value={selectedPartner}[39m
[31m+                   onChange={(e) => setSelectedPartner(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+                   className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary appearance-none transition-all"[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   <option value="">Seleccionar repartidor...</option>[39m
[31m+                   {partners.map((p) => ([39m
[31m+                     <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>[39m
[31m+                       {p.name} — {p.zone} — S/{p.fee.toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+                     </option>[39m
[31m+                   ))}[39m
[31m+                 </select>[39m
[31m+                 <ChevronDown className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none" />[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">[39m
[31m+               <button[39m
[31m+                 onClick={() => setAssignModal({ open: false })}[39m
[31m+                 className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 Cancelar[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+               <button[39m
[31m+                 onClick={handleAssign}[39m
[31m+                 disabled={assigning || !selectedPartner}[39m
[31m+                 className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-white bg-primary hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {assigning ? ([39m
[31m+                   <div className="h-4 w-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+                 ) : ([39m
[31m+                   <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+                 Asignar[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Permisos[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function PermisosTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [permissions, setPermissions] = useState<StorePermission[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError]             = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [savingId, setSavingId]       = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const load = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/store-permissions")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject()))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => setPermissions(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []))[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setError("No se pudieron cargar los permisos."))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const togglePermission = async (permId: string, perm: string) => {[39m
[31m+     const target = permissions.find((p) => p.id === permId);[39m
[31m+     if (!target) return;[39m
[31m+     const has = target.permissions.includes(perm);[39m
[31m+     const newPerms = has[39m
[31m+       ? target.permissions.filter((p) => p !== perm)[39m
[31m+       : [...target.permissions, perm];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     setSavingId(permId);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/store-permissions/${permId}`, {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ permissions: newPerms }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+       setPermissions((prev) =>[39m
[31m+         prev.map((p) => (p.id === permId ? { ...p, permissions: newPerms } : p))[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al actualizar permisos.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setSavingId(null);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {error && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+           <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {error}[39m
[31m+           <button onClick={() => setError(null)} className="ml-auto"><X className="h-4 w-4" /></button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {permissions.length === 0 && !error ? ([39m
[31m+         <EmptyState icon={Shield} title="Sin permisos configurados" description="Los permisos de acceso a la tienda apareceran aqui." />[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl  overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+             <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+               <thead className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+                 <tr>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Usuario</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">Tipo</th>[39m
[31m+                   {PERMISSION_TYPES.map((p) => ([39m
[31m+                     <th key={p} className="text-center px-3 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                       {p}[39m
[31m+                     </th>[39m
[31m+                   ))}[39m
[31m+                 </tr>[39m
[31m+               </thead>[39m
[31m+               <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">[39m
[31m+                 {permissions.map((p) => ([39m
[31m+                   <tr key={p.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{p.userName}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{p.userEmail}</p>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 hidden sm:table-cell">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700">[39m
[31m+                         {p.permissionType}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     {PERMISSION_TYPES.map((perm) => ([39m
[31m+                       <td key={perm} className="px-3 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                         <button[39m
[31m+                           onClick={() => togglePermission(p.id, perm)}[39m
[31m+                           disabled={savingId === p.id}[39m
[31m+                           className={cn([39m
[31m+                             "w-5 h-5 rounded border-2 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto transition-colors",[39m
[31m+                             p.permissions.includes(perm)[39m
[31m+                               ? "bg-primary border-primary"[39m
[31m+                               : "bg-white border-gray-300 hover:border-primary",[39m
[31m+                             savingId === p.id && "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed"[39m
[31m+                           )}[39m
[31m+                           title={p.permissions.includes(perm) ? `Quitar permiso ${perm}` : `Otorgar permiso ${perm}`}[39m
[31m+                         >[39m
[31m+                           {p.permissions.includes(perm) && ([39m
[31m+                             <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3 text-white" />[39m
[31m+                           )}[39m
[31m+                         </button>[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                     ))}[39m
[31m+                   </tr>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </tbody>[39m
[31m+             </table>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Tab: Solicitudes de repartidores[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ interface DriverApplication {[39m
[31m+   id: string;[39m
[31m+   title: string;[39m
[31m+   body: string;[39m
[31m+   readAt: string | null;[39m
[31m+   createdAt: string;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function parseApplicationBody(body: string) {[39m
[31m+   // Format: "Name (Phone) - Zona: zone - Vehículo: type - Horario: avail"[39m
[31m+   const match = body.match([39m
[31m+     /^(.+?)\s*\((.+?)\)\s*-\s*Zona:\s*(.+?)\s*-\s*Vehículo:\s*(.+?)\s*-\s*Horario:\s*(.+)$/[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   if (!match) return { name: body, phone: "", zone: "", vehicle: "", availability: "" };[39m
[31m+   return {[39m
[31m+     name: match[1].trim(),[39m
[31m+     phone: match[2].trim(),[39m
[31m+     zone: match[3].trim(),[39m
[31m+     vehicle: match[4].trim(),[39m
[31m+     availability: match[5].trim(),[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const AVAILABILITY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+   manana: "Mañana",[39m
[31m+   tarde: "Tarde",[39m
[31m+   noche: "Noche",[39m
[31m+   full: "Todo el día",[39m
[31m+   fines: "Fines de semana",[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const VEHICLE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+   moto: "Moto",[39m
[31m+   bicicleta: "Bicicleta",[39m
[31m+   auto: "Auto",[39m
[31m+   a_pie: "A pie",[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Ranking Tab[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ interface RankingEntry {[39m
[31m+   id: string;[39m
[31m+   name: string;[39m
[31m+   phone: string;[39m
[31m+   zone: string;[39m
[31m+   vehicleType: string;[39m
[31m+   rating: number;[39m
[31m+   isActive: boolean;[39m
[31m+   baseFee: number;[39m
[31m+   deliveries: number;[39m
[31m+   pending: number;[39m
[31m+   avgFee: number;[39m
[31m+   avgTimeMin: number;[39m
[31m+   score: number;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const RANK_MEDALS = ["🥇", "🥈", "🥉"];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function RankingTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<RankingEntry[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [period, setPeriod] = useState<"semana" | "mes" | "todo">("mes");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchRanking = useCallback((p: string) => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch(`/api/delivery/ranking?period=${p}`)[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : { ranking: [] }))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => setData(d.ranking ?? []))[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setData([]))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchRanking(period); }, [fetchRanking, period]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const PERIODS: { id: "semana" | "mes" | "todo"; label: string }[] = [[39m
[31m+     { id: "semana", label: "Esta semana" },[39m
[31m+     { id: "mes",    label: "Este mes" },[39m
[31m+     { id: "todo",   label: "Todo" },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Period selector */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+         {PERIODS.map((p) => ([39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             key={p.id}[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => setPeriod(p.id)}[39m
[31m+             className={cn([39m
[31m+               "px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors",[39m
[31m+               period === p.id[39m
[31m+                 ? "bg-primary text-white"[39m
[31m+                 : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {p.label}[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {loading ? ([39m
[31m+         <TableSkeleton />[39m
[31m+       ) : data.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+           <Trophy className="h-10 w-10 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm">No hay datos de ranking para este periodo</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+           <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+             <thead>[39m
[31m+               <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 text-left text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-2">#</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-3">Repartidor</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-3">Zona</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-3 text-center">Rating</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-3 text-center">Entregas</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-3 text-center">Prom. min</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 pr-3 text-center">Prom. tarifa</th>[39m
[31m+                 <th className="pb-2 text-center">Score</th>[39m
[31m+               </tr>[39m
[31m+             </thead>[39m
[31m+             <tbody>[39m
[31m+               {data.map((entry, idx) => ([39m
[31m+                 <tr[39m
[31m+                   key={entry.id}[39m
[31m+                   className={cn([39m
[31m+                     "border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50/50 transition-colors",[39m
[31m+                     idx < 3 && "bg-amber-50/30"[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-2 font-bold text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+                     {idx < 3 ? RANK_MEDALS[idx] : idx + 1}[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-3">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                       <div[39m
[31m+                         className={cn([39m
[31m+                           "h-8 w-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold text-white shrink-0",[39m
[31m+                           entry.isActive ? "bg-primary" : "bg-gray-300"[39m
[31m+                         )}[39m
[31m+                       >[39m
[31m+                         {entry.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                       <div>[39m
[31m+                         <p className="font-semibold text-gray-900 text-xs">{entry.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                         <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{entry.vehicleType}</p>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-3">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-xs text-gray-600 flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                       <MapPin className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                       {entry.zone}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-600">[39m
[31m+                       <Star className="h-3 w-3 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />[39m
[31m+                       {entry.rating.toFixed(1)}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-800">{entry.deliveries}</span>[39m
[31m+                     {entry.pending > 0 && ([39m
[31m+                       <span className="ml-1 text-[10px] text-amber-500">+{entry.pending} pen.</span>[39m
[31m+                     )}[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-xs text-gray-600">[39m
[31m+                       <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                       {entry.avgTimeMin > 0 ? `${entry.avgTimeMin} min` : "—"}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">[39m
[31m+                       S/ {entry.avgFee.toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                   <td className="py-2.5 text-center">[39m
[31m+                     <span[39m
[31m+                       className={cn([39m
[31m+                         "inline-flex items-center justify-center h-7 w-12 rounded-full text-xs font-extrabold",[39m
[31m+                         entry.score >= 70[39m
[31m+                           ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"[39m
[31m+                           : entry.score >= 40[39m
[31m+                             ? "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"[39m
[31m+                             : "bg-red-100 text-red-600"[39m
[31m+                       )}[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       {entry.score}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </td>[39m
[31m+                 </tr>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </tbody>[39m
[31m+           </table>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function SolicitudesTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [apps, setApps] = useState<DriverApplication[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [processing, setProcessing] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"pending" | "all">("pending");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchApps = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/admin/driver-applications")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : { data: [] }))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => setApps(d.data ?? []))[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setApps([]))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchApps(); }, [fetchApps]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleAction = async (id: string, action: "approve" | "reject") => {[39m
[31m+     setProcessing(id);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/admin/driver-applications", {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ action, notificationId: id }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         fetchApps();[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       // silently fail[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setProcessing(null);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const filtered = filter === "pending"[39m
[31m+     ? apps.filter((a) => !a.readAt)[39m
[31m+     : apps;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const pendingCount = apps.filter((a) => !a.readAt).length;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Filter bar */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setFilter("pending")}[39m
[31m+           className={cn([39m
[31m+             "px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors",[39m
[31m+             filter === "pending"[39m
[31m+               ? "bg-primary text-white"[39m
[31m+               : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           Pendientes {pendingCount > 0 && `(${pendingCount})`}[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setFilter("all")}[39m
[31m+           className={cn([39m
[31m+             "px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors",[39m
[31m+             filter === "all"[39m
[31m+               ? "bg-primary text-white"[39m
[31m+               : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           Todas[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={fetchApps}[39m
[31m+           className="ml-auto p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <RefreshCw className={cn("h-4 w-4", loading && "animate-spin")} />[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {loading ? ([39m
[31m+         <TableSkeleton />[39m
[31m+       ) : filtered.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-12">[39m
[31m+           <FileText className="h-12 w-12 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-3" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">[39m
[31m+             {filter === "pending" ? "No hay solicitudes pendientes" : "No hay solicitudes"}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+           {filtered.map((app) => {[39m
[31m+             const data = parseApplicationBody(app.body);[39m
[31m+             const isPending = !app.readAt;[39m
[31m+             const isProcessing = processing === app.id;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             return ([39m
[31m+               <div[39m
[31m+                 key={app.id}[39m
[31m+                 className={cn([39m
[31m+                   "bg-white border rounded-xl p-4  transition-all",[39m
[31m+                   isPending ? "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/30" : "border-gray-200 opacity-70"[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">[39m
[31m+                       <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm truncate">{data.name}</h4>[39m
[31m+                       {isPending ? ([39m
[31m+                         <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-700">[39m
[31m+                           Pendiente[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       ) : ([39m
[31m+                         <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-gray-100 text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                           Revisado[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       )}[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                         <Phone className="h-3 w-3" /> {data.phone}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                         <MapPin className="h-3 w-3" /> {data.zone}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                         <Truck className="h-3 w-3" /> {VEHICLE_LABELS[data.vehicle] ?? data.vehicle}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                         <Clock className="h-3 w-3" /> {AVAILABILITY_LABELS[data.availability] ?? data.availability}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">[39m
[31m+                       {new Date(app.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE", {[39m
[31m+                         year: "numeric", month: "short", day: "numeric", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit",[39m
[31m+                       })}[39m
[31m+                     </p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   {isPending && ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                       <button[39m
[31m+                         onClick={() => handleAction(app.id, "approve")}[39m
[31m+                         disabled={isProcessing}[39m
[31m+                         className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                       >[39m
[31m+                         <ThumbsUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Aprobar[39m
[31m+                       </button>[39m
[31m+                       <button[39m
[31m+                         onClick={() => handleAction(app.id, "reject")}[39m
[31m+                         disabled={isProcessing}[39m
[31m+                         className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                       >[39m
[31m+                         <ThumbsDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Rechazar[39m
[31m+                       </button>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           })}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ interface DeliveryKPIs {[39m
[31m+   activePartners: number;[39m
[31m+   deliveriesToday: number;[39m
[31m+   pendingDeliveries: number;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ export default function DeliveryPartnersModule() {[39m
[31m+   const [tab, setTab] = useState<TabId>(TABS[0].id);[39m
[31m+   const [kpis, setKpis] = useState<DeliveryKPIs>({[39m
[31m+     activePartners: 0,[39m
[31m+     deliveriesToday: 0,[39m
[31m+     pendingDeliveries: 0,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   const [kpisLoading, setKpisLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const refreshKpis = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setKpisLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/delivery/kpis")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => { if (d) setKpis(d as DeliveryKPIs); })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setKpisLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { refreshKpis(); }, [refreshKpis]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       <AdminModuleHeader[39m
[31m+         title="Delivery"[39m
[31m+         description="Gestiona repartidores, asignaciones y permisos"[39m
[31m+         icon={Truck}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={refreshKpis}[39m
[31m+           className="p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+           title="Actualizar"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <RefreshCw className={cn("h-4 w-4", kpisLoading && "animate-spin")} />[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </AdminModuleHeader>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* KPI strip */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {[[39m
[31m+           { label: "Repartidores activos", value: kpis.activePartners,     color: "text-primary" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Entregas hoy",          value: kpis.deliveriesToday,    color: "text-emerald-600" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Pendientes",            value: kpis.pendingDeliveries,  color: "text-amber-600" },[39m
[31m+         ].map(({ label, value, color }) => ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             key={label}[39m
[31m+             className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 sm:p-4  text-center"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {kpisLoading ? ([39m
[31m+               <div className="h-7 w-12 mx-auto bg-gray-200 rounded animate-pulse" />[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               <p className={cn("text-2xl font-extrabold", color)}>{value}</p>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 leading-tight">{label}</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <AdminTabBar[39m
[31m+         tabs={TABS}[39m
[31m+         activeTab={tab}[39m

[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   { id: "reportes" as const,         label: "Reportes",                 icon: FileBarChart },[39m
[31m+   { id: "inteligencia" as const,     label: "Inteligencia",             icon: Sparkles     },[39m
