[31m+ // ── Mejora 11: Semáforo de salud financiera ──────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ type HealthData = {[39m
[31m+   ingresos: number;[39m
[31m+   gastos: number;[39m
[31m+   efectivo: number;[39m
[31m+   gastosMensuales: number;[39m
[31m+   fiadosVencidos: number;[39m
[31m+   payablesVencidos: number;[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function calcHealthScore(d: HealthData) {[39m
[31m+   // Factor 1: Margen[39m
[31m+   const margen = d.ingresos > 0 ? ((d.ingresos - d.gastos) / d.ingresos) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+   const margenPts = margen > 25 ? 33 : margen >= 15 ? 20 : 5;[39m
[31m+   // Factor 2: Liquidez[39m
[31m+   const liquidez = d.gastosMensuales > 0 ? d.efectivo / d.gastosMensuales : 0;[39m
[31m+   const liquidezPts = liquidez > 2 ? 33 : liquidez >= 1 ? 20 : 5;[39m
[31m+   // Factor 3: Deudas[39m
[31m+   const deudaRatio = d.ingresos > 0 ? ((d.fiadosVencidos + d.payablesVencidos) / d.ingresos) * 100 : 100;[39m
[31m+   const deudaPts = deudaRatio < 10 ? 34 : deudaRatio <= 30 ? 20 : 5;[39m
[31m+   const total = margenPts + liquidezPts + deudaPts;[39m
[31m+   return { total, margenPts, liquidezPts, deudaPts, margen, liquidez, deudaRatio };[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars[39m
[31m+ function HealthSemaphore() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<HealthData | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+     ])[39m
[31m+       .then(([expenses, kpis]) => {[39m
[31m+         const ingresos = kpis?.ventasMes ?? kpis?.salesMonth ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const gastos = expenses?.totalMonth ?? expenses?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const efectivo = kpis?.cashToday ?? kpis?.efectivoHoy ?? ingresos * 0.3;[39m
[31m+         const fiadosVencidos = kpis?.fiadosVencidosMonto ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const payablesVencidos = kpis?.payablesVencidosMonto ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         setData({ ingresos, gastos, efectivo, gastosMensuales: gastos, fiadosVencidos, payablesVencidos });[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const score = useMemo(() => data ? calcHealthScore(data) : null, [data]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading || !score) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-20 w-20 rounded-full bg-gray-200 mx-auto" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const color = score.total > 70 ? "#22c55e" : score.total >= 40 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444";[39m
[31m+   const label = score.total > 70 ? "Saludable" : score.total >= 40 ? "Precaucion" : "Critico";[39m
[31m+   const bgRing = score.total > 70 ? "ring-green-200" : score.total >= 40 ? "ring-amber-200" : "ring-red-200";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const factors = [[39m
[31m+     { label: "Margen", pts: score.margenPts, max: 33, detail: `${score.margen.toFixed(1)}%` },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Liquidez", pts: score.liquidezPts, max: 33, detail: `${score.liquidez.toFixed(1)}x` },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Deudas", pts: score.deudaPts, max: 34, detail: `${score.deudaRatio.toFixed(1)}%` },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 ">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-4">[39m
[31m+         {/* Circulo grande */}[39m
[31m+         <div className={`w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ring-4 ${bgRing} shrink-0`} style={{ backgroundColor: `${color}20` }}>[39m
[31m+           <div className="text-center">[39m
[31m+             <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold" style={{ color }}>{score.total}</span>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[9px] font-bold" style={{ color }}>{label}</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {/* Mini barras */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex-1 w-full space-y-2">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Salud Financiera</p>[39m
[31m+           {factors.map(f => ([39m
[31m+             <div key={f.label} className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-gray-500 w-14">{f.label}</span>[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex-1 h-2 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+                 <div[39m
[31m+                   className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"[39m
[31m+                   style={{ width: `${(f.pts / f.max) * 100}%`, backgroundColor: f.pts === f.max ? "#22c55e" : f.pts >= f.max * 0.6 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444" }}[39m
[31m+                 />[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 w-10 text-right">{f.detail}</span>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Mejora 12: Gráfica comparativa mes a mes ─────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const MESES = ["Ene", "Feb", "Mar", "Abr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Ago", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dic"];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function ComparativoMensual() {[39m
[31m+   const [chartData, setChartData] = useState<Array<{ mes: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; utilidad: number }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/sales?limit=5000").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+     ])[39m
[31m+       .then(([expenses, sales]) => {[39m
[31m+         const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+         const months: Array<{ mes: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; utilidad: number }> = [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+           const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);[39m
[31m+           const monthKey = d.toISOString().slice(0, 7);[39m
[31m+           const label = MESES[d.getMonth()];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           // Ingresos: sum sales in this month[39m
[31m+           const monthSales = Array.isArray(sales)[39m
[31m+             ? sales.filter((s: { createdAt?: string; total: number }) => (s.createdAt ?? "").startsWith(monthKey))[39m
[31m+             : [];[39m
[31m+           const ingresos = monthSales.reduce((sum: number, s: { total: number }) => sum + (s.total ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           // Gastos: from monthly breakdown or estimate[39m
[31m+           let gastos = 0;[39m
[31m+           if (expenses?.monthly && Array.isArray(expenses.monthly)) {[39m
[31m+             const m = expenses.monthly.find((e: { month: string; total: number }) => e.month === monthKey);[39m
[31m+             gastos = m?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+           } else if (expenses?.totalMonth && i === 0) {[39m
[31m+             gastos = expenses.totalMonth;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           months.push({ mes: label, ingresos: Math.round(ingresos), gastos: Math.round(gastos), utilidad: Math.round(ingresos - gastos) });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         setChartData(months);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-75 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (chartData.length === 0 || chartData.every(d => d.ingresos === 0 && d.gastos === 0)) {[39m
[31m+     return null;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 ">[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-3">Comparativo Mensual</p>[39m
[31m+       <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>[39m
[31m+         <BarChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 5, right: 10, left: 0, bottom: 5 }}>[39m
[31m+           <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+           <XAxis dataKey="mes" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />[39m
[31m+           <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatCurrency(v, { decimals: 0 })} />[39m
[31m+           <Tooltip[39m
[31m+             formatter={(value: unknown, name: unknown) => {[39m
[31m+               const v = Number(value);[39m
[31m+               const n = String(name);[39m
[31m+               return [formatCurrency(v, { decimals: 0 }), n === "ingresos" ? "Ingresos" : n === "gastos" ? "Gastos" : "Utilidad"];[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+             labelFormatter={(label: unknown) => `${String(label)} 2026`}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+           <Legend formatter={(value: unknown) => { const v = String(value); return v === "ingresos" ? "Ingresos" : v === "gastos" ? "Gastos" : "Utilidad"; }} />[39m
[31m+           <Bar dataKey="ingresos" fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+           <Bar dataKey="gastos" fill="#e63946" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+           <Bar dataKey="utilidad" fill="#457b9d" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+           {/* Mejora 14: Linea de punto de equilibrio (promedio gastos) */}[39m
[31m+           {(() => {[39m
[31m+             const avgGastos = chartData.reduce((s, d) => s + d.gastos, 0) / Math.max(chartData.length, 1);[39m
[31m+             return avgGastos > 0 ? ([39m
[31m+               <ReferenceLine[39m
[31m+                 y={avgGastos}[39m
[31m+                 stroke="#e63946"[39m
[31m+                 strokeDasharray="5 5"[39m
[31m+                 strokeWidth={1.5}[39m
[31m+                 label={{ value: `PE: S/${Math.round(avgGastos)}`, position: "right", fill: "#e63946", fontSize: 10 }}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             ) : null;[39m
[31m+           })()}[39m
[31m+         </BarChart>[39m
[31m+       </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Mejora 14: Punto de equilibrio visual ─────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars[39m
[31m+ function PuntoEquilibrio() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<{ gastoDiario: number; ventasHoy: number } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+     ])[39m
[31m+       .then(([expenses, kpis]) => {[39m
[31m+         const gastosMes = expenses?.totalMonth ?? expenses?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+         const diasTranscurridos = Math.max(1, now.getDate());[39m
[31m+         const gastoDiario = gastosMes / diasTranscurridos;[39m
[31m+         const ventasHoy = kpis?.ventasHoy ?? kpis?.salesToday ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         setData({ gastoDiario: Math.round(gastoDiario), ventasHoy: Math.round(ventasHoy) });[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading || !data) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-16 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (data.gastoDiario === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const cubierto = data.ventasHoy >= data.gastoDiario;[39m
[31m+   const diferencia = Math.abs(data.ventasHoy - data.gastoDiario);[39m
[31m+   const pct = Math.min((data.ventasHoy / data.gastoDiario) * 100, 150);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 ">[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-3">Punto de Equilibrio Diario</p>[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex-1">[39m
[31m+           <div className="relative h-3 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+             <div[39m
[31m+               className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700"[39m
[31m+               style={{[39m
[31m+                 width: `${Math.min(pct, 100)}%`,[39m
[31m+                 backgroundColor: cubierto ? "#22c55e" : "#ef4444",[39m
[31m+               }}[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+             {/* Linea de punto de equilibrio */}[39m
[31m+             <div[39m
[31m+               className="absolute top-0 bottom-0 w-0.5 bg-gray-900"[39m
[31m+               style={{ left: `${Math.min(100 / (pct > 100 ? pct / 100 : 1), 100)}%` }}[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex justify-between mt-1.5 text-[10px] text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+             <span>S/0</span>[39m
[31m+             <span className="font-bold text-gray-700">Meta: S/{data.gastoDiario}</span>[39m
[31m+             <span>S/{Math.round(data.gastoDiario * 1.5)}</span>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-right shrink-0">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-lg font-extrabold" style={{ color: cubierto ? "#22c55e" : "#ef4444" }}>[39m
[31m+             S/{data.ventasHoy}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">vendido hoy</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <p className={`text-xs font-bold mt-2 ${cubierto ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-600"}`}>[39m
[31m+         {cubierto[39m
[31m+           ? `Hoy ya cubriste los gastos (+S/${diferencia} de ganancia)`[39m
[31m+           : `Faltan S/${diferencia} para cubrir gastos del dia`}[39m
[31m+       </p>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Mejora 15: Desglose de gastos con donut ──────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const EXPENSE_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+   "Mercaderia": "var(--color-primary)",[39m
[31m+   "mercaderia": "var(--color-primary)",[39m
[31m+   "Alquiler": "#f97316",[39m
[31m+   "alquiler": "#f97316",[39m
[31m+   "Servicios": "#457b9d",[39m
[31m+   "servicios": "#457b9d",[39m
[31m+   "Personal": "#9b5de5",[39m
[31m+   "personal": "#9b5de5",[39m
[31m+   "Transporte": "#e63946",[39m
[31m+   "transporte": "#e63946",[39m
[31m+   "Marketing": "#2dd4bf",[39m
[31m+   "marketing": "#2dd4bf",[39m
[31m+   "Otros": "#6b7280",[39m
[31m+   "otros": "#6b7280",[39m
[31m+   "limpieza": "#06b6d4",[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars[39m
[31m+ function GastosDonut() {[39m
[31m+   const [gastos, setGastos] = useState<Array<{ category: string; total: number }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 13: Promedio historico por categoria para detectar gastos inusuales[39m
[31m+   const [categoryAvg, setCategoryAvg] = useState<Map<string, number>>(new Map());[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/expenses?limit=2000")[39m
[31m+       .then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : [])[39m
[31m+       .then((data) => {[39m
[31m+         const items = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.expenses ?? []);[39m
[31m+         const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+         const startOfMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);[39m
[31m+         const threeMonthsAgo = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 3, 1);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // Group expenses of current month by category[39m
[31m+         const map = new Map<string, number>();[39m
[31m+         // Mejora 13: Track last 3 months by category for average[39m
[31m+         const historyMap = new Map<string, number[]>();[39m
[31m+         for (const e of items) {[39m
[31m+           const d = new Date(e.date ?? e.createdAt ?? "");[39m
[31m+           const cat = e.category ?? "otros";[39m
[31m+           if (d >= startOfMonth) {[39m
[31m+             map.set(cat, (map.get(cat) ?? 0) + (e.amount ?? 0));[39m
[31m+           } else if (d >= threeMonthsAgo && d < startOfMonth) {[39m
[31m+             if (!historyMap.has(cat)) historyMap.set(cat, []);[39m
[31m+             historyMap.get(cat)!.push(e.amount ?? 0);[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // Compute average per category from last 3 months[39m
[31m+         const avgMap = new Map<string, number>();[39m
[31m+         for (const [cat, amounts] of historyMap.entries()) {[39m
[31m+           const totalHist = amounts.reduce((s, a) => s + a, 0);[39m
[31m+           avgMap.set(cat, totalHist / 3); // average over 3 months[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         setCategoryAvg(avgMap);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const result = Array.from(map.entries())[39m
[31m+           .map(([category, total]) => ({ category, total: Math.round(total) }))[39m
[31m+           .filter(g => g.total > 0)[39m
[31m+           .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         setGastos(result);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-55 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (gastos.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const total = gastos.reduce((s, g) => s + g.total, 0);[39m
[31m+   const chartData = gastos.map(g => ({[39m
[31m+     name: g.category.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + g.category.slice(1),[39m
[31m+     value: g.total,[39m
[31m+     pct: total > 0 ? Math.round((g.total / total) * 100) : 0,[39m
[31m+   }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getColor = (category: string) => EXPENSE_COLORS[category] ?? EXPENSE_COLORS[category.toLowerCase()] ?? "#6b7280";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 ">[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-3">Gastos del Mes por Categoria</p>[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="relative w-45 h-45">[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">[39m
[31m+             <PieChart>[39m
[31m+               <Pie[39m
[31m+                 data={chartData}[39m
[31m+                 cx="50%"[39m
[31m+                 cy="50%"[39m
[31m+                 innerRadius={55}[39m
[31m+                 outerRadius={80}[39m
[31m+                 paddingAngle={2}[39m
[31m+                 dataKey="value"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {chartData.map((entry, index) => ([39m
[31m+                   <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={getColor(entry.name)} />[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </Pie>[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip[39m
[31m+                 formatter={(value: unknown, name: unknown) => [formatCurrency(Number(value), { decimals: 0 }), String(name)]}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </PieChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+           {/* Center label */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">[39m
[31m+             <div className="text-center">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Total</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className={cn("text-sm font-extrabold", total === 0 ? "text-gray-300" : "text-gray-900")}>{formatCurrency(total, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex-1 space-y-1.5 w-full">[39m
[31m+           {chartData.filter(g => g.name).map((g, i) => {[39m
[31m+             // Mejora 13: Detectar gasto inusual (> 2x promedio)[39m
[31m+             const avg = categoryAvg.get(g.name) ?? categoryAvg.get(g.name.toLowerCase()) ?? 0;[39m
[31m+             const isUnusual = avg > 0 && g.value > avg * 2;[39m
[31m+             const pctOver = avg > 0 ? Math.round(((g.value - avg) / avg) * 100) : 0;[39m
[31m+             return ([39m
[31m+               <div key={g.name || i} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                 <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: getColor(g.name) }} />[39m
[31m+                 <span className="flex-1 text-gray-700 font-semibold truncate">{g.name}</span>[39m
[31m+                 {isUnusual && ([39m
[31m+                   <span[39m
[31m+                     className="shrink-0 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 text-[9px] font-bold"[39m
[31m+                     title={`Este gasto es ${pctOver}% mayor al promedio de S/${Math.round(avg)} en ${g.name}`}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     Gasto inusual[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-gray-500">{formatCurrency(g.value, { decimals: 0 })}</span>[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-gray-400 w-8 text-right">{g.pct}%</span>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           })}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Mejora 16: Proyeccion de cierre de mes ───────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars[39m
[31m+ function ProyeccionCierreMes() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<{[39m
[31m+     ventasMes: number;[39m
[31m+     gastosMes: number;[39m
[31m+     diasTranscurridos: number;[39m
[31m+     diasTotales: number;[39m
[31m+   } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+     ])[39m
[31m+       .then(([expenses, kpis]) => {[39m
[31m+         const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+         const diasTranscurridos = Math.max(1, now.getDate());[39m
[31m+         const diasTotales = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();[39m
[31m+         const ventasMes = kpis?.ventasMes ?? kpis?.salesMonth ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const gastosMes = expenses?.totalMonth ?? expenses?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         setData({ ventasMes, gastosMes, diasTranscurridos, diasTotales });[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading || !data) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-32 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const ventasDiarias = data.ventasMes / data.diasTranscurridos;[39m
[31m+   const ventasProyectadas = Math.round(ventasDiarias * data.diasTotales);[39m
[31m+   const gastosProyectados = Math.round((data.gastosMes / data.diasTranscurridos) * data.diasTotales);[39m
[31m+   const utilidadProyectada = ventasProyectadas - gastosProyectados;[39m
[31m+   const progreso = data.diasTotales > 0 ? (data.diasTranscurridos / data.diasTotales) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+   const mesNombre = new Date().toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 ">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">[39m
[31m+         <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 text-purple-500" />[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">[39m
[31m+           Proyeccion {mesNombre.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mesNombre.slice(1)}[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Ventas proy.</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-base font-extrabold", ventasProyectadas === 0 ? "text-gray-300" : "text-primary")}>{formatCurrency(ventasProyectadas, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Gastos proy.</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-base font-extrabold", gastosProyectados === 0 ? "text-gray-300" : "text-red-500")}>{formatCurrency(gastosProyectados, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Utilidad est.</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-base font-extrabold", utilidadProyectada === 0 ? "text-gray-300" : utilidadProyectada >= 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-600")}>[39m
[31m+             {utilidadProyectada >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{formatCurrency(Math.abs(utilidadProyectada), { decimals: 0 })}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       {/* Progress bar */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+           <span>Dia {data.diasTranscurridos} de {data.diasTotales}</span>[39m
[31m+           <span>{Math.round(progreso)}% del mes</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-2 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 bg-primary"[39m
[31m+             style={{ width: `${progreso}%` }}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 text-center">[39m
[31m+           Ventas actuales: {formatCurrency(Math.round(data.ventasMes), { decimals: 0 })} de {formatCurrency(ventasProyectadas, { decimals: 0 })} proyectados[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Mejora 12: Resumen fiscal mensual ────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars[39m
[31m+ function ResumenFiscal() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<{ ventas: number; compras: number } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+     ])[39m
[31m+       .then(([kpis, expenses]) => {[39m
[31m+         const ventas = kpis?.ventasMes ?? kpis?.salesMonth ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const compras = expenses?.totalMonth ?? expenses?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         setData({ ventas, compras });[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading || !data) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 animate-pulse">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-32 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const igvCobrado = data.ventas * 0.18 / 1.18;[39m
[31m+   const igvPagado = data.compras * 0.18 / 1.18;[39m
[31m+   const igvNeto = igvCobrado - igvPagado;[39m
[31m+   const mesActual = new Date().toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 ">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">[39m
[31m+         <Calculator className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">[39m
[31m+           Resumen Fiscal — {mesActual.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mesActual.slice(1)}[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-gray-600">Ventas gravadas</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{formatCurrency(Math.round(data.ventas), { decimals: 0 })}</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-gray-600">IGV cobrado (18%)</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{formatCurrency(Math.round(igvCobrado), { decimals: 0 })}</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-gray-600">Compras deducibles</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{formatCurrency(Math.round(data.compras), { decimals: 0 })}</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-gray-600">IGV pagado</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{formatCurrency(Math.round(igvPagado), { decimals: 0 })}</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="border-t border-gray-200 pt-2 mt-2 flex justify-between text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-800">IGV a pagar</span>[39m
[31m+           <span className={`font-extrabold ${igvNeto > 0 ? "text-red-600" : "text-emerald-600"}`}>[39m
[31m+             {igvNeto > 0 ? "" : "-"}{formatCurrency(Math.abs(Math.round(igvNeto)), { decimals: 0 })}[39m
[31m+             {igvNeto <= 0 && <span className="text-xs font-normal ml-1">(crédito fiscal)</span>}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-3 italic">[39m
[31m+         Referencia aproximada — consulte con su contador[39m
[31m+       </p>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Mejora 9 nueva: Reporte Bancario ─────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function generarReporteBancario() {[39m
[31m+   Promise.all([[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/sales?limit=5000").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+   ])[39m
[31m+     .then(([expenses, sales, kpis]) => {[39m
[31m+       const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+       const allSales = Array.isArray(sales) ? sales : [];[39m
[31m+       const meses: Array<{ mes: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; utilidad: number }> = [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+         const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);[39m
[31m+         const monthKey = d.toISOString().slice(0, 7);[39m
[31m+         const label = d.toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });[39m
[31m+         const monthSales = allSales.filter((s: { createdAt?: string; total: number }) => (s.createdAt ?? "").startsWith(monthKey));[39m
[31m+         const ingresos = monthSales.reduce((sum: number, s: { total: number }) => sum + (s.total ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+         let gastos = 0;[39m
[31m+         if (expenses?.monthly && Array.isArray(expenses.monthly)) {[39m
[31m+           const m = expenses.monthly.find((e: { month: string; total: number }) => e.month === monthKey);[39m
[31m+           gastos = m?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         meses.push({ mes: label, ingresos: Math.round(ingresos), gastos: Math.round(gastos), utilidad: Math.round(ingresos - gastos) });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const totalIngresos = meses.reduce((s, m) => s + m.ingresos, 0);[39m
[31m+       const totalGastos = meses.reduce((s, m) => s + m.gastos, 0);[39m
[31m+       const totalUtilidad = totalIngresos - totalGastos;[39m
[31m+       const margen = totalIngresos > 0 ? ((totalUtilidad / totalIngresos) * 100).toFixed(1) : "0";[39m
[31m+       const clientesActivos = kpis?.clientesActivos ?? kpis?.customersActive ?? "--";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Proyeccion[39m
[31m+       const avgIngresosMensual = totalIngresos / 6;[39m
[31m+       const proyeccion = Math.round(avgIngresosMensual * 1.05);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const tablaRows = meses.map(m =>[39m
[31m+         `<tr><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd">${m.mes}</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:right">S/${m.ingresos.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:right">S/${m.gastos.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:right;font-weight:bold;color:${m.utilidad >= 0 ? "var(--color-primary)" : "#e63946"}">S/${m.utilidad.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td></tr>`[39m
[31m+       ).join("");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Barras simples CSS[39m
[31m+       const maxVal = Math.max(...meses.map(m => m.ingresos), 1);[39m
[31m+       const barrasHtml = meses.map(m =>[39m
[31m+         `<div style="display:flex;align-items:end;gap:4px;flex:1;flex-direction:column;text-align:center"><div style="background:var(--color-primary);width:30px;height:${Math.round((m.ingresos / maxVal) * 120)}px;border-radius:4px 4px 0 0"></div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#666">${m.mes.split(" ")[0].slice(0, 3)}</div></div>`[39m
[31m+       ).join("");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const fecha = now.toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "2-digit", month: "long", year: "numeric" });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const html = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Reporte Financiero - Buleje</title><style>body{font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;max-width:800px;margin:0 auto;padding:40px;color:#333;font-size:14px}h1{color:var(--color-primary);border-bottom:3px solid var(--color-primary);padding-bottom:10px;font-size:22px}h2{color:#333;margin-top:30px;font-size:16px;border-bottom:1px solid #ddd;padding-bottom:5px}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:15px 0}th{background:#f8f9fa;padding:10px 8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:left;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.5px}td{padding:8px;font-size:13px}.kpi{display:inline-block;background:#f8f9fa;border:1px solid #ddd;border-radius:8px;padding:15px 20px;margin:5px;text-align:center;min-width:150px}.kpi-label{font-size:11px;color:#666;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.5px}.kpi-value{font-size:20px;font-weight:bold;color:var(--color-primary);margin-top:4px}.footer{margin-top:40px;padding-top:15px;border-top:1px solid #ddd;color:#999;font-size:11px;text-align:center}@media print{body{padding:20px}}</style></head><body><h1>REPORTE FINANCIERO &mdash; Buleje</h1><p style="color:#666;font-size:12px">Per&iacute;odo: &uacute;ltimos 6 meses &middot; Generado el ${fecha}</p><h2>1. Datos del Negocio</h2><table><tr><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;width:200px;font-weight:bold">Razon Social</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd">Buleje</td></tr><tr><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;font-weight:bold">Ubicacion</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd">Pucallpa, Ucayali, Peru</td></tr><tr><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;font-weight:bold">Giro</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd">Comercio minorista - Abarrotes</td></tr></table><h2>2. Resumen de Ingresos</h2><table><thead><tr><th>Mes</th><th style="text-align:right">Ingresos</th><th style="text-align:right">Gastos</th><th style="text-align:right">Utilidad</th></tr></thead><tbody>${tablaRows}<tr style="background:#f0f0f0;font-weight:bold"><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd">TOTAL</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:right">S/${totalIngresos.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:right">S/${totalGastos.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td><td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;text-align:right;color:${totalUtilidad >= 0 ? "var(--color-primary)" : "#e63946"}">S/${totalUtilidad.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td></tr></tbody></table><h2>3. Tendencia de Ingresos</h2><div style="display:flex;align-items:end;gap:8px;height:140px;padding:10px;background:#fafafa;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:8px">${barrasHtml}</div><h2>4. Indicadores Clave</h2><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px"><div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-label">Margen de utilidad</div><div class="kpi-value">${margen}%</div></div><div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-label">Clientes activos</div><div class="kpi-value">${clientesActivos}</div></div><div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-label">Ingreso prom./mes</div><div class="kpi-value">S/${Math.round(avgIngresosMensual).toLocaleString("es-PE")}</div></div></div><h2>5. Proyeccion</h2><p>Basado en la tendencia de los ultimos 6 meses, el ingreso estimado para el proximo mes es: <strong style="color:var(--color-primary);font-size:18px">S/${proyeccion.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</strong></p><div class="footer">Generado el ${fecha} &mdash; Buleje &middot; Este reporte es de caracter informativo</div></body></html>`;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const w = window.open("", "_blank");[39m
[31m+       if (w) {[39m
[31m+         w.document.write(html);[39m
[31m+         w.document.close();[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+     .catch(() => {[39m
[31m+       alert("Error al generar el reporte. Intenta nuevamente.");[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ /* Semicircular gauge built from PieChart */[39m
[31m+ function GaugeChart({ value, max, label, unit, color }: { value: number; max: number; label: string; unit: string; color: string }) {[39m
[31m+   const pct = Math.min(Math.max(value / max, 0), 1);[39m
[31m+   const filled = pct * 100;[39m
[31m+   const empty = 100 - filled;[39m
[31m+   const data = [[39m
[31m+     { name: "filled", value: filled },[39m
[31m+     { name: "empty", value: empty },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4  flex flex-col items-center">[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">{label}</p>[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative w-35 h-20">[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={80}>[39m
[31m+           <PieChart>[39m
[31m+             <Pie[39m
[31m+               data={data}[39m
[31m+               cx="50%"[39m
[31m+               cy="100%"[39m
[31m+               startAngle={180}[39m
[31m+               endAngle={0}[39m
[31m+               innerRadius={45}[39m
[31m+               outerRadius={65}[39m
[31m+               dataKey="value"[39m
[31m+               stroke="none"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <Cell fill={color} />[39m
[31m+               <Cell fill="#e5e7eb" className="" />[39m
[31m+             </Pie>[39m
[31m+           </PieChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-end justify-center pb-0 pointer-events-none">[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-lg font-extrabold" style={{ color }}>{unit === "%" ? `${value.toFixed(0)}%` : `${value.toFixed(1)}x`}</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ // Flat wrapper (animations removed for professional style)[39m
[31m+ function StaggerItem({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode; index?: number }) {[39m
[31m+   return <div>{children}</div>;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ function FinanzasDashboard() {[39m
[31m+   const [kpis, setKpis] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});[39m
[31m+   const [monthlyData, setMonthlyData] = useState<Array<{ mes: string; fullMonth: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; utilidad: number }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [expensesByCategory, setExpensesByCategory] = useState<Array<{ name: string; value: number }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [paymentMethods, setPaymentMethods] = useState<Array<{ name: string; value: number }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [cashFlow, setCashFlow] = useState<Array<{ dia: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; balance: number }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 12: Click-to-filter en PieChart de gastos[39m
[31m+   const [gastosPieFilter, setGastosPieFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 13: Expand chart modal[39m
[31m+   const [expandedChart, setExpandedChart] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 20: Comparar meses[39m
[31m+   const [cmpMonth1, setCmpMonth1] = useState(() => { const d = new Date(); d.setMonth(d.getMonth() - 1); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 7); });[39m
[31m+   const [cmpMonth2, setCmpMonth2] = useState(() => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));[39m
[31m+   const [topPayables, setTopPayables] = useState<Array<{ name: string; monto: number; vencido: boolean }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [topFiados, setTopFiados] = useState<Array<{ name: string; monto: number; vencido: boolean }>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [projection, setProjection] = useState<{ ventasMes: number; gastosMes: number; diasTranscurridos: number; diasTotales: number } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [fiscal, setFiscal] = useState<{ ventas: number; compras: number } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [healthData, setHealthData] = useState<HealthData | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 1: Period selector[39m
[31m+   const [period, setPeriod] = useState<"today" | "7d" | "30d" | "month">("month");[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 3: Auto-refresh[39m
[31m+   const [lastRefresh, setLastRefresh] = useState(new Date());[39m
[31m+   const [minAgo, setMinAgo] = useState(0);[39m
[31m+   // Mock KPI deltas — lazy-init so Math.random runs once per mount (React Compiler purity rule)[39m
[31m+   const [kpiMockChanges] = useState<number[]>(() =>[39m
[31m+     Array.from({ length: KPI_DEFS.length }, () => Math.round((Math.random() - 0.3) * 30))[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 5: Favoritos[39m
[31m+   const finFavs = useFavoriteCharts("finanzas");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     Promise.allSettled([[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses/summary").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/sales?limit=5000").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/expenses?limit=2000").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/payables").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+       fetch("/api/fiados?status=ACTIVO").then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : []),[39m
[31m+     ]).then(([kR, eR, sR, exR, pR, fR]) => {[39m
[31m+       const kpisData = kR.status === "fulfilled" ? kR.value : null;[39m
[31m+       const expSummary = eR.status === "fulfilled" ? eR.value : null;[39m
[31m+       const salesRaw = sR.status === "fulfilled" ? sR.value : [];[39m
[31m+       const expensesRaw = exR.status === "fulfilled" ? exR.value : [];[39m
[31m+       const payablesRaw = pR.status === "fulfilled" ? (Array.isArray(pR.value) ? pR.value : []) : [];[39m
[31m+       const fiadosRaw = fR.status === "fulfilled" ? (Array.isArray(fR.value) ? fR.value : []) : [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+       const sales = Array.isArray(salesRaw) ? salesRaw : [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── KPIs ──[39m
[31m+       const ingresos = kpisData?.ventasMes ?? kpisData?.salesMonth ?? 0;[39m
[31m+       const gastosMes = expSummary?.totalMonth ?? expSummary?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+       const utilidad = ingresos - gastosMes;[39m
[31m+       const margen = ingresos > 0 ? Math.round(((ingresos - gastosMes) / ingresos) * 100) : 0;[39m
[31m+       const deuda = kpisData?.payablesVencidosMonto ?? payablesRaw.reduce((s: number, p: { amount?: number; total?: number }) => s + (p.amount ?? p.total ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+       const fiados = kpisData?.fiadosPendienteMonto ?? kpisData?.fiadosVencidosMonto ?? fiadosRaw.reduce((s: number, f: { total?: number; amount?: number }) => s + (f.total ?? f.amount ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+       const igvCobrado = ingresos * 0.18 / 1.18;[39m
[31m+       const igvPagado = gastosMes * 0.18 / 1.18;[39m
[31m+       const igvNeto = Math.round(igvCobrado - igvPagado);[39m
[31m+       const diasTranscurridos = Math.max(1, now.getDate());[39m
[31m+       const puntoEq = diasTranscurridos > 0 ? Math.round(gastosMes / diasTranscurridos) : 0;[39m
[31m+       setKpis({[39m
[31m+         ingresos: Math.round(ingresos), gastos: Math.round(gastosMes), utilidad: Math.round(utilidad),[39m
[31m+         margen, deuda: Math.round(deuda), fiados: Math.round(fiados), igv: igvNeto, puntoEq,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Fiscal ──[39m
[31m+       setFiscal({ ventas: ingresos, compras: gastosMes });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Projection ──[39m
[31m+       const diasTotales = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();[39m
[31m+       setProjection({ ventasMes: ingresos, gastosMes, diasTranscurridos, diasTotales });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Health ──[39m
[31m+       const efectivo = kpisData?.cashToday ?? kpisData?.efectivoHoy ?? ingresos * 0.3;[39m
[31m+       const fiadosVencidos = kpisData?.fiadosVencidosMonto ?? 0;[39m
[31m+       const payablesVencidos = kpisData?.payablesVencidosMonto ?? 0;[39m
[31m+       setHealthData({ ingresos, gastos: gastosMes, efectivo, gastosMensuales: gastosMes, fiadosVencidos, payablesVencidos });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Monthly chart (last 6 months) ──[39m
[31m+       const months: Array<{ mes: string; fullMonth: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; utilidad: number }> = [];[39m
[31m+       for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+         const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);[39m
[31m+         const monthKey = d.toISOString().slice(0, 7);[39m
[31m+         const label = MESES[d.getMonth()];[39m
[31m+         const fullLabel = d.toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });[39m
[31m+         const monthSales = sales.filter((s: { createdAt?: string; total: number }) => (s.createdAt ?? "").startsWith(monthKey));[39m
[31m+         const ing = monthSales.reduce((sum: number, s: { total: number }) => sum + (s.total ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+         let gas = 0;[39m
[31m+         if (expSummary?.monthly && Array.isArray(expSummary.monthly)) {[39m
[31m+           const m = expSummary.monthly.find((e: { month: string; total: number }) => e.month === monthKey);[39m
[31m+           gas = m?.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         } else if (expSummary?.totalMonth && i === 0) {[39m
[31m+           gas = expSummary.totalMonth;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         months.push({ mes: label, fullMonth: fullLabel, ingresos: Math.round(ing), gastos: Math.round(gas), utilidad: Math.round(ing - gas) });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setMonthlyData(months);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Expenses by category (donut) ──[39m
[31m+       const items = Array.isArray(expensesRaw) ? expensesRaw : (expensesRaw?.expenses ?? []);[39m
[31m+       const startOfMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);[39m
[31m+       const catMap = new Map<string, number>();[39m
[31m+       for (const e of items) {[39m
[31m+         const eDate = new Date(e.date ?? e.createdAt ?? "");[39m
[31m+         if (eDate >= startOfMonth) {[39m
[31m+           const cat = (e.category ?? "otros").charAt(0).toUpperCase() + (e.category ?? "otros").slice(1);[39m
[31m+           catMap.set(cat, (catMap.get(cat) ?? 0) + (e.amount ?? 0));[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setExpensesByCategory([39m
[31m+         Array.from(catMap.entries())[39m
[31m+           .map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value: Math.round(value) }))[39m
[31m+           .filter(g => g.value > 0)[39m
[31m+           .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value)[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Payment methods breakdown ──[39m
[31m+       const pmMap = new Map<string, number>();[39m
[31m+       for (const s of sales) {[39m
[31m+         const d = new Date(s.createdAt ?? "");[39m
[31m+         if (d >= startOfMonth) {[39m
[31m+           const method = (s.paymentMethod ?? s.metodoPago ?? "efectivo").charAt(0).toUpperCase() + (s.paymentMethod ?? s.metodoPago ?? "efectivo").slice(1).toLowerCase();[39m
[31m+           pmMap.set(method, (pmMap.get(method) ?? 0) + (s.total ?? 0));[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setPaymentMethods([39m
[31m+         Array.from(pmMap.entries())[39m
[31m+           .map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value: Math.round(value) }))[39m
[31m+           .filter(g => g.value > 0)[39m
[31m+           .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value)[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Cash flow (last 30 days) ──[39m
[31m+       const flowData: Array<{ dia: string; ingresos: number; gastos: number; balance: number }> = [];[39m
[31m+       for (let i = 29; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+         const dd = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - i);[39m
[31m+         const dayKey = dd.toISOString().slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+         const dayLabel = `${dd.getDate()}/${dd.getMonth() + 1}`;[39m
[31m+         const daySales = sales.filter((s: { createdAt?: string; total: number }) => (s.createdAt ?? "").startsWith(dayKey));[39m
[31m+         const dayIngresos = daySales.reduce((sum: number, s: { total: number }) => sum + (s.total ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+         const dayExpenses = items.filter((e: { date?: string; createdAt?: string; amount: number }) => (e.date ?? e.createdAt ?? "").slice(0, 10) === dayKey);[39m
[31m+         const dayGastos = dayExpenses.reduce((sum: number, e: { amount: number }) => sum + (e.amount ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+         flowData.push({ dia: dayLabel, ingresos: Math.round(dayIngresos), gastos: Math.round(dayGastos), balance: Math.round(dayIngresos - dayGastos) });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setCashFlow(flowData);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Top payables (proveedores) ──[39m
[31m+       const pGrouped = new Map<string, { monto: number; vencido: boolean }>();[39m
[31m+       for (const p of payablesRaw) {[39m
[31m+         const name = p.supplierName ?? p.supplier?.name ?? p.description ?? "Proveedor";[39m
[31m+         const prev = pGrouped.get(name) ?? { monto: 0, vencido: false };[39m
[31m+         prev.monto += (p.amount ?? p.total ?? 0);[39m
[31m+         if (p.status === "VENCIDO" || (p.dueDate && new Date(p.dueDate) < now)) prev.vencido = true;[39m
[31m+         pGrouped.set(name, prev);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setTopPayables([39m
[31m+         Array.from(pGrouped.entries())[39m
[31m+           .map(([name, d]) => ({ name: name.length > 18 ? name.slice(0, 18) + "..." : name, monto: Math.round(d.monto), vencido: d.vencido }))[39m
[31m+           .sort((a, b) => b.monto - a.monto)[39m
[31m+           .slice(0, 5)[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── Top fiados (deudores) ──[39m
[31m+       const fGrouped = new Map<string, { monto: number; vencido: boolean }>();[39m
[31m+       for (const f of fiadosRaw) {[39m
[31m+         const name = f.customerName ?? f.customer?.name ?? f.description ?? "Cliente";[39m
[31m+         const prev = fGrouped.get(name) ?? { monto: 0, vencido: false };[39m
[31m+         prev.monto += (f.total ?? f.amount ?? 0);[39m
[31m+         if (f.status === "VENCIDO" || (f.dueDate && new Date(f.dueDate) < now)) prev.vencido = true;[39m
[31m+         fGrouped.set(name, prev);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setTopFiados([39m
[31m+         Array.from(fGrouped.entries())[39m
[31m+           .map(([name, d]) => ({ name: name.length > 18 ? name.slice(0, 18) + "..." : name, monto: Math.round(d.monto), vencido: d.vencido }))[39m
[31m+           .sort((a, b) => b.monto - a.monto)[39m
[31m+           .slice(0, 5)[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+       setLastRefresh(new Date());[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 3: Auto-refresh timer[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const minuteInterval = setInterval(() => {[39m
[31m+       setMinAgo(Math.floor((Date.now() - lastRefresh.getTime()) / 60000));[39m
[31m+     }, 60000);[39m
[31m+     return () => clearInterval(minuteInterval);[39m
[31m+   }, [lastRefresh]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const healthScore = useMemo(() => healthData ? calcHealthScore(healthData) : null, [healthData]);[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+   const totalIncome = paymentMethods.reduce((s, g) => s + g.value, 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Projection calculations[39m
[31m+   const projVentasDiarias = projection ? projection.ventasMes / projection.diasTranscurridos : 0;[39m

[31m+   const projGastos = projection ? Math.round((projection.gastosMes / projection.diasTranscurridos) * projection.diasTotales) : 0;[39m

[31m+   const fiscIgvCobrado = fiscal ? fiscal.ventas * 0.18 / 1.18 : 0;[39m
[31m+   const fiscIgvPagado = fiscal ? fiscal.compras * 0.18 / 1.18 : 0;[39m
[31m+   const fiscIgvNeto = fiscIgvCobrado - fiscIgvPagado;[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+[39m

[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       </StaggerItem>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ════════ SECCION 9: Mejora 19 — Salud del Negocio (gauge 0-100) ════════ */}[39m
[31m+       {healthScore && ([39m
[31m+         <StaggerItem index={8}>[39m
[31m+           <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-6 ">[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">[39m
[31m+               <div className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: healthScore.total > 70 ? "#22c55e" : healthScore.total >= 40 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444" }} />[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Salud del Negocio</p>[39m
[31m+               <span className="ml-auto text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full" style={{[39m
[31m+                 backgroundColor: healthScore.total > 70 ? "#22c55e20" : healthScore.total >= 40 ? "#f59e0b20" : "#ef444420",[39m
[31m+                 color: healthScore.total > 70 ? "#22c55e" : healthScore.total >= 40 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444",[39m
[31m+               }}>[39m
[31m+                 {healthScore.total}/100[39m
[31m+               </span>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-6">[39m
[31m+               {/* Gauge semicircular */}[39m
[31m+               <div className="relative w-40 h-22.5 shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                 <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={90}>[39m
[31m+                   <PieChart>[39m
[31m+                     <Pie[39m
[31m+                       data={[{ name: "score", value: healthScore.total }, { name: "empty", value: 100 - healthScore.total }]}[39m
[31m+                       cx="50%" cy="100%" startAngle={180} endAngle={0} innerRadius={50} outerRadius={70} dataKey="value" stroke="none"[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       <Cell fill={healthScore.total > 70 ? "#22c55e" : healthScore.total >= 40 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444"} />[39m
[31m+                       <Cell fill="#e5e7eb" className="" />[39m
[31m+                     </Pie>[39m
[31m+                   </PieChart>[39m
[31m+                 </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-end justify-center pb-1 pointer-events-none">[39m
[31m+                   <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold" style={{ color: healthScore.total > 70 ? "#22c55e" : healthScore.total >= 40 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444" }}>[39m
[31m+                     {healthScore.total}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               {/* Breakdown */}[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex-1 w-full space-y-3">[39m
[31m+                 {[[39m
[31m+                   { label: "Margen bruto", pts: healthScore.margenPts, max: 33, detail: `${healthScore.margen.toFixed(1)}%`, desc: "Cuanto ganas por cada sol vendido" },[39m
[31m+                   { label: "Liquidez", pts: healthScore.liquidezPts, max: 33, detail: `${healthScore.liquidez.toFixed(1)}x`, desc: "Efectivo vs gastos mensuales" },[39m
[31m+                   { label: "Rotacion inv.", pts: 17, max: 25, detail: "Est.", desc: "Que tan rapido vendes tu stock" },[39m
[31m+                   { label: "Crecimiento", pts: Math.min(25, Math.max(5, monthlyData.length >= 2 && monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 2].ingresos > 0 ? Math.round(((monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 1].ingresos - monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 2].ingresos) / monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 2].ingresos) * 25 + 12.5) : 12)), max: 25, detail: monthlyData.length >= 2 ? `${Math.round(((monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 1].ingresos - monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 2].ingresos) / Math.max(monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 2].ingresos, 1)) * 100)}%` : "N/A", desc: "Ventas vs mes anterior" },[39m
[31m+                 ].map(f => ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={f.label}>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600">{f.label}</span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">{f.detail}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="h-2 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+                       <div className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style={{ width: `${(f.pts / f.max) * 100}%`, backgroundColor: f.pts >= f.max * 0.8 ? "#22c55e" : f.pts >= f.max * 0.5 ? "#f59e0b" : "#ef4444" }} />[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{f.desc}</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </StaggerItem>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ════════ SECCION 10: Mejora 20 — Comparativo entre meses ════════ */}[39m
[31m+       <StaggerItem index={9}>[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-6 ">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4 flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Comparar Meses</p>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               <select value={cmpMonth1} onChange={e => setCmpMonth1(e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 bg-white text-gray-700">[39m
[31m+                 {monthlyData.map(m => <option key={m.fullMonth} value={m.mes}>{m.mes}</option>)}[39m
[31m+               </select>[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">vs</span>[39m
[31m+               <select value={cmpMonth2} onChange={e => setCmpMonth2(e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 bg-white text-gray-700">[39m
[31m+                 {monthlyData.map(m => <option key={m.fullMonth} value={m.mes}>{m.mes}</option>)}[39m
[31m+               </select>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           {(() => {[39m
[31m+             const d1 = monthlyData.find(m => m.mes === cmpMonth1);[39m
[31m+             const d2 = monthlyData.find(m => m.mes === cmpMonth2);[39m
[31m+             if (!d1 || !d2) return <EmptyState icon={BarChart3} title="Selecciona meses con datos" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres ventas" />;[39m
[31m+             const diffIngresos = d1.ingresos > 0 ? Math.round(((d2.ingresos - d1.ingresos) / d1.ingresos) * 100) : 0;[39m
[31m+             const diffGastos = d1.gastos > 0 ? Math.round(((d2.gastos - d1.gastos) / d1.gastos) * 100) : 0;[39m
[31m+             const compareData = [[39m
[31m+               { tipo: "Ingresos", [cmpMonth1]: d1.ingresos, [cmpMonth2]: d2.ingresos },[39m
[31m+               { tipo: "Gastos", [cmpMonth1]: d1.gastos, [cmpMonth2]: d2.gastos },[39m
[31m+               { tipo: "Utilidad", [cmpMonth1]: d1.utilidad, [cmpMonth2]: d2.utilidad },[39m
[31m+             ];[39m
[31m+             return ([39m
[31m+               <>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-4">[39m
[31m+                   <div className="text-center p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold">Ventas</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className={cn("text-sm font-bold", diffIngresos >= 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-600")}>{diffIngresos >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{diffIngresos}%</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="text-center p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold">Gastos</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className={cn("text-sm font-bold", diffGastos <= 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-600")}>{diffGastos >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{diffGastos}%</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>[39m
[31m+                   <BarChart data={compareData}>[39m
[31m+                     <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(107,114,128,0.12)" />[39m
[31m+                     <XAxis dataKey="tipo" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+                     <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+                     <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+                     <Legend />[39m
[31m+                     <Bar dataKey={cmpMonth1} fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+                     <Bar dataKey={cmpMonth2} fill="#f97316" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+                   </BarChart>[39m
[31m+                 </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+               </>[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           })()}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </StaggerItem>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ════════ Expand Chart Modals ════════ */}[39m
[31m+       {expandedChart && ([39m
[31m+         <ChartExpandModal title={expandedChart === "ingresos-gastos" ? "Ingresos vs Gastos vs Utilidad" : expandedChart === "flujo-caja" ? "Flujo de Caja" : expandedChart === "gastos-cat" ? "Gastos por Categoria" : expandedChart} onClose={() => setExpandedChart(null)}>[39m
[31m+             {expandedChart === "ingresos-gastos" && monthlyData.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+               <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+                 <ComposedChart data={monthlyData}>[39m
[31m+                   <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+                   <XAxis dataKey="mes" tick={{ fontSize: 14 }} />[39m
[31m+                   <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m
[31m+                   <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+                   <Legend />[39m
[31m+                   <Bar dataKey="ingresos" fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+                   <Bar dataKey="gastos" fill="#e63946" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+                   <Line type="monotone" dataKey="utilidad" stroke="#3b82f6" strokeWidth={3} dot={{ r: 5, fill: "#3b82f6" }} />[39m
[31m+                 </ComposedChart>[39m
[31m+               </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             {expandedChart === "flujo-caja" && cashFlow.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+               <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+                 <AreaChart data={cashFlow}>[39m
[31m+                   <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+                   <XAxis dataKey="dia" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />[39m
[31m+                   <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m
[31m+                   <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+                   <Area type="monotone" dataKey="ingresos" stroke="#22c55e" fill="#22c55e20" strokeWidth={2} />[39m
[31m+                   <Area type="monotone" dataKey="gastos" stroke="#ef4444" fill="#ef444420" strokeWidth={2} />[39m
[31m+                   <Area type="monotone" dataKey="balance" stroke="var(--color-primary)" fill="var(--color-primary)30" strokeWidth={3} />[39m
[31m+                 </AreaChart>[39m
[31m+               </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             {expandedChart === "gastos-cat" && expensesByCategory.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+               <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+                 <PieChart>[39m
[31m+                   <Pie data={expensesByCategory} cx="50%" cy="50%" innerRadius={100} outerRadius={200} paddingAngle={3} dataKey="value" label>[39m
[31m+                     {expensesByCategory.map((_, index) => ([39m
[31m+                       <Cell key={`ec-big-${index}`} fill={DASHBOARD_EXPENSE_COLORS[index % DASHBOARD_EXPENSE_COLORS.length]} />[39m
[31m+                     ))}[39m
[31m+                   </Pie>[39m
[31m+                   <Tooltip formatter={(value: unknown, name: unknown) => [formatCurrency(Number(value), { decimals: 0 }), String(name)]} />[39m
[31m+                   <Legend />[39m
[31m+                 </PieChart>[39m
[31m+               </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+         </ChartExpandModal>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── IntelligenceKPIStrip — quick KPIs for Inteligencia tab ───────────────────[39m
[31m+ function IntelligenceKPIStrip() {[39m
[31m+   const [kpis, setKpis] = useState<Record<string, number> | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/analytics/kpis-v2", { credentials: "include" })[39m
[31m+       .then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null)[39m
[31m+       .then(d => {[39m
[31m+         if (d) setKpis({[39m
[31m+           margen: d.margenPromedio ?? d.margin ?? 0,[39m
[31m+           ventasMes: d.ventasMes ?? d.salesMonth ?? 0,[39m
[31m+           ticketPromedio: d.ticketPromedio ?? d.avgTicket ?? 0,[39m
[31m+           productos: d.productosActivos ?? d.activeProducts ?? 0,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {});[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!kpis) return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const cards = [[39m
[31m+     { label: "Margen", value: `${kpis.margen.toFixed(1)}%`, color: "text-primary" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Ventas/mes", value: formatCurrency(kpis.ventasMes, { decimals: 0 }), color: "text-primary" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Ticket prom.", value: formatCurrency(kpis.ticketPromedio, { decimals: 0 }), color: "text-secondary" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Productos", value: String(kpis.productos), color: "text-gray-700" },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">[39m
[31m+       {cards.map(c => ([39m
[31m+         <div key={c.label} className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3  text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-semibold">{c.label}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-lg font-extrabold mt-0.5", c.color)}>{c.value}</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ))}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ // ── Main Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export default function FinanzasModule() {[39m
[31m+   const [sub, setSub] = useState(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === "undefined") return TABS[0].id;[39m
[31m+     return (localStorage.getItem(`admin-last-tab-${MODULE_ID}`) as typeof TABS[number]["id"]) || TABS[0].id;[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(`admin-last-tab-${MODULE_ID}`, sub); }, [sub]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Auto-refresh every 5 minutes[39m
[31m+   const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);[39m
[31m+   const autoRefresh = useAutoRefresh({[39m
[31m+     intervalSeconds: 300,[39m
[31m+     onRefresh: useCallback(() => setRefreshKey(k => k + 1), []),[39m
[31m+     enabled: sub === "dashboard",[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       <AdminModuleHeader[39m
[31m+         title="Mi Plata"[39m
[31m+         description="Pérdidas y ganancias, gastos, flujo de caja y reportes financieros"[39m
[31m+         icon={Wallet}[39m
[31m+         bgTint="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/20"[39m
[31m+         iconColorClass="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 flex-wrap">[39m
[31m+         {sub === "dashboard" && ([39m
[31m+           <AutoRefreshControl[39m
[31m+             secondsLeft={autoRefresh.secondsLeft}[39m
[31m+             paused={autoRefresh.paused}[39m
[31m+             isActive={autoRefresh.isActive}[39m
[31m+             onTogglePause={autoRefresh.togglePause}[39m
[31m+             onRefreshNow={autoRefresh.refreshNow}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={generarReporteBancario}[39m
[31m+           className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-white bg-primary hover:bg-primary-dark  transition-colors"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           Reporte Bancario[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <AdminTabBar[39m
[31m+         tabs={TABS}[39m
[31m+         activeTab={sub}[39m
[31m+         onTabChange={(id) => setSub(id as typeof TABS[number]["id"])}[39m
[31m+         moduleId="finanzas"[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+       {sub === "dashboard" && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-6" key={refreshKey}>[39m
[31m+           <FinanzasDashboard />[39m
[31m+           {/* Resumen automático integrado en dashboard */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">[39m
[31m+             <ProfitLossAutoCard />[39m
[31m+             <MonthProjectionCard />[39m
[31m+             <BudgetAlertWidget />[39m
[31m+             <WeeklyReportCard />[39m
[31m+             <MoneyLeakDetector />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "pl" && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+           <PLTab />[39m
[31m+           <ComparativoMensual />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "gastos" && <ExpensesTab />}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "rentabilidad" && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+           <ProfitabilityTab />[39m
[31m+           <BreakEvenDashboard />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "presupuesto" && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+           <BudgetVsRealTab />[39m
[31m+           <PresupuestoMensualTab />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "flujo-caja" && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-6">[39m

[31m+           <CashflowRollingTable />[39m
[31m+           <div className="pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-white/10">[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 mb-3">[39m
[31m+               Proyección legacy (30 días)[39m

[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-5 ">[39m

[31m+ // ── Mejora 8: Category Treemap View ─────────────────────────────────────────[39m

[31m+ interface TreemapProduct {[39m
[31m+   id: number;[39m
[31m+   name: string;[39m
[31m+   category: string;[39m
[31m+   stock: number | null;[39m
[31m+   costPrice: number | null;[39m

[31m+   active: boolean;[39m
