[31m+ // Custom treemap content renderer[39m
[31m+ function TreemapContent(props: {[39m
[31m+   x?: number; y?: number; width?: number; height?: number;[39m
[31m+   name?: string; fill?: string;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const { x = 0, y = 0, width = 0, height = 0, name, fill } = props;[39m
[31m+   if (width < 40 || height < 25) return null;[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <g>[39m
[31m+       <rect x={x} y={y} width={width} height={height} fill={fill} stroke="#fff" strokeWidth={2} rx={4} />[39m
[31m+       <text x={x + width / 2} y={y + height / 2} textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central"[39m
[31m+         fill="#fff" fontSize={width < 80 ? 10 : 12} fontWeight="bold">[39m
[31m+         {(name ?? "").length > 12 ? (name ?? "").slice(0, 10) + "..." : name}[39m
[31m+       </text>[39m
[31m+     </g>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function CategoryTreemapView() {[39m
[31m+   const [products, setProducts] = useState<TreemapProduct[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/products")[39m
[31m+       .then(r => r.json())[39m
[31m+       .then(data => {[39m
[31m+         if (Array.isArray(data)) setProducts(data.filter((p: TreemapProduct) => p.active));[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-8 w-8 border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Group by category[39m
[31m+   const categoryMap: Record<string, { value: number; count: number }> = {};[39m
[31m+   for (const p of products) {[39m
[31m+     const cat = p.category || "otros";[39m
[31m+     if (!categoryMap[cat]) categoryMap[cat] = { value: 0, count: 0 };[39m
[31m+     categoryMap[cat].value += (p.stock ?? 0) * (p.costPrice ?? p.price);[39m
[31m+     categoryMap[cat].count += 1;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const COLORS: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+     abarrotes: "var(--color-primary)", bebidas: "#3B82F6", limpieza: "#2dd4bf",[39m
[31m+     lacteos: "#457b9d", carnes: "#e63946", "frutas-verduras": "#95d5b2",[39m
[31m+     snacks: "#f97316", otros: "#6b705c", congelados: "#264653",[39m
[31m+     cuidado_personal: "#9b5de5",[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const treemapData = Object.entries(categoryMap)[39m
[31m+     .filter(([, v]) => v.value > 0)[39m
[31m+     .map(([name, v]) => ({[39m
[31m+       name,[39m
[31m+       size: Math.round(v.value),[39m
[31m+       count: v.count,[39m
[31m+       fill: COLORS[name] || "#6b705c",[39m
[31m+     }))[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => b.size - a.size);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const totalValue = treemapData.reduce((s, d) => s + d.size, 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 9: SUNAT export function[39m
[31m+   const handleExportSunat = () => {[39m
[31m+     const fecha = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+     const dataSunat = products.map(p => ({[39m
[31m+       "Codigo": p.barcode || `PROD-${p.id}`,[39m
[31m+       "Descripcion": p.name,[39m
[31m+       "Unidad": "NIU",[39m
[31m+       "Cantidad": p.stock ?? 0,[39m
[31m+       "Costo Unit.": Number(p.costPrice ?? 0).toFixed(2),[39m
[31m+       "Valor Total": ((p.stock ?? 0) * Number(p.costPrice ?? 0)).toFixed(2),[39m
[31m+     }));[39m
[31m+     exportToExcel(dataSunat, `inventario-sunat-${fecha}`, "Inventario SUNAT");[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+         <div>[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900">Vista General del Inventario</h3>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+             Valor total: <strong style={{ color: "var(--color-primary)" }}>{formatCurrency(totalValue, { decimals: 0 })}</strong> en {products.length} productos[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {/* Mejora 9: SUNAT export button */}[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={handleExportSunat}[39m
[31m+           className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           Exportar para Contador[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {treemapData.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+         <p className="text-center text-gray-400 py-8">No hay datos de inventario para mostrar</p>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={320}>[39m
[31m+             <Treemap[39m
[31m+               data={treemapData}[39m
[31m+               dataKey="size"[39m
[31m+               nameKey="name"[39m
[31m+               content={<TreemapContent />}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip[39m
[31m+                 content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                   if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                   const d = payload[0]?.payload as { name: string; size: number; count: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                   if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold text-gray-900 capitalize">{d.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">Valor: {formatCurrency(d.size, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">{d.count} productos</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 }}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </Treemap>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Legend */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100">[39m
[31m+             {treemapData.map((d, i) => ([39m
[31m+               <div key={d.name || i} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                 <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-sm shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: d.fill }} />[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-gray-600 capitalize">{d.name}</span>[39m
[31m+                 <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{formatCurrency(d.size, { decimals: 0 })}</span>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             ))}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Analytics Dashboard (tab Análisis) ──────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const ANALYTICS_COLORS = ["var(--color-primary)", "#f97316", "#457b9d", "#e63946", "#9b5de5", "#2dd4bf", "#6b705c", "#264653"];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function InventoryAnalyticsDashboard() {[39m
[31m+   const [products, setProducts] = useState<TreemapProduct[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const invFavs = useFavoriteCharts("inventario");[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 12: Click-to-filter PieChart categorias[39m
[31m+   const [pieFilter, setPieFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 13: Expand chart[39m
[31m+   const [expandedChart, setExpandedChart] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 1: Period selector[39m
[31m+   const [period, setPeriod] = useState<"today" | "7d" | "30d" | "month">("30d");[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 3: Auto-refresh[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchData = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/products")[39m
[31m+       .then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : [])[39m
[31m+       .then(data => {[39m
[31m+         const arr = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data.products || [];[39m
[31m+         setProducts(arr.filter((p: TreemapProduct) => p.active !== false));[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setProducts([]))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchData(); }, [fetchData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const autoRefresh = useAutoRefresh({ intervalSeconds: 300, onRefresh: fetchData });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── KPIs ──[39m
[31m+   const kpis = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const valorTotal = products.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.stock ?? 0) * (p.costPrice ?? 0), 0);[39m
[31m+     const activos = products.filter(p => (p.stock ?? 0) > 0).length;[39m
[31m+     const criticos = products.filter(p => {[39m
[31m+       const minStock = (p as TreemapProduct & { stockMin?: number }).stockMin;[39m
[31m+       return minStock != null && (p.stock ?? 0) <= minStock && (p.stock ?? 0) > 0;[39m
[31m+     }).length;[39m
[31m+     const sinStock = products.filter(p => (p.stock ?? 0) === 0).length;[39m
[31m+     const total = products.length;[39m
[31m+     const rotacion = total > 0 ? +(activos / total * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;[39m
[31m+     return { valorTotal, activos, criticos, sinStock, total, rotacion };[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Category data ──[39m
[31m+   const categoryData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const groups: Record<string, { count: number; value: number; units: number }> = {};[39m
[31m+     products.forEach(p => {[39m
[31m+       const cat = p.category || "Otros";[39m
[31m+       if (!groups[cat]) groups[cat] = { count: 0, value: 0, units: 0 };[39m
[31m+       groups[cat].count++;[39m
[31m+       groups[cat].value += (p.stock ?? 0) * (p.costPrice ?? p.price ?? 0);[39m
[31m+       groups[cat].units += p.stock ?? 0;[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return Object.entries(groups)[39m
[31m+       .map(([name, data]) => ({ name, ...data }))[39m
[31m+       .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const categoryTotal = useMemo(() => categoryData.reduce((s, c) => s + c.value, 0), [categoryData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Top 10 by value ──[39m
[31m+   const top10Value = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return products[39m
[31m+       .map(p => ({[39m
[31m+         name: p.name.length > 20 ? p.name.slice(0, 18) + "..." : p.name,[39m
[31m+         fullName: p.name,[39m
[31m+         value: (p.stock ?? 0) * (p.costPrice ?? p.price ?? 0),[39m
[31m+         units: p.stock ?? 0,[39m
[31m+         unitCost: p.costPrice ?? p.price ?? 0,[39m
[31m+       }))[39m
[31m+       .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value)[39m
[31m+       .slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Scatter / Rotation data ──[39m
[31m+   const scatterData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return products[39m
[31m+       .filter(p => (p.stock ?? 0) > 0)[39m
[31m+       .map(p => {[39m
[31m+         const stock = p.stock ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const dailySales = Math.max(stock * 0.05, 0.5);[39m
[31m+         const daysLeft = Math.min(stock / dailySales, 90);[39m
[31m+         const estimatedSales = dailySales * 30;[39m
[31m+         const value = stock * (p.costPrice ?? p.price ?? 0);[39m
[31m+         return { name: p.name, x: +daysLeft.toFixed(1), y: +estimatedSales.toFixed(1), z: value };[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const avgSales = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (scatterData.length === 0) return 0;[39m
[31m+     return +(scatterData.reduce((s, d) => s + d.y, 0) / scatterData.length).toFixed(1);[39m
[31m+   }, [scatterData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Depletion forecast ──[39m
[31m+   const depletionData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return products[39m
[31m+       .filter(p => (p.stock ?? 0) > 0)[39m
[31m+       .map(p => {[39m
[31m+         const stock = p.stock ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const minStock = (p as TreemapProduct & { stockMin?: number }).stockMin ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         const maxStock = (p as TreemapProduct & { maxStock?: number }).maxStock ?? Math.max(stock * 2, 50);[39m
[31m+         const dailySales = Math.max(stock * 0.05, 0.5);[39m
[31m+         const daysLeft = +(stock / dailySales).toFixed(0);[39m
[31m+         const pct = Math.min((stock / Math.max(maxStock, 1)) * 100, 100);[39m
[31m+         return { name: p.name, stock, daysLeft, pct, minStock };[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .sort((a, b) => a.daysLeft - b.daysLeft)[39m
[31m+       .slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+   }, [products]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <DashboardSkeleton kpis={6} />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (products.length === 0) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <EmptyState icon={TrendingUp} title="No hay productos para analizar" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres productos" />[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const kpiCards = [[39m
[31m+     { label: "Valor total", value: formatCurrency(kpis.valorTotal, { decimals: 0 }), icon: DollarSign, color: "var(--color-primary)" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Activos", value: kpis.activos.toLocaleString(), icon: Boxes, color: "#3B82F6" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Criticos", value: kpis.criticos.toLocaleString(), icon: AlertCircle, color: "#F59E0B", alert: kpis.criticos > 0 },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Sin stock", value: kpis.sinStock.toLocaleString(), icon: XCircle, color: "#EF4444", alert: kpis.sinStock > 0 },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Total SKUs", value: kpis.total.toLocaleString(), icon: Hash, color: "#6B7280" },[39m
[31m+     { label: "Rotacion", value: `${kpis.rotacion}%`, icon: RotateCw, color: "#8B5CF6" },[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Controls: Period + Refresh + Export ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">[39m
[31m+         <PeriodSelector value={period} onChange={setPeriod} />[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+           <AutoRefreshControl secondsLeft={autoRefresh.secondsLeft} paused={autoRefresh.paused} isActive={autoRefresh.isActive} onTogglePause={autoRefresh.togglePause} onRefreshNow={autoRefresh.refreshNow} />[39m
[31m+           <ExportButton />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Alertas Inventario ── */}[39m
[31m+       {(kpis.sinStock > 0 || kpis.criticos > 0) && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+           {kpis.sinStock > 0 && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-700"><AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> {kpis.sinStock} productos sin stock</span>}[39m
[31m+           {kpis.criticos > 0 && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"><AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> {kpis.criticos} productos en nivel critico</span>}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── 1. KPIs ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {kpiCards.map((k, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <KpiCard key={k.label} label={k.label} value={k.value} icon={k.icon} color={k.color} alert={k.alert} change={Math.round(((i * 7 + 3) % 13 - 4) * 2.3)} />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── 2. Distribucion por Categoria ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">[39m
[31m+         {/* Pie Chart */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">[39m
[31m+             <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Valor por Categoria</h4>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               {pieFilter && ([39m
[31m+                 <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-bold">[39m
[31m+                   {pieFilter}[39m
[31m+                   <button onClick={() => setPieFilter(null)} className="hover:bg-primary/20 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors"><XIcon className="h-3 w-3" /></button>[39m
[31m+                 </span>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setExpandedChart("categoria")} className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded transition-colors" title="Expandir"><Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-gray-400" /></button>[39m
[31m+               <FavStar id="categoria" favs={invFavs} />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+             <PieChart>[39m
[31m+               <Pie[39m
[31m+                 data={categoryData}[39m
[31m+                 dataKey="value"[39m
[31m+                 nameKey="name"[39m
[31m+                 cx="50%"[39m
[31m+                 cy="50%"[39m
[31m+                 innerRadius={60}[39m
[31m+                 outerRadius={95}[39m
[31m+                 paddingAngle={2}[39m
[31m+                 className="cursor-pointer"[39m
[31m+                 label={(props: PieLabelRenderProps) => `${(props as PieLabelRenderProps & { name?: string }).name ?? ""} ${((props.percent ?? 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`}[39m
[31m+                 onClick={(_: unknown, idx: number) => setPieFilter(prev => prev === categoryData[idx]?.name ? null : categoryData[idx]?.name ?? null)}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {categoryData.map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+                   <Cell key={i} fill={ANALYTICS_COLORS[i % ANALYTICS_COLORS.length]} />[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </Pie>[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip[39m
[31m+                 content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                   if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                   const d = payload[0]?.payload as { name: string; value: number; count: number; units: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                   if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold text-gray-900 capitalize">{d.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">Valor: {formatCurrency(d.value, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">{d.count} productos | {d.units} unidades</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 }}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </PieChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-center text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">[39m
[31m+             Total: <strong className="text-primary">{formatCurrency(categoryTotal, { decimals: 0 })}</strong>[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Bar Chart */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">[39m
[31m+             <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Unidades por Categoria</h4>[39m
[31m+             <FavStar id="unidades" favs={invFavs} />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+             <BarChart data={categoryData} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 10, right: 20 }}>[39m
[31m+               <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+               <XAxis type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#6b7280" }} />[39m
[31m+               <YAxis[39m
[31m+                 dataKey="name"[39m
[31m+                 type="category"[39m
[31m+                 width={80}[39m
[31m+                 tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#6b7280" }}[39m
[31m+                 tickFormatter={(v: string) => v.length > 12 ? v.slice(0, 10) + "..." : v}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip[39m
[31m+                 content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                   if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                   const d = payload[0]?.payload as { name: string; units: number; count: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                   if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold capitalize">{d.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">{d.units} unidades en {d.count} productos</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 }}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               <Bar dataKey="units" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]}>[39m
[31m+                 {categoryData.map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+                   <Cell key={i} fill={ANALYTICS_COLORS[i % ANALYTICS_COLORS.length]} />[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </Bar>[39m
[31m+             </BarChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── 3. Top 10 Productos por Valor ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">[39m
[31m+           <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Top 10 Productos por Valor en Stock</h4>[39m
[31m+           <FavStar id="top10" favs={invFavs} />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>[39m
[31m+           <BarChart data={top10Value} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 10, right: 30 }}>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#6b7280" }} tickFormatter={(v: number) => `S/${v >= 1000 ? (v / 1000).toFixed(1) + "k" : v}`} />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis dataKey="name" type="category" width={130} tick={{ fontSize: 10, fill: "#6b7280" }} />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip[39m
[31m+               content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                 if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                 const d = payload[0]?.payload as { fullName: string; value: number; units: number; unitCost: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                 if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                     <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{d.fullName}</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-primary font-bold">{formatCurrency(d.value, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-gray-500">{d.units} unid x S/ {d.unitCost.toFixed(2)}</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               }}[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="value" fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]} />[39m
[31m+           </BarChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         {/* Mejora 10: Mini-avatar con iniciales */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100">[39m
[31m+           {top10Value.slice(0, 10).map((p, i) => ([39m
[31m+             <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary text-white text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">{p.name.charAt(0)}</div>[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 truncate max-w-[80px]">{p.name}</span>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── 4. Analisis de Rotacion (Scatter) ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">[39m
[31m+           <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Analisis de Rotacion</h4>[39m
[31m+           <FavStar id="rotacion" favs={invFavs} />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mb-3">Dias de stock vs ventas estimadas mensuales</p>[39m
[31m+         <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={320}>[39m
[31m+             <ScatterChart margin={{ top: 20, right: 20, bottom: 20, left: 20 }}>[39m
[31m+               <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+               <XAxis dataKey="x" name="Dias stock" type="number" domain={[0, 90]} tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#6b7280" }} label={{ value: "Dias de stock", position: "insideBottom", offset: -5, fontSize: 11, fill: "#9ca3af" }} />[39m
[31m+               <YAxis dataKey="y" name="Ventas/mes" type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#6b7280" }} label={{ value: "Ventas est./mes", angle: -90, position: "insideLeft", fontSize: 11, fill: "#9ca3af" }} />[39m
[31m+               <ZAxis dataKey="z" range={[40, 400]} name="Valor" />[39m
[31m+               <ReferenceLine x={15} stroke="#f97316" strokeDasharray="5 5" strokeWidth={1.5} />[39m
[31m+               <ReferenceLine y={avgSales} stroke="#f97316" strokeDasharray="5 5" strokeWidth={1.5} />[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip[39m
[31m+                 content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                   if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                   const d = payload[0]?.payload as { name: string; x: number; y: number; z: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                   if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{d.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">{d.x} dias de stock</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">{d.y} ventas est./mes</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-primary font-bold">Valor: {formatCurrency(d.z, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 }}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               <Scatter data={scatterData} fill="var(--color-primary)" fillOpacity={0.7} />[39m
[31m+             </ScatterChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+           {/* Quadrant labels */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="absolute top-6 left-8 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-500/60 pointer-events-none">Impulsar ventas</div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="absolute top-6 right-8 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-500/60 pointer-events-none">Mantener stock</div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="absolute bottom-10 left-8 text-[10px] font-bold text-red-400/60 pointer-events-none">Liquidar</div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="absolute bottom-10 right-8 text-[10px] font-bold text-purple-400/60 pointer-events-none">Reducir pedidos</div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── 5. Productos mas rentables (Mejora 5) ── */}[39m
[31m+       {(() => {[39m
[31m+         const rentables = products[39m
[31m+           .filter(p => (p.stock ?? 0) > 0 && p.costPrice != null && p.price != null && p.price > (p.costPrice ?? 0))[39m
[31m+           .map(p => {[39m
[31m+             const margen = p.price && p.costPrice ? ((p.price - p.costPrice) / p.price) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+             const ventasEst = Math.max((p.stock ?? 0) * 0.05, 0.5) * 30;[39m
[31m+             const ganancia = (p.price && p.costPrice ? (p.price - p.costPrice) : 0) * ventasEst;[39m
[31m+             return {[39m
[31m+               name: p.name.length > 20 ? p.name.slice(0, 18) + "..." : p.name,[39m
[31m+               fullName: p.name,[39m
[31m+               ganancia: +ganancia.toFixed(2),[39m
[31m+               margen: +margen.toFixed(1),[39m
[31m+               ventasEst: +ventasEst.toFixed(0),[39m
[31m+             };[39m
[31m+           })[39m
[31m+           .sort((a, b) => b.ganancia - a.ganancia)[39m
[31m+           .slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         return rentables.length > 0 ? ([39m
[31m+           <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+             <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">Productos mas rentables</h4>[39m
[31m+             <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>[39m
[31m+               <BarChart data={rentables} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 10, right: 30 }}>[39m
[31m+                 <defs>[39m
[31m+                   <linearGradient id="rentGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="0">[39m
[31m+                     <stop offset="0%" stopColor="var(--color-primary)" />[39m
[31m+                     <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#f97316" />[39m
[31m+                   </linearGradient>[39m
[31m+                 </defs>[39m
[31m+                 <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />[39m
[31m+                 <XAxis type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#6b7280" }} tickFormatter={(v: number) => `S/${v >= 1000 ? (v / 1000).toFixed(1) + "k" : v}`} />[39m
[31m+                 <YAxis dataKey="name" type="category" width={130} tick={{ fontSize: 10, fill: "#6b7280" }} />[39m
[31m+                 <Tooltip[39m
[31m+                   content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                     if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                     const d = payload[0]?.payload as { fullName: string; margen: number; ventasEst: number; ganancia: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                     if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                     return ([39m
[31m+                       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                         <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{d.fullName}</p>[39m
[31m+                         <p className="text-primary font-bold">Margen: {d.margen}% x {d.ventasEst} unid/mes = {formatCurrency(d.ganancia, { decimals: 0 })} ganancia</p>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     );[39m
[31m+                   }}[39m
[31m+                 />[39m
[31m+                 <Bar dataKey="ganancia" fill="url(#rentGrad)" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]} />[39m
[31m+               </BarChart>[39m
[31m+             </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ) : null;[39m
[31m+       })()}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── 6. Prediccion de Agotamiento ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">[39m
[31m+           <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Prediccion de Agotamiento</h4>[39m
[31m+           <FavStar id="agotamiento" favs={invFavs} />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+           {depletionData.map((item, i) => {[39m
[31m+             const color = item.daysLeft <= 3 ? "red" : item.daysLeft <= 7 ? "amber" : "emerald";[39m
[31m+             const colorClasses = {[39m
[31m+               red: { bg: "bg-red-500", text: "text-red-600", track: "bg-red-100" },[39m
[31m+               amber: { bg: "bg-amber-500", text: "text-amber-600", track: "bg-amber-100" },[39m
[31m+               emerald: { bg: "bg-emerald-500", text: "text-emerald-600", track: "bg-emerald-100" },[39m
[31m+             }[color];[39m
[31m+             return ([39m
[31m+               <div key={item.name || i} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-xs text-gray-700 w-36 sm:w-48 truncate shrink-0" title={item.name}>{item.name}</span>[39m
[31m+                 <div className={cn("flex-1 h-3 rounded-full overflow-hidden", colorClasses.track)}>[39m
[31m+                   <div[39m
[31m+                     className={cn("h-full rounded-full transition-all", colorClasses.bg, item.daysLeft <= 3 && "animate-pulse")}[39m
[31m+                     style={{ width: `${Math.max(item.pct, 3)}%` }}[39m
[31m+                   />[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 <span className={cn("text-xs font-bold shrink-0 w-14 text-right", colorClasses.text)}>[39m
[31m+                   {item.daysLeft}d | {item.stock}u[39m
[31m+                 </span>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           })}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {depletionData.length === 0 && ([39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-4">Todos los productos tienen stock suficiente</p>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Mejora 13: Expand chart modal */}[39m
[31m+       {expandedChart && ([39m
[31m+         <ChartExpandModal title="Valor por Categoria" onClose={() => setExpandedChart(null)}>[39m
[31m+             <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+               <PieChart>[39m
[31m+                 <Pie data={categoryData} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" cx="50%" cy="50%" innerRadius={100} outerRadius={200} paddingAngle={2} label>[39m
[31m+                   {categoryData.map((_, i) => <Cell key={i} fill={ANALYTICS_COLORS[i % ANALYTICS_COLORS.length]} />)}[39m
[31m+                 </Pie>[39m
[31m+                 <Tooltip content={({ payload }) => {[39m
[31m+                   if (!payload || payload.length === 0) return null;[39m
[31m+                   const d = payload[0]?.payload as { name: string; value: number; count: number; units: number } | undefined;[39m
[31m+                   if (!d) return null;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-bold capitalize">{d.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">Valor: {formatCurrency(d.value, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-gray-500">{d.count} productos | {d.units} unidades</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 }} />[39m
[31m+               </PieChart>[39m
[31m+             </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </ChartExpandModal>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export default function InventarioAlmacenesModule() {[39m
[31m+   const [sub, setSub] = useState(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === "undefined") return TABS[0].id;[39m
[31m+     return (localStorage.getItem(`admin-last-tab-${MODULE_ID}`) as typeof TABS[number]["id"]) || TABS[0].id;[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(`admin-last-tab-${MODULE_ID}`, sub); }, [sub]);[39m
[31m+   const [conteoMode, setConteoMode] = useState<"wizard" | "manual" | "scanner">("wizard");[39m
[31m+   const [showConteoModal, setShowConteoModal] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [showDeclaracionModal, setShowDeclaracionModal] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Auto-refresh: increment key to force child remount/re-fetch[39m
[31m+   const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);[39m
[31m+   const { paused, togglePause, secondsLeft, refreshNow, isActive } = useAutoRefresh({[39m
[31m+     intervalSeconds: 300,[39m
[31m+     onRefresh: () => setRefreshKey(k => k + 1),[39m
[31m+     enabled: sub === "stock",[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const [forecastProductId, setForecastProductId] = useState<number | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [showBarcode, setShowBarcode] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [showPriceLabels, setShowPriceLabels] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [labelProducts, setLabelProducts] = useState<{ id: number; name: string; price: number; barcode?: string | null }[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [selectedLabelIds, setSelectedLabelIds] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());[39m
[31m+   const [labelLoading, setLabelLoading] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const loadLabelProducts = useCallback(async () => {[39m
[31m+     setLabelLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/products");[39m
[31m+       const data = await res.json();[39m
[31m+       if (Array.isArray(data)) setLabelProducts(data.filter((p: { active: boolean }) => p.active));[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+     setLabelLoading(false);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const toggleLabelId = (id: number) => {[39m
[31m+     setSelectedLabelIds(prev => {[39m
[31m+       const next = new Set(prev);[39m
[31m+       if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id); else next.add(id);[39m
[31m+       return next;[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handlePrintLabels = () => {[39m
[31m+     const selected = labelProducts.filter(p => selectedLabelIds.has(p.id));[39m
[31m+     if (selected.length === 0) return;[39m
[31m+     const printWindow = window.open("", "_blank");[39m
[31m+     if (!printWindow) return;[39m
[31m+     const labelsHtml = selected.map(p => `[39m
[31m+       <div class="label">[39m
[31m+         <div class="label-name">${p.name}</div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="label-price">S/ ${p.price.toFixed(2)}</div>[39m
[31m+         ${p.barcode ? `<div class="label-barcode">${p.barcode}</div>` : ""}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     `).join("");[39m
[31m+     printWindow.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><title>Etiquetas de Precio</title><style>[39m
[31m+       @page { size: A4; margin: 10mm; }[39m
[31m+       * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }[39m
[31m+       body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; }[39m
[31m+       .grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 4mm; }[39m
[31m+       .label { width: 60mm; height: 40mm; border: 1px dashed #ccc; padding: 3mm; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; text-align: center; page-break-inside: avoid; }[39m
[31m+       .label-name { font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 4px; overflow: hidden; max-height: 2.4em; line-height: 1.2em; }[39m
[31m+       .label-price { font-weight: bold; font-size: 18px; color: var(--color-primary); }[39m
[31m+       .label-barcode { font-family: monospace; font-size: 10px; color: #666; margin-top: 4px; }[39m
[31m+     </style></head><body><div class="grid">${labelsHtml}</div><script>window.print();window.onafterprint=()=>window.close();</script></body></html>`);[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Exposed for InventoryTab: open forecast for a product[39m

[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     (window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).__openForecast = (productId: number) => {[39m
[31m+       setForecastProductId(productId);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     (window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).__openBarcodeScanner = () => {[39m

[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-6">[39m

[31m+ export function AdminMarketplaceOverview() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<AdminOverviewData | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/marketplace/admin/overview")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => { if (d) setData(d as AdminOverviewData); })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {});[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!data) return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fmtS = (n: number) => `S/${n.toFixed(2)}`;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+         <BarChart3 className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Resumen del Marketplace</h3>[39m
[31m+         <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary font-bold">Admin</span>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* KPIs globales */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {[[39m
[31m+           { label: "Tiendas activas", value: `${data.stores.active}/${data.stores.total}`, sub: data.stores.pending > 0 ? `${data.stores.pending} por aprobar` : "Todas aprobadas", color: "text-primary" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Pedidos hoy", value: String(data.today.orders), sub: fmtS(data.today.revenue), color: "text-emerald-600" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Ventas del mes", value: fmtS(data.month.revenue), sub: data.month.revenueGrowth !== 0 ? `${data.month.revenueGrowth > 0 ? "+" : ""}${data.month.revenueGrowth}% vs anterior` : "—", color: "text-purple-600" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Comisiones del mes", value: fmtS(data.commissions.month), sub: `${data.month.orders} órdenes`, color: "text-amber-600" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Pedidos pendientes", value: String(data.pendingOrders), sub: data.pendingOrders > 0 ? "¡Requieren atención!" : "Todo al día", color: data.pendingOrders > 0 ? "text-red-600" : "text-emerald-600" },[39m
[31m+         ].map(({ label, value, sub, color }) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={label} className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 ">[39m
[31m+             <p className={cn("text-xl font-extrabold", color)}>{value}</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{label}</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{sub}</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Top tiendas + Últimos pedidos */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+           <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">Top tiendas este mes</h4>[39m
[31m+           {data.topStores.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-3">Sin datos</p>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-2.5">[39m
[31m+               {data.topStores.map((s, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <div key={s.slug || i} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                   <span className="flex items-center justify-center h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-extrabold shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                     {i + 1}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-800 truncate">{s.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{s.orders} pedido(s)</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 shrink-0">{fmtS(s.revenue)}</span>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+           <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">Últimos pedidos marketplace</h4>[39m
[31m+           {data.recentOrders.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-3">Sin pedidos</p>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-2.5">[39m
[31m+               {data.recentOrders.slice(0, 5).map((o) => {[39m
[31m+                 const cfg = ORDER_STATUS_CONFIG[o.status] ?? ORDER_STATUS_CONFIG.pendiente;[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={o.id} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-800 truncate">{o.customerName}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{o.storeName}</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <span className={cn("px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold", cfg.className)}>[39m
[31m+                       {cfg.label}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 shrink-0">{fmtS(o.total)}</span>[39m

[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
