[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Dashboard (Vendor)[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ interface AnalyticsData {[39m
[31m+   store: { name: string; slug: string; rating: number; reviewCount: number };[39m
[31m+   today: { orders: number; revenue: number };[39m
[31m+   month: { orders: number; revenue: number; avgTicket: number; revenueGrowth: number };[39m
[31m+   week: { orders: number; revenue: number };[39m
[31m+   products: { published: number; total: number; lowStock: number };[39m
[31m+   pendingOrders: number;[39m
[31m+   pendingReviews: number;[39m
[31m+   topProducts: { name: string; qty: number; revenue: number }[];[39m
[31m+   recentOrders: { id: string; customerName: string; total: number; status: string; createdAt: string; itemsCount: number }[];[39m
[31m+   dailySales: { date: string; revenue: number; orders: number }[];[39m
[31m+   allChannels?: { today: { orders: number; revenue: number }; month: { orders: number; revenue: number } };[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function DashboardTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<AnalyticsData | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [confirmingId, setConfirmingId] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ void DashboardTab;[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/marketplace/analytics")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => { if (d) setData(d as AnalyticsData); })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleQuickConfirm = useCallback(async (orderId: string) => {[39m
[31m+     setConfirmingId(orderId);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/marketplace/orders/${orderId}`, {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ status: "confirmado" }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok && data) {[39m
[31m+         setData({[39m
[31m+           ...data,[39m
[31m+           pendingOrders: Math.max(0, data.pendingOrders - 1),[39m
[31m+           recentOrders: data.recentOrders.map((o) =>[39m
[31m+             o.id === orderId ? { ...o, status: "confirmado" } : o[39m
[31m+           ),[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* silent */ }[39m
[31m+     setConfirmingId(null);[39m
[31m+   }, [data]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+   if (!data) return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+       <BarChart3 className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-sm">No hay datos del marketplace todavía.</p>[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-xs mt-1">Configura tu tienda primero en la pestaña &ldquo;Mi Tienda Personal&rdquo;.</p>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fmtS = (n: number) => `S/${n.toFixed(2)}`;[39m
[31m+   const maxRevenue = Math.max(...data.dailySales.map((d) => d.revenue), 1);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-5">[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Admin overview (solo visible para admins de la plataforma) ── */}[39m
[31m+       <AdminMarketplaceOverview />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── KPIs rápidos (Marketplace) ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {[[39m
[31m+           { label: "Hoy", value: fmtS(data.today.revenue), sub: `${data.today.orders} pedido(s)`, color: "text-primary" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Este mes", value: fmtS(data.month.revenue), sub: `${data.month.orders} pedido(s)`, color: "text-emerald-600" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Ticket promedio", value: fmtS(data.month.avgTicket), sub: data.month.revenueGrowth !== 0 ? `${data.month.revenueGrowth > 0 ? "+" : ""}${data.month.revenueGrowth}% vs mes anterior` : "Sin comparación", color: "text-purple-600" },[39m
[31m+           { label: "Reseñas", value: `★ ${data.store.rating.toFixed(1)}`, sub: `${data.store.reviewCount} opiniones`, color: "text-amber-500" },[39m
[31m+         ].map(({ label, value, sub, color }) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={label} className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 sm:p-4 ">[39m
[31m+             <p className={cn("text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold", color)}>{value}</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 leading-tight">{label}</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{sub}</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Resumen todos los canales (Marketplace + Directa + POS) ── */}[39m
[31m+       {data.allChannels && (data.allChannels.today.orders > data.today.orders || data.allChannels.month.orders > data.month.orders) && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-linear-to-r from-primary/5 to-emerald-50 border border-primary/20 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">[39m
[31m+             <Store className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+             Resumen total (todos los canales)[39m
[31m+           </h3>[39m
[31m+           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-primary">{fmtS(data.allChannels.today.revenue)}</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">Hoy (total)</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-emerald-600">{data.allChannels.today.orders}</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">Pedidos hoy (total)</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-purple-600">{fmtS(data.allChannels.month.revenue)}</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">Este mes (total)</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-amber-600">{data.allChannels.month.orders}</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">Pedidos mes (total)</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 mt-2">Incluye ventas directas, POS y marketplace.</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Alertas rápidas ── */}[39m
[31m+       {(data.products.lowStock > 0 || data.pendingReviews > 0) && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+           {data.products.lowStock > 0 && ([39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 text-xs font-semibold">[39m
[31m+               <AlertCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+               {data.products.lowStock} producto(s) con stock bajo[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+           {data.pendingReviews > 0 && ([39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 text-xs font-semibold">[39m
[31m+               <MessageSquare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+               {data.pendingReviews} reseña(s) por moderar[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Acciones rápidas del vendedor ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">[39m
[31m+           <Zap className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800">Qué hacer ahora</h3>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+           {/* Pedidos pendientes */}[39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => {[39m
[31m+               const tabBar = document.querySelector('[data-module-id="marketplace"]');[39m
[31m+               if (tabBar) {[39m
[31m+                 const ordenesBtn = tabBar.querySelector('button[data-tab-id="ordenes"]') as HTMLButtonElement;[39m
[31m+                 if (ordenesBtn) ordenesBtn.click();[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+             className={cn([39m
[31m+               "flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all hover:shadow-sm",[39m
[31m+               (data.pendingOrders ?? 0) > 0[39m
[31m+                 ? "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100"[39m
[31m+                 : "border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-100"[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className={cn([39m
[31m+               "h-10 w-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0",[39m
[31m+               (data.pendingOrders ?? 0) > 0 ? "bg-amber-200 text-amber-700" : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-500"[39m
[31m+             )}>[39m
[31m+               <ShoppingCart className="h-5 w-5" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="min-w-0">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                 {(data.pendingOrders ?? 0) > 0[39m
[31m+                   ? `${data.pendingOrders} pedido(s) por confirmar`[39m
[31m+                   : "Sin pedidos pendientes"}[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                 Ir a órdenes <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Stock bajo */}[39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => {[39m
[31m+               const tabBar = document.querySelector('[data-module-id="marketplace"]');[39m
[31m+               if (tabBar) {[39m
[31m+                 const prodBtn = tabBar.querySelector('button[data-tab-id="productos"]') as HTMLButtonElement;[39m
[31m+                 if (prodBtn) prodBtn.click();[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+             className={cn([39m
[31m+               "flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all hover:shadow-sm",[39m
[31m+               data.products.lowStock > 0[39m
[31m+                 ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100"[39m
[31m+                 : "border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-100"[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className={cn([39m
[31m+               "h-10 w-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0",[39m
[31m+               data.products.lowStock > 0 ? "bg-red-200 text-red-700" : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-500"[39m
[31m+             )}>[39m
[31m+               <Package className="h-5 w-5" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="min-w-0">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                 {data.products.lowStock > 0[39m
[31m+                   ? `${data.products.lowStock} producto(s) stock bajo`[39m
[31m+                   : "Stock OK"}[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                 Ver productos <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Ver mi tienda */}[39m
[31m+           <a[39m
[31m+             href={data.store.slug ? `/marketplace/${data.store.slug}` : "/marketplace"}[39m
[31m+             target="_blank"[39m
[31m+             rel="noopener noreferrer"[39m
[31m+             className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border border-primary/20 bg-primary/5 text-left transition-all hover:shadow-sm hover:bg-primary/10"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 bg-primary/20 text-primary">[39m
[31m+               <ExternalLink className="h-5 w-5" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="min-w-0">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Ver mi tienda</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                 Abrir en marketplace <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </a>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Ir al Marketplace */}[39m
[31m+           <a[39m
[31m+             href="/marketplace"[39m
[31m+             target="_blank"[39m
[31m+             rel="noopener noreferrer"[39m
[31m+             className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 bg-purple-50 text-left transition-all hover:shadow-sm hover:bg-purple-100"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 bg-purple-200 text-purple-600">[39m
[31m+               <Store className="h-5 w-5" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="min-w-0">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Ir al Marketplace</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                 Ver todas las tiendas <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </a>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Gráfico de ventas 7 días (barras simples CSS) ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Ventas últimos 7 días</h3>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-end gap-1.5 h-32">[39m
[31m+           {data.dailySales.map((day) => {[39m
[31m+             const pct = maxRevenue > 0 ? (day.revenue / maxRevenue) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+             const dayLabel = new Date(day.date + "T12:00:00").toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { weekday: "short" });[39m
[31m+             return ([39m
[31m+               <div key={day.date} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+                 <div className="w-full relative" style={{ height: "96px" }}>[39m
[31m+                   <div[39m
[31m+                     className="absolute bottom-0 w-full rounded-t-lg bg-primary transition-all duration-500"[39m
[31m+                     style={{ height: `${Math.max(pct, 4)}%` }}[39m
[31m+                     title={`${fmtS(day.revenue)} — ${day.orders} pedido(s)`}[39m
[31m+                   />[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 capitalize">{dayLabel}</span>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           })}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">[39m
[31m+         {/* ── Top productos ── */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Top 5 productos del mes</h3>[39m
[31m+           {data.topProducts.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 py-4 text-center">Sin ventas este mes</p>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-2.5">[39m
[31m+               {data.topProducts.map((p, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <div key={p.name} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                   <span className="flex items-center justify-center h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-extrabold shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                     {i + 1}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-800 truncate">{p.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{p.qty} vendido(s)</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 shrink-0">{fmtS(p.revenue)}</span>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* ── Últimos pedidos ── */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 ">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Últimos pedidos</h3>[39m
[31m+           {data.recentOrders.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 py-4 text-center">Sin pedidos aún</p>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-2.5">[39m
[31m+               {data.recentOrders.map((o) => {[39m
[31m+                 const cfg = ORDER_STATUS_CONFIG[o.status] ?? ORDER_STATUS_CONFIG.pendiente;[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={o.id} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-800 truncate">{o.customerName}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+                         {o.itemsCount} producto(s) · {new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}[39m
[31m+                       </p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     {o.status === "pendiente" ? ([39m
[31m+                       <button[39m
[31m+                         onClick={() => handleQuickConfirm(o.id)}[39m
[31m+                         disabled={confirmingId === o.id}[39m
[31m+                         className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-[10px] font-bold hover:bg-[#009B8D] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 shrink-0"[39m
[31m+                       >[39m
[31m+                         <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                         {confirmingId === o.id ? "..." : "Confirmar"}[39m
[31m+                       </button>[39m
[31m+                     ) : ([39m
[31m+                       <span className={cn("px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold", cfg.className)}>[39m
[31m+                         {cfg.label}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     )}[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 shrink-0">{fmtS(o.total)}</span>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               })}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Inventario rápido ── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center ">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xl font-extrabold text-primary">{data.products.published}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Publicados</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center ">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-600">{data.products.total}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Total productos</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center ">[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-xl font-extrabold", data.products.lowStock > 0 ? "text-amber-600" : "text-emerald-600")}>[39m
[31m+             {data.products.lowStock}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Stock bajo</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Mi Tienda Personal[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function TiendaTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [store, setStore] = useState<StoreData>({[39m
[31m+     slug: "", name: "", description: "", logoUrl: "",[39m
[31m+     category: "Abarrotes", zone: "Centro", commissionRate: 5, isActive: false,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading]   = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [saving, setSaving]     = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError]       = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [saved, setSaved]       = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/marketplace/stores?my=true")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => {[39m
[31m+         if (d && (d.slug || d.name)) setStore(d as StoreData);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setError("Error al cargar datos de la tienda."))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSave = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!store.name?.trim()) {[39m
[31m+       setError("El nombre de la tienda es obligatorio.");[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setSaving(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/marketplace/stores", {[39m
[31m+         method: store.id ? "PUT" : "POST",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify(store),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));[39m
[31m+         throw new Error(errData.error || "Error al guardar");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       const data = await res.json();[39m
[31m+       setStore(data);[39m
[31m+       setSaved(true);[39m
[31m+       setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2500);[39m
[31m+     } catch (err) {[39m
[31m+       setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error al guardar la tienda. Intenta nuevamente.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setSaving(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <Spinner />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {error && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+           <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {error}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-5  space-y-5">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm">Configuración de la tienda</h3>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">[39m
[31m+           {/* Slug */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">[39m
[31m+               URL de la tienda (slug)[39m
[31m+             </label>[39m
[31m+             <input[39m
[31m+               type="text"[39m
[31m+               value={store.slug}[39m
[31m+               onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, slug: e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-") }))}[39m
[31m+               placeholder="mi-bodega"[39m
[31m+               className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+             {store.slug && ([39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">marketplace.com/{store.slug}</p>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Nombre */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Nombre de la tienda</label>[39m
[31m+             <input[39m
[31m+               type="text"[39m
[31m+               value={store.name}[39m
[31m+               onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, name: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+               placeholder="Mi Bodega"[39m
[31m+               className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Categoría */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Categoría principal</label>[39m
[31m+             <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+               <select[39m
[31m+                 value={store.category}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, category: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary appearance-none transition-all"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {CATEGORIAS.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}[39m
[31m+               </select>[39m
[31m+               <ChevronDown className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Zona */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Zona de cobertura</label>[39m
[31m+             <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+               <select[39m
[31m+                 value={store.zone}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, zone: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary appearance-none transition-all"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {ZONAS.map((z) => <option key={z} value={z}>{z}</option>)}[39m
[31m+               </select>[39m
[31m+               <ChevronDown className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Comisión */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Comisión acordada (%)</label>[39m
[31m+             <input[39m
[31m+               type="number"[39m
[31m+               min={0}[39m
[31m+               max={30}[39m
[31m+               step={0.5}[39m
[31m+               value={store.commissionRate}[39m
[31m+               onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, commissionRate: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0 }))}[39m
[31m+               className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Logo URL */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">[39m
[31m+             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Logo de la tienda</label>[39m
[31m+             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">[39m
[31m+               <ImageUpload[39m
[31m+                 value={store.logoUrl}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(url) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, logoUrl: url }))}[39m
[31m+                 onClear={() => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, logoUrl: "" }))}[39m
[31m+                 folder="marketplace-logos"[39m
[31m+                 label=""[39m
[31m+                 hint="Logo cuadrado recomendado (200×200)"[39m
[31m+                 aspectRatio="square"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                   Sube tu logo o pega una URL. Se mostrará en la tarjeta de tu tienda en el marketplace.[39m
[31m+                 </p>[39m
[31m+                 <input[39m
[31m+                   type="url"[39m
[31m+                   value={store.logoUrl}[39m
[31m+                   onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, logoUrl: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                   placeholder="https://... o sube una imagen"[39m
[31m+                   className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary transition-all"[39m
[31m+                 />[39m
[31m+                 {store.logoUrl && ([39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-green-600">[39m
[31m+                     <CheckCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+                     Logo configurado[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Descripción */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+           <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Descripción de la tienda</label>[39m
[31m+           <textarea[39m
[31m+             rows={3}[39m
[31m+             value={store.description}[39m
[31m+             onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, description: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+             placeholder="Describe tu bodega, horarios, especialidades..."[39m
[31m+             className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary resize-none transition-all"[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Estado activo */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+           <div>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Tienda activa en marketplace</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Los clientes podrán encontrar y comprar en tu tienda</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, isActive: !p.isActive }))}[39m
[31m+             className={cn([39m
[31m+               "relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 items-center rounded-full transition-colors focus:outline-none",[39m
[31m+               store.isActive ? "bg-primary" : "bg-gray-300"[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <span className={cn([39m
[31m+               "inline-block h-4 w-4 transform rounded-full bg-white  transition-transform",[39m
[31m+               store.isActive ? "translate-x-6" : "translate-x-1"[39m
[31m+             )} />[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Modo vacaciones */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-3 p-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">🏖️ Modo vacaciones</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Pausa pedidos temporalmente sin despublicar tu tienda</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, vacationMode: !p.vacationMode }))}[39m
[31m+               className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 items-center rounded-full transition-colors focus:outline-none",[39m
[31m+                 store.vacationMode ? "bg-amber-500" : "bg-gray-300"[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <span className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "inline-block h-4 w-4 transform rounded-full bg-white  transition-transform",[39m
[31m+                 store.vacationMode ? "translate-x-6" : "translate-x-1"[39m
[31m+               )} />[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           {store.vacationMode && ([39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">Mensaje para tus clientes (opcional)</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                 value={store.vacationMessage ?? ""}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setStore((p) => ({ ...p, vacationMessage: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Ej: Volvemos el lunes 15. ¡Gracias por tu paciencia!"[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-300 focus:border-amber-400 transition-all"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2">[39m
[31m+           {saved && ([39m
[31m+             <span className="text-sm text-emerald-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+               <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" /> Guardado[39m
[31m+             </span>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={handleSave}[39m
[31m+             disabled={saving}[39m
[31m+             className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-sm font-bold hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {saving ? ([39m
[31m+               <div className="h-4 w-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               <Save className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             {saving ? "Guardando..." : "Guardar cambios"}[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Productos[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function ProductosTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [products, setProducts]   = useState<MarketplaceProduct[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading]     = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError]         = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [toggling, setToggling]   = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [syncing, setSyncing]     = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [syncResult, setSyncResult] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const load = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/marketplace/stores/my/products")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject()))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => setProducts(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []))[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setError("No se pudieron cargar los productos del marketplace."))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSync = async () => {[39m
[31m+     setSyncing(true);[39m
[31m+     setSyncResult(null);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/marketplace/stores/my/sync", { method: "POST" });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Error al sincronizar");[39m
[31m+       const data = await res.json();[39m
[31m+       const d = data.data;[39m
[31m+       setSyncResult(`✅ ${d.created} nuevos · ${d.updated} reactivados · ${d.deactivated} desactivados`);[39m
[31m+       load(); // recargar lista de productos[39m
[31m+       setTimeout(() => setSyncResult(null), 5000);[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al sincronizar inventario. Intenta nuevamente.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setSyncing(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const toggleActive = async (product: MarketplaceProduct) => {[39m
[31m+     setToggling(product.id);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/marketplace/stores/my/products/${product.id}`, {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ isActive: !product.isActive }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+       setProducts((prev) =>[39m
[31m+         prev.map((p) => (p.id === product.id ? { ...p, isActive: !p.isActive } : p))[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al actualizar el producto.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setToggling(null);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Barra de acciones: Sincronizar inventario */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+           {products.length} producto{products.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""} en marketplace[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={handleSync}[39m
[31m+           disabled={syncing}[39m
[31m+           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-sm font-bold hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <RefreshCw className={cn("h-4 w-4", syncing && "animate-spin")} />[39m
[31m+           {syncing ? "Sincronizando..." : "Sincronizar inventario"}[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {syncResult && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-emerald-700">[39m
[31m+           <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {syncResult}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {error && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+           <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {error}[39m
[31m+           <button onClick={load} className="ml-auto text-xs underline">Reintentar</button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {products.length === 0 && !error ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-16 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+           <Package className="h-10 w-10 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Sin productos publicados</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs mt-1">Activa productos desde tu catálogo para mostrarlos en el marketplace.</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl  overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+             <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+               <thead className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+                 <tr>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Producto</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Precio retail</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Mayorista</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Stock</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-center px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Publicado</th>[39m
[31m+                 </tr>[39m
[31m+               </thead>[39m
[31m+               <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">[39m
[31m+                 {products.map((p) => ([39m
[31m+                   <tr key={p.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{p.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">{p.sku}</p>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                       S/{p.retailPrice.toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-gray-600">[39m
[31m+                       S/{p.wholesalePrice.toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">[39m
[31m+                       <span className={cn([39m
[31m+                         "inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold",[39m
[31m+                         p.stock > 10 ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"[39m
[31m+                           : p.stock > 0 ? "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"[39m
[31m+                           : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"[39m
[31m+                       )}>[39m
[31m+                         {p.stock}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                     <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                       <button[39m
[31m+                         onClick={() => toggleActive(p)}[39m
[31m+                         disabled={toggling === p.id}[39m
[31m+                         title={p.isActive ? "Despublicar del marketplace" : "Publicar en marketplace"}[39m
[31m+                         className={cn([39m
[31m+                           "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors min-w-25 justify-center",[39m
[31m+                           p.isActive[39m
[31m+                             ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-200"[39m
[31m+                             : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-200"[39m
[31m+                         )}[39m
[31m+                       >[39m
[31m+                         {toggling === p.id ? ([39m
[31m+                           <div className="h-3.5 w-3.5 border-2 border-current border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+                         ) : p.isActive ? ([39m
[31m+                           <><Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Publicado</>[39m
[31m+                         ) : ([39m
[31m+                           <><EyeOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Inactivo</>[39m
[31m+                         )}[39m
[31m+                       </button>[39m
[31m+                     </td>[39m
[31m+                   </tr>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </tbody>[39m
[31m+             </table>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Órdenes[39m

[31m+ function OrdenesTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [orders, setOrders]   = useState<MarketplaceOrder[]>([]);[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+   const load = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m

[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m

[31m+           <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Sin órdenes del marketplace aún</p>[39m

[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Cliente</th>[39m

[31m+                     className: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600",[39m
[31m+                   };[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <tr key={o.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3">[39m

[31m+                           {statusConfig.label}[39m

[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                         {new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "2-digit", month: "short", year: "numeric" })}[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m

[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 })}[39m
