[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Comisiones[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ function ComisionesTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [entries, setEntries]   = useState<CommissionEntry[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [summary, setSummary]   = useState<CommissionSummary>({ pendiente: 0, liquidado: 0, pagado: 0 });[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading]   = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError]       = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<string>("all");[39m
[31m+   const [markingPaid, setMarkingPaid] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const load = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/commissions/ledger")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject()))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => {[39m
[31m+         const list: CommissionEntry[] = Array.isArray(d?.entries) ? d.entries : [];[39m
[31m+         setEntries(list);[39m
[31m+         const s: CommissionSummary = { pendiente: 0, liquidado: 0, pagado: 0 };[39m
[31m+         list.forEach((e) => { s[e.status] = (s[e.status] || 0) + e.amount; });[39m
[31m+         setSummary(s);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => setError("No se pudieron cargar las comisiones."))[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleMarkPaid = async (entryId: string) => {[39m
[31m+     setMarkingPaid(entryId);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/commissions/ledger", {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ ids: [entryId], status: "pagado" }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+       setEntries((prev) => prev.map((e) => e.id === entryId ? { ...e, status: "pagado" } : e));[39m
[31m+       setSummary((prev) => {[39m
[31m+         const entry = entries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);[39m
[31m+         if (!entry) return prev;[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           ...prev,[39m
[31m+           [entry.status]: prev[entry.status as keyof CommissionSummary] - entry.amount,[39m
[31m+           pagado: prev.pagado + entry.amount,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al marcar como pagado.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setMarkingPaid(null);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleBulkPay = async () => {[39m
[31m+     const settledIds = entries.filter((e) => e.status === "liquidado").map((e) => e.id);[39m
[31m+     if (settledIds.length === 0) return;[39m
[31m+     setMarkingPaid("bulk");[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/commissions/ledger", {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ ids: settledIds, status: "pagado" }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error();[39m
[31m+       load();[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al marcar comisiones como pagadas.");[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setMarkingPaid(null);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const filtered = filterStatus === "all" ? entries : entries.filter((e) => e.status === filterStatus);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-5">[39m
[31m+       {error && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-red-700">[39m
[31m+           <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+           {error}[39m
[31m+           <button onClick={load} className="ml-auto text-xs underline">Reintentar</button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* KPI cards */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         {[[39m
[31m+           { key: "pendiente", label: "Por pagar", color: "text-amber-600", bg: "bg-amber-50" },[39m
[31m+           { key: "liquidado", label: "Liquidado",  color: "text-emerald-600",  bg: "bg-emerald-50" },[39m
[31m+           { key: "pagado",    label: "Pagado",     color: "text-emerald-600", bg: "bg-emerald-50" },[39m
[31m+         ].map(({ key, label, color, bg }) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={key} className={cn("rounded-xl p-4 border border-gray-200 ", bg)}>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">{label}</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className={cn("text-2xl font-extrabold mt-1", color)}>[39m
[31m+               S/{(summary[key as keyof CommissionSummary] || 0).toFixed(2)}[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Filters + Bulk actions */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Filtrar:</span>[39m
[31m+           {[[39m
[31m+             { value: "all", label: "Todos" },[39m
[31m+             { value: "pendiente", label: "Pendiente" },[39m
[31m+             { value: "liquidado", label: "Liquidado" },[39m
[31m+             { value: "pagado", label: "Pagado" },[39m
[31m+           ].map((f) => ([39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               key={f.value}[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => setFilterStatus(f.value)}[39m
[31m+               className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors",[39m
[31m+                 filterStatus === f.value[39m
[31m+                   ? "bg-primary text-white"[39m
[31m+                   : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {f.label}[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {summary.liquidado > 0 && ([39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={handleBulkPay}[39m
[31m+             disabled={markingPaid === "bulk"}[39m
[31m+             className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <DollarSign className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+             {markingPaid === "bulk" ? "Procesando..." : `Pagar todo liquidado (S/${summary.liquidado.toFixed(2)})`}[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {filtered.length === 0 && !error ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-16 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+           <DollarSign className="h-10 w-10 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Sin comisiones {filterStatus !== "all" ? `en estado "${filterStatus}"` : "registradas aún"}</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl  overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-x-auto">[39m
[31m+             <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+               <thead className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+                 <tr>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Orden</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Total orden</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Comisión</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-center px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Estado</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Fecha</th>[39m
[31m+                   <th className="text-center px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">Acción</th>[39m
[31m+                 </tr>[39m
[31m+               </thead>[39m
[31m+               <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">[39m
[31m+                 {filtered.map((e) => {[39m
[31m+                   const sc = COMMISSION_STATUS_CONFIG[e.status] ?? COMMISSION_STATUS_CONFIG.pendiente;[39m
[31m+                   const StatusIcon = sc.icon;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <tr key={e.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3">[39m
[31m+                         <p className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+                           #{e.orderId.slice(-8).toUpperCase()}[39m
[31m+                         </p>[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-gray-600">S/{e.orderTotal.toFixed(2)}</td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-bold text-gray-900">S/{e.amount.toFixed(2)}</td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                         <span className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold", sc.className)}>[39m
[31m+                           <StatusIcon className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                           {sc.label}[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                         {new Date(e.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" })}[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                       <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+                         {e.status === "liquidado" && ([39m
[31m+                           <button[39m
[31m+                             onClick={() => handleMarkPaid(e.id)}[39m
[31m+                             disabled={markingPaid === e.id}[39m
[31m+                             className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 text-xs font-bold hover:bg-emerald-200 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+                             title="Marcar como pagado"[39m
[31m+                           >[39m
[31m+                             <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                             {markingPaid === e.id ? "..." : "Pagar"}[39m
[31m+                           </button>[39m
[31m+                         )}[39m
[31m+                         {e.status === "pagado" && ([39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">✓ Pagado</span>[39m
[31m+                         )}[39m
[31m+                       </td>[39m
[31m+                     </tr>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 })}[39m
[31m+               </tbody>[39m
[31m+             </table>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Componente principal[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Reseñas (moderación)[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ interface ReviewItem {[39m
[31m+   id: string;[39m
[31m+   name: string;[39m
[31m+   text: string;[39m
[31m+   rating: number;[39m
[31m+   status: string;[39m
[31m+   date: string;[39m
[31m+   phone?: string | null;[39m
[31m+   storeId?: string | null;[39m
[31m+   adminReply?: string | null;[39m
[31m+   adminReplyDate?: string | null;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Cupones[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ interface CouponItem {[39m
[31m+   id: string;[39m
[31m+   code: string;[39m
[31m+   description: string;[39m
[31m+   discountType: string;[39m
[31m+   discountValue: number;[39m
[31m+   minPurchase: number | null;[39m
[31m+   maxUses: number | null;[39m
[31m+   usedCount: number;[39m
[31m+   active: boolean;[39m
[31m+   expiresAt: string | null;[39m
[31m+   createdAt: string;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function CuponesTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [coupons, setCoupons] = useState<CouponItem[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [form, setForm] = useState({[39m
[31m+     code: "",[39m
[31m+     description: "",[39m
[31m+     discountType: "percent" as "percent" | "fixed",[39m
[31m+     discountValue: "",[39m
[31m+     minPurchase: "",[39m
[31m+     maxUses: "",[39m
[31m+     expiresAt: "",[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchCoupons = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/marketplace/coupons")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))[39m
[31m+       .then((d) => { if (d?.data) setCoupons(d.data); })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { fetchCoupons(); }, [fetchCoupons]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleCreate = async () => {[39m
[31m+     setSaving(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/marketplace/coupons", {[39m
[31m+         method: "POST",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({[39m
[31m+           code: form.code,[39m
[31m+           description: form.description,[39m
[31m+           discountType: form.discountType,[39m
[31m+           discountValue: Number(form.discountValue),[39m
[31m+           minPurchase: form.minPurchase ? Number(form.minPurchase) : null,[39m
[31m+           maxUses: form.maxUses ? Number(form.maxUses) : null,[39m
[31m+           expiresAt: form.expiresAt || null,[39m
[31m+         }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         setShowForm(false);[39m
[31m+         setForm({ code: "", description: "", discountType: "percent", discountValue: "", minPurchase: "", maxUses: "", expiresAt: "" });[39m
[31m+         fetchCoupons();[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch {}[39m
[31m+     setSaving(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const toggleActive = async (id: string, active: boolean) => {[39m
[31m+     await fetch(`/api/marketplace/coupons/${id}`, {[39m
[31m+       method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+       body: JSON.stringify({ active: !active }),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     fetchCoupons();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const deleteCoupon = async (id: string) => {[39m
[31m+     if (!confirm("¿Eliminar este cupón?")) return;[39m
[31m+     await fetch(`/api/marketplace/coupons/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });[39m
[31m+     fetchCoupons();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{coupons.length} cupón(es)</p>[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}[39m
[31m+           className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-primary text-white hover:opacity-90 transition"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           + Nuevo Cupón[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {showForm && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-3">[39m
[31m+           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Código</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="BIENVENIDO10"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.code}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, code: e.target.value })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Tipo</label>[39m
[31m+               <select[39m
[31m+                 value={form.discountType}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, discountType: e.target.value as "percent" | "fixed" })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 <option value="percent">Porcentaje (%)</option>[39m
[31m+                 <option value="fixed">Monto fijo (S/)</option>[39m
[31m+               </select>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">[39m
[31m+                 Valor {form.discountType === "percent" ? "(%)" : "(S/)"}[39m
[31m+               </label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="number"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="10"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.discountValue}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, discountValue: e.target.value })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Compra mínima (S/)</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="number"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Opcional"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.minPurchase}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, minPurchase: e.target.value })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Usos máximos</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="number"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Ilimitado"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.maxUses}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, maxUses: e.target.value })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Descripción</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Descuento de bienvenida"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.description}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Vence el</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="datetime-local"[39m
[31m+                 value={form.expiresAt}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, expiresAt: e.target.value ? new Date(e.target.value).toISOString() : "" })}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex gap-2 justify-end">[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}[39m
[31m+               className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-600 border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 transition"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Cancelar[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={handleCreate}[39m
[31m+               disabled={saving || !form.code || !form.discountValue}[39m
[31m+               className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-primary text-white hover:opacity-90 transition disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {saving ? "Guardando…" : "Crear Cupón"}[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {coupons.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+           <Ticket className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm">No hay cupones todavía</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs mt-1">Crea un cupón para atraer más clientes al marketplace.</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+         <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+           {coupons.map((c) => ([39m
[31m+             <div[39m
[31m+               key={c.id}[39m
[31m+               className={cn([39m
[31m+                 "flex items-center justify-between bg-white border rounded-xl p-3 ",[39m
[31m+                 !c.active && "opacity-60"[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                 <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                   <span className="font-mono font-bold text-sm text-primary">{c.code}</span>[39m
[31m+                   <span className={cn([39m
[31m+                     "text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full",[39m
[31m+                     c.active ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-500"[39m
[31m+                   )}>[39m
[31m+                     {c.active ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">[39m
[31m+                   {c.discountType === "percent" ? `${c.discountValue}%` : `S/${c.discountValue.toFixed(2)}`} de descuento[39m
[31m+                   {c.minPurchase ? ` · Mín S/${c.minPurchase.toFixed(2)}` : ""}[39m
[31m+                   {c.maxUses ? ` · ${c.usedCount}/${c.maxUses} usos` : ` · ${c.usedCount} usos`}[39m
[31m+                   {c.expiresAt ? ` · Vence ${new Date(c.expiresAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE")}` : ""}[39m
[31m+                 </p>[39m
[31m+                 {c.description && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{c.description}</p>}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 ml-2">[39m
[31m+                 <button[39m
[31m+                   onClick={() => toggleActive(c.id, c.active)}[39m
[31m+                   title={c.active ? "Desactivar" : "Activar"}[39m
[31m+                   className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-100 transition"[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   {c.active ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" />}[39m
[31m+                 </button>[39m
[31m+                 <button[39m
[31m+                   onClick={() => deleteCoupon(c.id)}[39m
[31m+                   title="Eliminar"[39m
[31m+                   className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition"[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   <X className="h-4 w-4 text-red-400" />[39m
[31m+                 </button>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ // Sub-tab: Fidelidad (Programa de puntos)[39m
[31m+ // ─────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ interface LoyaltyData {[39m
[31m+   phone: string;[39m
[31m+   name: string;[39m
[31m+   points: number;[39m
[31m+   tier: string;[39m
[31m+   totalSpent: number;[39m
[31m+   transactions: { id: string; type: string; points: number; description: string; createdAt: string }[];[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const TIER_CONFIG: Record<string, { label: string; className: string; minPoints: string }> = {[39m
[31m+   bronce: { label: "Bronce", className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700", minPoints: "0 - 499" },[39m
[31m+   plata:  { label: "Plata",  className: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600",   minPoints: "500 - 999" },[39m
[31m+   oro:    { label: "Oro",    className: "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700", minPoints: "1000+" },[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function FidelidadTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [data, setData] = useState<LoyaltyData | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [earnPoints, setEarnPoints] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const searchCustomer = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!phone.trim()) return;[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/marketplace/loyalty?phone=${encodeURIComponent(phone.trim())}`);[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         const d = await res.json();[39m
[31m+         setData(d.data);[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         setData(null);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch {}[39m
[31m+     setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleEarn = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!data || !earnPoints) return;[39m
[31m+     setSaving(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/marketplace/loyalty", {[39m
[31m+         method: "POST",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({[39m
[31m+           action: "earn",[39m
[31m+           phone: data.phone,[39m
[31m+           points: Number(earnPoints),[39m
[31m+           description: "Puntos asignados manualmente",[39m
[31m+         }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         setEarnPoints("");[39m
[31m+         searchCustomer();[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch {}[39m
[31m+     setSaving(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Info cards */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center">[39m
[31m+         {Object.entries(TIER_CONFIG).map(([key, cfg]) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={key} className={cn("rounded-xl p-2 text-xs font-semibold", cfg.className)}>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-sm">{cfg.label}</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] font-normal mt-0.5">{cfg.minPoints} pts</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Search */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex gap-2">[39m
[31m+         <input[39m
[31m+           type="text"[39m
[31m+           placeholder="Teléfono del cliente (ej: 961234567)"[39m
[31m+           value={phone}[39m
[31m+           onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+           onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && searchCustomer()}[39m
[31m+           className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+         <button[39m
[31m+           onClick={searchCustomer}[39m
[31m+           disabled={loading}[39m
[31m+           className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-primary text-white hover:opacity-90 transition disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           {loading ? "Buscando…" : "Buscar"}[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {data && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-4">[39m
[31m+           {/* Customer info */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="font-semibold text-sm">{data.name}</p>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{data.phone}</p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="text-right">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-primary">{data.points}</p>[39m
[31m+               <span className={cn("text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full", TIER_CONFIG[data.tier]?.className ?? "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600")}>[39m
[31m+                 {TIER_CONFIG[data.tier]?.label ?? data.tier}[39m
[31m+               </span>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Gasto total: S/{data.totalSpent.toFixed(2)}</p>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Manual earn */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex gap-2 items-end">[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex-1">[39m
[31m+               <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 block mb-1">Asignar puntos</label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="number"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="100"[39m
[31m+                 value={earnPoints}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setEarnPoints(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={handleEarn}[39m
[31m+               disabled={saving || !earnPoints}[39m
[31m+               className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-emerald-500 text-white hover:opacity-90 transition disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {saving ? "…" : "+ Dar puntos"}[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {/* Transaction history */}[39m
[31m+           {data.transactions.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-2">Historial reciente</p>[39m
[31m+               <div className="space-y-1 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">[39m
[31m+                 {data.transactions.map((tx) => ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs py-1 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0">[39m
[31m+                     <div>[39m
[31m+                       <span className={tx.type === "earn" ? "text-emerald-600 font-semibold" : "text-red-500 font-semibold"}>[39m
[31m+                         {tx.points > 0 ? "+" : ""}{tx.points} pts[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-gray-400 ml-2">{tx.description}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-gray-400 text-[10px]">[39m
[31m+                       {new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("es-PE")}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {!data && !loading && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+           <Gift className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm">Programa de Fidelidad</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs mt-1">Busca un cliente por teléfono para ver y gestionar sus puntos.</p>[39m
[31m+           <div className="mt-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-3 text-left max-w-xs mx-auto">[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-1">Reglas de puntos:</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">• 1 punto por cada S/1 de compra</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">• 500 pts = Nivel Plata (5% descuento)</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">• 1000 pts = Nivel Oro (10% descuento)</p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">• 100 pts = S/1 de descuento al canjear</p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const REVIEW_STATUS_CONFIG: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {[39m
[31m+   pending:  { label: "Pendiente", className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700" },[39m
[31m+   approved: { label: "Aprobada",  className: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" },[39m
[31m+   rejected: { label: "Rechazada", className: "bg-red-100 text-red-600" },[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function ResenasTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [reviews, setReviews] = useState<ReviewItem[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"all" | "pending" | "approved" | "rejected">("all");[39m
[31m+   const [replyingTo, setReplyingTo] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [replyText, setReplyText] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [saving, setSaving] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const loadReviews = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/reviews?all=1")[39m
[31m+       .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : []))[39m
[31m+       .then((data: ReviewItem[]) => {[39m
[31m+         // Filter to only store reviews (storeId present)[39m
[31m+         const storeReviews = data.filter((r) => r.storeId);[39m
[31m+         setReviews(storeReviews);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => {})[39m
[31m+       .finally(() => setLoading(false));[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { loadReviews(); }, [loadReviews]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleStatusChange = async (id: string, status: string) => {[39m
[31m+     setSaving(id);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/reviews/${id}`, {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ status }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         setReviews((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r)));[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* silencioso */ }[39m
[31m+     setSaving(null);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleReply = async (id: string) => {[39m
[31m+     if (!replyText.trim()) return;[39m
[31m+     setSaving(id);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/reviews/${id}`, {[39m
[31m+         method: "PATCH",[39m
[31m+         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({ adminReply: replyText.trim() }),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         setReviews((prev) =>[39m
[31m+           prev.map((r) =>[39m
[31m+             r.id === id ? { ...r, adminReply: replyText.trim(), adminReplyDate: new Date().toISOString() } : r[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         setReplyingTo(null);[39m
[31m+         setReplyText("");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* silencioso */ }[39m
[31m+     setSaving(null);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const filtered = filter === "all" ? reviews : reviews.filter((r) => r.status === filter);[39m
[31m+   const pendingCount = reviews.filter((r) => r.status === "pending").length;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) return <TableSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Summary strip */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-primary">{reviews.length}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Total reseñas</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-amber-600">{pendingCount}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Por moderar</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-600">[39m
[31m+             {reviews.length > 0[39m
[31m+               ? (reviews.reduce((s, r) => s + r.rating, 0) / reviews.length).toFixed(1)[39m
[31m+               : "—"}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Promedio ★</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Filter buttons */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+         {(["all", "pending", "approved", "rejected"] as const).map((f) => ([39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             key={f}[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => setFilter(f)}[39m
[31m+             className={cn([39m
[31m+               "px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",[39m
[31m+               filter === f[39m
[31m+                 ? "bg-primary text-white"[39m
[31m+                 : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {f === "all" ? `Todas (${reviews.length})` :[39m
[31m+              f === "pending" ? `Pendientes (${pendingCount})` :[39m
[31m+              f === "approved" ? `Aprobadas (${reviews.filter((r) => r.status === "approved").length})` :[39m
[31m+              `Rechazadas (${reviews.filter((r) => r.status === "rejected").length})`}[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Empty state */}[39m
[31m+       {filtered.length === 0 && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+           <Star className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm">No hay reseñas {filter !== "all" ? "con este filtro" : "todavía"}</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Review list */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+         {filtered.map((review) => {[39m
[31m+           const cfg = REVIEW_STATUS_CONFIG[review.status] ?? REVIEW_STATUS_CONFIG.pending;[39m
[31m+           return ([39m
[31m+             <div key={review.id} className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-3">[39m
[31m+               {/* Header: name, stars, status badge */}[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">[39m
[31m+                 <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-900 truncate">{review.name || "Anónimo"}</span>[39m
[31m+                     <span className={cn("px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold", cfg.className)}>[39m
[31m+                       {cfg.label}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">[39m
[31m+                     {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((s) => ([39m
[31m+                       <Star[39m
[31m+                         key={s}[39m
[31m+                         className={cn("h-3.5 w-3.5", s <= review.rating ? "fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" : "text-gray-200")}[39m
[31m+                       />[39m
[31m+                     ))}[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 ml-1">[39m
[31m+                       {new Date(review.date).toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" })}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 {/* Action buttons */}[39m
[31m+                 <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                   {review.status !== "approved" && ([39m
[31m+                     <button[39m
[31m+                       onClick={() => handleStatusChange(review.id, "approved")}[39m
[31m+                       disabled={saving === review.id}[39m
[31m+                       className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-100 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                       title="Aprobar"[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                     </button>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                   {review.status !== "rejected" && ([39m
[31m+                     <button[39m
[31m+                       onClick={() => handleStatusChange(review.id, "rejected")}[39m
[31m+                       disabled={saving === review.id}[39m
[31m+                       className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                       title="Rechazar"[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       <XCircle className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                     </button>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                   <button[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => { setReplyingTo(replyingTo === review.id ? null : review.id); setReplyText(review.adminReply ?? ""); }}[39m
[31m+                     className={cn([39m
[31m+                       "p-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors",[39m
[31m+                       replyingTo === review.id ? "bg-primary/20 text-primary" : "bg-gray-50 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100"[39m
[31m+                     )}[39m
[31m+                     title="Responder"[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <MessageSquare className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                   </button>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {/* Review text */}[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed">{review.text}</p>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {/* Existing admin reply */}[39m
[31m+               {review.adminReply && replyingTo !== review.id && ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="bg-primary/5 border border-primary/20 rounded-xl p-3">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-primary mb-1">Tu respuesta:</p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-xs text-gray-700">{review.adminReply}</p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {/* Reply form */}[39m
[31m+               {replyingTo === review.id && ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                   <textarea[39m
[31m+                     value={replyText}[39m
[31m+                     onChange={(e) => setReplyText(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+                     placeholder="Escribe tu respuesta al cliente..."[39m
[31m+                     rows={2}[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30 focus:border-primary resize-none"[39m
[31m+                   />[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <button[39m
[31m+                       onClick={() => handleReply(review.id)}[39m
[31m+                       disabled={saving === review.id || !replyText.trim()}[39m
[31m+                       className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       {saving === review.id ? "Guardando..." : "Enviar respuesta"}[39m
[31m+                     </button>[39m
[31m+                     <button[39m
[31m+                       onClick={() => { setReplyingTo(null); setReplyText(""); }}[39m
[31m+                       className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 text-xs font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       Cancelar[39m
[31m+                     </button>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+         })}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m

[31m+ // ─── Sub-tab Semana/Mes ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function SemaMesTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [monthlyGoal, setMonthlyGoal] = useState(50000);[39m
[31m+   const [editing, setEditing]         = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [tempGoal, setTempGoal]       = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [monthTotal, setMonthTotal]   = useState(0);[39m
[31m+   const [dailyHits, setDailyHits]     = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Cargar meta mensual y datos del mes desde /api/sales[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const stored = localStorage.getItem(LS_MONTHLY_GOAL);[39m
[31m+       if (stored) {[39m
[31m+         const n = Number(stored);[39m
[31m+         if (n > 0) setMonthlyGoal(n);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     void (async () => {[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const res = await fetch("/api/sales?limit=500", { credentials: "include" });[39m
[31m+         if (!res.ok) return;[39m
[31m+         const data: { total: number; createdAt: string }[] = await res.json();[39m
[31m+         const now   = new Date();[39m
[31m+         const year  = now.getFullYear();[39m
[31m+         const month = now.getMonth();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const dailyMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+         let total = 0;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         for (const s of Array.isArray(data) ? data : []) {[39m
[31m+           const d = new Date(s.createdAt);[39m
[31m+           if (d.getFullYear() !== year || d.getMonth() !== month) continue;[39m
[31m+           total += s.total ?? 0;[39m
[31m+           const key = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+           dailyMap[key] = (dailyMap[key] ?? 0) + (s.total ?? 0);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // Meta diaria aprox = monthly / días del mes[39m
[31m+         const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();[39m
[31m+         const dailyTarget = monthlyGoal / daysInMonth;[39m
[31m+         const hits: Record<string, boolean> = {};[39m
[31m+         for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(dailyMap)) {[39m
[31m+           hits[k] = v >= dailyTarget;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         setMonthTotal(total);[39m
[31m+         setDailyHits(hits);[39m
[31m+       } catch { /* silent */ }[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+   }, [monthlyGoal]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const pct = Math.min(100, monthlyGoal > 0 ? (monthTotal / monthlyGoal) * 100 : 0);[39m
[31m+   const barColor = pct >= 70 ? "bg-emerald-500" : pct >= 30 ? "bg-amber-500" : "bg-red-500";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSave = () => {[39m
[31m+     const val = Number(tempGoal);[39m
[31m+     if (val > 0) {[39m
[31m+       setMonthlyGoal(val);[39m
[31m+       localStorage.setItem(LS_MONTHLY_GOAL, String(val));[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setEditing(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mini-calendario del mes actual[39m
[31m+   const calendarDays = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const now       = new Date();[39m
[31m+     const year      = now.getFullYear();[39m
[31m+     const month     = now.getMonth();[39m
[31m+     const total     = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();[39m
[31m+     const firstDay  = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay(); // 0=dom[39m
[31m+     // Ajustar para que lunes sea columna 0[39m
[31m+     const offset    = firstDay === 0 ? 6 : firstDay - 1;[39m
[31m+     return { total, offset, year, month };[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* WeeklyGoalCard necesita prop sales — se pasa array vacío como fallback */}[39m
[31m+       <WeeklyGoalCard sales={[]} />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Card mensual */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 space-y-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+             <span className="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-primary/10">[39m
[31m+               <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+             </span>[39m
[31m+             <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+               Meta del mes[39m
[31m+             </span>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           {!editing ? ([39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => { setTempGoal(String(monthlyGoal)); setEditing(true); }}[39m
[31m+               className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-gray-400 hover:text-primary transition-colors px-2 py-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <Pencil className="w-3 h-3" /> Editar[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-1">[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 mr-1">S/</span>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="number"[39m
[31m+                 value={tempGoal}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setTempGoal(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+                 onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && handleSave()}[39m
[31m+                 autoFocus[39m
[31m+                 className="w-20 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-900 outline-none focus:border-primary"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={handleSave} className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600">[39m
[31m+                 <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setEditing(false)} className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 text-red-500">[39m
[31m+                 <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Barra de progreso */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-4 rounded-full bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className={cn("h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ease-out", barColor)}[39m
[31m+             style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m

[31m+ // ─── Sub-tab Logros ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function LogrosTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [unlocked, setUnlocked]     = useState<Record<string, string>>({});[39m
[31m+   const [showConfetti, setConfetti] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Cargar logros desde localStorage[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const stored = localStorage.getItem(LS_ACHIEVEMENTS);[39m
[31m+       if (stored) setUnlocked(JSON.parse(stored) as Record<string, string>);[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Verificar nuevos logros con datos del dashboard[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     void (async () => {[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const res = await fetch("/api/admin/dashboard", { credentials: "include" });[39m
[31m+         if (!res.ok) return;[39m
[31m+         const data = await res.json() as {[39m
[31m+           totalOrders?: number;[39m
[31m+           totalCustomers?: number;[39m
[31m+           totalSalesToday?: number;[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const streak  = Number(localStorage.getItem(LS_STREAK) ?? 0);[39m
[31m+         const current = (() => {[39m
[31m+           try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_ACHIEVEMENTS) ?? "{}") as Record<string, string>; }[39m
[31m+           catch { return {} as Record<string, string>; }[39m
[31m+         })();[39m
[31m+         const now     = new Date().toLocaleDateString("es-PE");[39m
[31m+         let changed   = false;[39m
[31m+         const next    = { ...current };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const unlock = (id: string) => {[39m
[31m+           if (!next[id]) { next[id] = now; changed = true; }[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if ((data.totalOrders ?? 0) >= 1)    unlock("primera-venta");[39m
[31m+         if ((data.totalOrders ?? 0) >= 100)  unlock("100-ventas");[39m
[31m+         if ((data.totalOrders ?? 0) >= 1000) unlock("1000-ventas");[39m
[31m+         if ((data.totalCustomers ?? 0) >= 50) unlock("50-clientes");[39m
[31m+         if (streak >= 7)  unlock("racha-7");[39m
[31m+         if (streak >= 30) unlock("racha-30");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (changed) {[39m
[31m+           localStorage.setItem(LS_ACHIEVEMENTS, JSON.stringify(next));[39m
[31m+           setUnlocked(next);[39m
[31m+           setConfetti(true);[39m
[31m+           setTimeout(() => setConfetti(false), 3500);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch { /* silent */ }[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const totalUnlocked = Object.keys(unlocked).length;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Resumen */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{totalUnlocked}</span>[39m
[31m+           {" "}de{" "}[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{ACHIEVEMENTS_DEF.length}</span>[39m
[31m+           {" "}logros desbloqueados[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-2 w-32 rounded-full bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className="h-full rounded-full bg-primary transition-all duration-500"[39m
[31m+             style={{ width: `${(totalUnlocked / ACHIEVEMENTS_DEF.length) * 100}%` }}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Grid de badges */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+         {showConfetti && <Confetti />}[39m
[31m+         <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+           {ACHIEVEMENTS_DEF.map((a) => {[39m
[31m+             const isUnlocked = Boolean(unlocked[a.id]);[39m
[31m+             return ([39m
[31m+               <div[39m
[31m+                 key={a.id}[39m
[31m+                 className={cn([39m
[31m+                   "relative rounded-xl border p-4 flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-2 transition-all duration-300",[39m
[31m+                   isUnlocked[39m
[31m+                     ? "border-primary/40 bg-gradient-to-b from-primary/5 to-primary/10 "[39m
[31m+                     : "border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 opacity-40"[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {/* Efecto brillo si desbloqueado */}[39m
[31m+                 {isUnlocked && ([39m
[31m+                   <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-xl overflow-hidden pointer-events-none">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="absolute -inset-full animate-[spin_4s_linear_infinite] bg-gradient-conic from-transparent via-primary/10 to-transparent" />[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-3xl leading-none">{a.emoji}</span>[39m
[31m+                 <div>[39m
[31m+                   <p className={cn([39m
[31m+                     "text-xs font-bold leading-tight",[39m
[31m+                     isUnlocked ? "text-gray-900" : "text-gray-600"[39m
[31m+                   )}>[39m
[31m+                     {a.name}[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5 leading-tight">[39m
[31m+                     {a.desc}[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 {isUnlocked ? ([39m
[31m+                   <span className="text-[9px] font-semibold text-primary bg-primary/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">[39m
[31m+                     {unlocked[a.id]}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 ) : ([39m
[31m+                   <span className="text-[9px] font-semibold text-gray-400">[39m
[31m+                     Bloqueado[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           })}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
