[31m+         grid[day][d.getHours()] += Number(s.total) || 0;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return grid;[39m
[31m+   }, [salesForHooks]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const heatmapMax = useMemo(() => Math.max(...heatmapData.flat(), 1), [heatmapData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 18: Forecast 7 dias — MUST be before early return[39m
[31m+   const forecastData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const nowHook = new Date();[39m
[31m+     const dayMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+     for (let i = 13; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+       const d = new Date(nowHook.getFullYear(), nowHook.getMonth(), nowHook.getDate() - i);[39m
[31m+       dayMap[d.toISOString().split("T")[0]] = 0;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     for (const s of salesForHooks) {[39m
[31m+       const dk = String(s.createdAt ?? "").slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+       if (dk in dayMap) dayMap[dk] += Number(s.total) || 0;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const entries = Object.entries(dayMap).sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));[39m
[31m+     const result: Array<{ dia: string; real?: number; prediccion?: number }> = [];[39m
[31m+     for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {[39m
[31m+       const [date, total] = entries[i];[39m
[31m+       const label = new Date(date + "T12:00:00").toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" });[39m
[31m+       const windowStart = Math.max(0, i - 3);[39m
[31m+       const w = entries.slice(windowStart, i);[39m
[31m+       const avg = w.length > 0 ? Math.round(w.reduce((s, [, v]) => s + v, 0) / w.length) : 0;[39m
[31m+       result.push({ dia: label, real: i < entries.length - 7 ? total : total || undefined, prediccion: i >= 7 ? avg : undefined });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const lastVals = entries.slice(-7).map(([, v]) => v);[39m
[31m+     for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {[39m
[31m+       const d = new Date(nowHook.getFullYear(), nowHook.getMonth(), nowHook.getDate() + i);[39m
[31m+       const label = d.toLocaleDateString("es-PE", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" });[39m
[31m+       const avg = Math.round(lastVals.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / Math.max(lastVals.length, 1));[39m
[31m+       result.push({ dia: label, prediccion: avg });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return result;[39m
[31m+   }, [salesForHooks]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 20: Month comparison — MUST be before early return[39m
[31m+   const monthOptions = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const options: string[] = [];[39m
[31m+     for (let y = 2024; y <= 2026; y++) {[39m
[31m+       for (let m = 0; m < 12; m++) {[39m
[31m+         options.push(`${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return options;[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const monthComparisonData = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const getSalesForMonth = (monthKey: string) => salesForHooks.filter(s => String(s.createdAt ?? "").startsWith(monthKey));[39m
[31m+     const sales1 = getSalesForMonth(month1);[39m
[31m+     const sales2 = getSalesForMonth(month2);[39m
[31m+     const total1 = sales1.reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+     const total2 = sales2.reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+     const count1 = sales1.length;[39m
[31m+     const count2 = sales2.length;[39m
[31m+     const weekData: Array<{ semana: string; mes1: number; mes2: number }> = [];[39m
[31m+     for (let w = 1; w <= 5; w++) {[39m
[31m+       const dayStart = (w - 1) * 7 + 1;[39m
[31m+       const dayEnd = w * 7;[39m
[31m+       const w1 = sales1.filter(s => { const d = new Date(String(s.createdAt ?? "")).getDate(); return d >= dayStart && d <= dayEnd; }).reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+       const w2 = sales2.filter(s => { const d = new Date(String(s.createdAt ?? "")).getDate(); return d >= dayStart && d <= dayEnd; }).reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+       weekData.push({ semana: `Sem ${w}`, mes1: +w1.toFixed(0), mes2: +w2.toFixed(0) });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const diffPct = total1 > 0 ? Math.round(((total2 - total1) / total1) * 100) : 0;[39m
[31m+     return { total1, total2, count1, count2, weekData, diffPct };[39m
[31m+   }, [salesForHooks, month1, month2]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const MESES_LABEL: Record<string, string> = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const m: Record<string, string> = {};[39m
[31m+     const names = ["Ene", "Feb", "Mar", "Abr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Ago", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dic"];[39m
[31m+     monthOptions.forEach(o => { const [y, mo] = o.split("-"); m[o] = `${names[parseInt(mo) - 1]} ${y}`; });[39m
[31m+     return m;[39m
[31m+   }, [monthOptions]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading || !data) return <DashboardSkeleton />;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 7: Filter sales by period[39m
[31m+   const now = new Date();[39m
[31m+   const today = now.toISOString().split("T")[0];[39m
[31m+   const monthStart = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;[39m
[31m+   const filteredSales = data.sales.filter((s) => {[39m
[31m+     const d = String(s.createdAt ?? "");[39m
[31m+     if (period === "today") return d.startsWith(today);[39m
[31m+     if (period === "7d") {[39m
[31m+       const sevenAgo = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 7).toISOString().split("T")[0];[39m
[31m+       return d.slice(0, 10) >= sevenAgo;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (period === "30d") {[39m
[31m+       const thirtyAgo = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 30).toISOString().split("T")[0];[39m
[31m+       return d.slice(0, 10) >= thirtyAgo;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (period === "month") return d.startsWith(monthStart);[39m
[31m+     return true;[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Calcular metricas[39m
[31m+   const ventasPeriodo = filteredSales.reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+   const transacciones = filteredSales.length;[39m
[31m+   const ticketPromedio = transacciones > 0 ? ventasPeriodo / transacciones : 0;[39m
[31m+   const pedidosPendientes = data.orders.filter((o) => o.status === "pendiente" || o.status === "confirmado").length;[39m
[31m+   const ventasMes = data.sales.filter(s => String(s.createdAt ?? "").startsWith(monthStart)).reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Ventas por hora[39m
[31m+   const hourlyMap: Record<number, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   for (let h = 6; h <= 22; h++) hourlyMap[h] = 0;[39m
[31m+   for (const s of filteredSales) {[39m
[31m+     const d = new Date(String(s.createdAt ?? ""));[39m
[31m+     if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) {[39m
[31m+       const h = d.getHours();[39m
[31m+       hourlyMap[h] = (hourlyMap[h] ?? 0) + (Number(s.total) || 0);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   const hourlyData = Object.entries(hourlyMap).map(([h, total]) => ({ hora: `${h}`, total: +total.toFixed(2) }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Metodos de pago[39m
[31m+   const paymentMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   for (const s of filteredSales) {[39m
[31m+     const pm = String(s.payment ?? s.paymentMethod ?? "efectivo").toLowerCase();[39m
[31m+     const key = pm.includes("yape") ? "Yape" : pm.includes("plin") ? "Plin" : pm.includes("tarjeta") ? "Tarjeta" : pm.includes("fiado") ? "Fiado" : "Efectivo";[39m
[31m+     paymentMap[key] = (paymentMap[key] ?? 0) + (Number(s.total) || 0);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   const paymentData = Object.entries(paymentMap).filter(([, v]) => v > 0).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value: +value.toFixed(2) }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Top 5 productos[39m
[31m+   const productMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   for (const s of filteredSales) {[39m
[31m+     const items = Array.isArray(s.items) ? s.items : [];[39m
[31m+     for (const item of items) {[39m
[31m+       const name = String((item as Record<string, unknown>).name ?? "Producto");[39m
[31m+       const total = (Number((item as Record<string, unknown>).price) || 0) * (Number((item as Record<string, unknown>).quantity) || 1);[39m
[31m+       productMap[name] = (productMap[name] ?? 0) + total;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   const topProducts = Object.entries(productMap)[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[39m
[31m+     .slice(0, 5)[39m
[31m+     .map(([name, total]) => ({ name: name.length > 18 ? name.slice(0, 16) + "..." : name, total: +total.toFixed(2) }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Tendencia semanal[39m
[31m+   const weeklyMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {[39m
[31m+     const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - i);[39m
[31m+     const key = d.toISOString().split("T")[0];[39m
[31m+     weeklyMap[key] = 0;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   for (const s of data.sales) {[39m
[31m+     const d = String(s.createdAt ?? "").slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+     if (d in weeklyMap) weeklyMap[d] += Number(s.total) || 0;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   const dias = ["Dom", "Lun", "Mar", "Mie", "Jue", "Vie", "Sab"];[39m
[31m+   const weeklyData = Object.entries(weeklyMap).map(([date, total]) => {[39m
[31m+     const dayIdx = new Date(date + "T12:00:00").getDay();[39m
[31m+     return { dia: dias[dayIdx], total: +total.toFixed(2) };[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mejora 4: Comparativo vs semana pasada[39m
[31m+   const thisWeekMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   const lastWeekMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {[39m
[31m+     const dThis = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - (6 - i));[39m
[31m+     const dLast = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - (13 - i));[39m
[31m+     const keyThis = dThis.toISOString().split("T")[0];[39m
[31m+     const keyLast = dLast.toISOString().split("T")[0];[39m
[31m+     const dayLabel = dias[dThis.getDay()];[39m
[31m+     thisWeekMap[dayLabel] = 0;[39m
[31m+     lastWeekMap[dayLabel] = 0;[39m
[31m+     for (const s of data.sales) {[39m
[31m+       const d = String(s.createdAt ?? "").slice(0, 10);[39m
[31m+       if (d === keyThis) thisWeekMap[dayLabel] += Number(s.total) || 0;[39m
[31m+       if (d === keyLast) lastWeekMap[dayLabel] += Number(s.total) || 0;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   const comparisonData = Object.keys(thisWeekMap).map(dia => ({[39m
[31m+     dia,[39m
[31m+     estaSemana: +thisWeekMap[dia].toFixed(2),[39m
[31m+     semanaPasada: +lastWeekMap[dia].toFixed(2),[39m
[31m+   }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Fiados total[39m
[31m+   const fiadoTotal = filteredSales[39m
[31m+     .filter(s => String(s.payment ?? s.paymentMethod ?? "").toLowerCase().includes("fiado"))[39m
[31m+     .reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const sections = [[39m
[31m+     // Section 0: Period selector (Mejora 7) + Refresh (Mejora 9) + Export (Mejora 8)[39m
[31m+     <div key="controls" className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">[39m
[31m+       <PeriodSelector value={period} onChange={setPeriod} />[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+         <AutoRefreshControl secondsLeft={autoRefresh.secondsLeft} paused={autoRefresh.paused} isActive={autoRefresh.isActive} onTogglePause={autoRefresh.togglePause} onRefreshNow={autoRefresh.refreshNow} />[39m
[31m+         <ExportButton />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>,[39m
[31m+     // Mejora 6: Alertas inteligentes Ventas[39m
[31m+     ...(pedidosPendientes > 0 || fiadoTotal > 0 ? [[39m
[31m+       <div key="alertas" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">[39m
[31m+         {pedidosPendientes > 0 && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"><AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> {pedidosPendientes} pedidos pendientes</span>}[39m
[31m+         {fiadoTotal > 0 && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-700"><AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> S/ {fiadoTotal.toFixed(0)} en fiados</span>}[39m
[31m+       </div>,[39m
[31m+     ] : []),[39m
[31m+     // Section 1: KPIs with sparklines (Mejora 8) and comparativo (Mejora 7)[39m
[31m+     <div key="kpis" className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">[39m
[31m+       {[[39m
[31m+         { label: "Ventas periodo", value: `S/ ${ventasPeriodo.toFixed(0)}`, color: "border-b-4 border-primary", spark: true, strokeColor: "var(--color-primary)", sparkVal: ventasPeriodo },[39m
[31m+         { label: "Transacciones", value: String(transacciones), color: "border-b-4 border-emerald-500", spark: true, strokeColor: "#3b82f6", sparkVal: transacciones },[39m
[31m+         { label: "Ticket promedio", value: `S/ ${ticketPromedio.toFixed(0)}`, color: "border-b-4 border-purple-500", spark: true, strokeColor: "#8b5cf6", sparkVal: ticketPromedio },[39m
[31m+         { label: "Pedidos pend.", value: String(pedidosPendientes), color: "border-b-4 border-amber-500", spark: false, strokeColor: "", sparkVal: 0 },[39m
[31m+         { label: "Fiados", value: `S/ ${fiadoTotal.toFixed(0)}`, color: "border-b-4 border-orange-500", spark: false, strokeColor: "", sparkVal: 0, onClick: onNavigate ? () => onNavigate("cuentas-cobrar") : undefined },[39m
[31m+         { label: "Total ventas mes", value: `S/ ${ventasMes.toFixed(0)}`, color: "border-b-4 border-cyan-500", spark: false, strokeColor: "", sparkVal: 0 },[39m
[31m+       ].map((k, idx) => {[39m
[31m+         // Valores determinísticos (no Math.random — pureza en render). Placeholder hasta que[39m
[31m+         // venga un cálculo real de trend vs periodo anterior desde el backend.[39m
[31m+         const change = [12, 8, -3, -6, 15, 4][idx] ?? 0;[39m
[31m+         return ([39m
[31m+           <div key={k.label} onClick={k.onClick} className={cn("bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4  transition-shadow", k.color, k.onClick && "cursor-pointer hover:shadow-sm")}>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-medium">{k.label}</p>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-2xl font-mono font-bold mt-1 text-gray-900">{k.value}</p>[39m
[31m+               <span className={`text-xs ${change >= 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-500"}`}>{change >= 0 ? "\u2191" : "\u2193"} {Math.abs(change)}%</span>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             {k.spark && ([39m
[31m+               <div className="h-8 w-20 mt-1">[39m
[31m+                 <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">[39m
[31m+                   <LineChart data={[{v:k.sparkVal*0.7},{v:k.sparkVal*0.85},{v:k.sparkVal*0.75},{v:k.sparkVal*0.9},{v:k.sparkVal*0.82},{v:k.sparkVal*0.95},{v:k.sparkVal}]}>[39m
[31m+                     <Line type="monotone" dataKey="v" stroke={k.strokeColor} strokeWidth={1.5} dot={false} />[39m
[31m+                   </LineChart>[39m
[31m+                 </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       })}[39m
[31m+     </div>,[39m
[31m+     // Section 2: Ventas por hora[39m
[31m+     <div key="hourly" className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-6  relative">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><FavStar id="ventas-hora" favs={salesFavs} /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Ventas por hora</h3></div>[39m
[31m+         <button onClick={() => setExpandedChart("hourly")} className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded transition-colors" title="Expandir">[39m
[31m+           <Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-gray-400" />[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       {hourlyData.some(h => h.total > 0) ? ([39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>[39m
[31m+           <BarChart data={hourlyData} onClick={(e) => { const ev = e as { activePayload?: { payload?: { hora?: unknown } }[] } | null; if (ev?.activePayload?.[0]?.payload?.hora != null) setDrillHour(Number(ev.activePayload[0].payload.hora)); }}>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(107,114,128,0.12)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="hora" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+             <ReferenceLine y={500} stroke="#f97316" strokeDasharray="5 5" label={{ value: "Meta: S/500", position: "right", fill: "#f97316", fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+             <Bar dataKey="total" fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} className="cursor-pointer" />[39m
[31m+           </BarChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       ) : <EmptyState icon={BarChart3} title="Sin ventas en este periodo" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres ventas" />}[39m
[31m+     </div>,[39m
[31m+     // Section 3: PieChart + Top5[39m
[31m+     <div key="charts-row" className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><FavStar id="metodo-pago" favs={salesFavs} /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Por metodo de pago</h3></div>[39m
[31m+           {pieFilter && ([39m
[31m+             <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-bold">[39m
[31m+               Filtrando: {pieFilter}[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setPieFilter(null)} className="hover:bg-primary/20 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors"><X className="h-3 w-3" /></button>[39m
[31m+             </span>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {paymentData.length > 0 ? ([39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>[39m
[31m+             <PieChart>[39m
[31m+               <Pie data={paymentData} innerRadius={50} outerRadius={80} dataKey="value" label className="cursor-pointer"[39m
[31m+                 onClick={(_: unknown, idx: number) => setPieFilter(prev => prev === paymentData[idx]?.name ? null : paymentData[idx]?.name ?? null)}>[39m
[31m+                 {paymentData.map((_, i) => <Cell key={i} fill={DASHBOARD_COLORS[i % DASHBOARD_COLORS.length]} />)}[39m
[31m+               </Pie>[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+               <Legend />[39m
[31m+             </PieChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         ) : <EmptyState icon={BarChart3} title="Sin ventas en este periodo" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres ventas" />}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-6 ">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mb-4"><FavStar id="top-productos" favs={salesFavs} /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Top 5 productos</h3></div>[39m
[31m+         {topProducts.length > 0 ? ([39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-2.5">[39m
[31m+             {topProducts.map((p, i) => ([39m
[31m+               <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+                 <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary text-white text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">{p.name.charAt(0)}</div>[39m
[31m+                 <span className="flex-1 text-sm text-gray-700 truncate">{p.name}</span>[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-sm font-bold font-mono text-gray-900">S/ {p.total.toFixed(0)}</span>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             ))}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ) : <EmptyState icon={BarChart3} title="Sin productos vendidos" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres ventas" />}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>,[39m
[31m+     // Section 4: Tendencia semanal[39m

[31m+           <Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-gray-400" />[39m
[31m+         </button>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={250}>[39m

[31m+               <stop offset="100%" stopColor="var(--color-primary)" stopOpacity={0} />[39m
[31m+             </linearGradient>[39m

[31m+                 return ([39m

[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               }}[39m
[31m+             />[39m

[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       ) : <EmptyState icon={BarChart3} title="Sin datos comparativos" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres ventas" />}[39m
[31m+     </div>,[39m
[31m+     // Section 6: Mejora 17 — Mapa de calor[39m

[31m+           <select value={month1} onChange={e => setMonth1(e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 bg-white text-gray-700">[39m

[31m+           </select>[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">vs</span>[39m
[31m+           <select value={month2} onChange={e => setMonth2(e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 bg-white text-gray-700">[39m
[31m+             {monthOptions.map(o => <option key={o} value={o}>{MESES_LABEL[o]}</option>)}[39m
[31m+           </select>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold">{MESES_LABEL[month1]}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-lg font-extrabold", monthComparisonData.total1 === 0 ? "text-gray-300" : "text-primary")}>{formatCurrency(monthComparisonData.total1, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m

[31m+         <div className="text-center p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">[39m

[31m+           <p className={cn("text-lg font-extrabold", monthComparisonData.total2 === 0 ? "text-gray-300" : "text-[#f97316]")}>{formatCurrency(monthComparisonData.total2, { decimals: 0 })}</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{monthComparisonData.count2} ventas</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-center p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold">Diferencia</p>[39m
[31m+           <p className={cn("text-lg font-extrabold", monthComparisonData.diffPct >= 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-600")}>[39m

[31m+           </p>[39m

[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       {monthComparisonData.weekData.some(w => w.mes1 > 0 || w.mes2 > 0) ? ([39m
[31m+         <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>[39m
[31m+           <BarChart data={monthComparisonData.weekData}>[39m
[31m+             <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(107,114,128,0.12)" />[39m
[31m+             <XAxis dataKey="semana" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+             <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+             <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+             <Legend formatter={(v: string) => v === "mes1" ? MESES_LABEL[month1] : MESES_LABEL[month2]} />[39m

[31m+             <Bar dataKey="mes2" fill="#f97316" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+           </BarChart>[39m
[31m+         </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+       ) : <EmptyState icon={BarChart3} title="Sin datos para estos meses" description="Los datos apareceran cuando registres ventas" />}[39m
[31m+     </div>,[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="dashboard-print space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {sections.map((section, index) => ([39m
[31m+         <m.div[39m
[31m+           key={index}[39m
[31m+           initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}[39m
[31m+           animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}[39m
[31m+           transition={{ duration: 0.3, delay: index * 0.05 }}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           {section}[39m
[31m+         </m.div>[39m
[31m+       ))}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Expanded chart modals */}[39m
[31m+       {expandedChart === "hourly" && ([39m
[31m+         <ChartExpandModal title="Ventas por hora" onClose={() => setExpandedChart(null)}>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+             <BarChart data={hourlyData}>[39m
[31m+               <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(107,114,128,0.12)" />[39m
[31m+               <XAxis dataKey="hora" tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m
[31m+               <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+               <Bar dataKey="total" fill="var(--color-primary)" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />[39m
[31m+             </BarChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </ChartExpandModal>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {expandedChart === "weekly" && ([39m
[31m+         <ChartExpandModal title="Tendencia de ventas" onClose={() => setExpandedChart(null)}>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+             <AreaChart data={weeklyData}>[39m
[31m+               <defs>[39m
[31m+                 <linearGradient id="salesGradBig" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">[39m
[31m+                   <stop offset="0%" stopColor="var(--color-primary)" stopOpacity={0.3} />[39m
[31m+                   <stop offset="100%" stopColor="var(--color-primary)" stopOpacity={0} />[39m
[31m+                 </linearGradient>[39m
[31m+               </defs>[39m
[31m+               <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(107,114,128,0.12)" />[39m
[31m+               <XAxis dataKey="dia" tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m
[31m+               <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m
[31m+               <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />[39m
[31m+               <Area dataKey="total" stroke="var(--color-primary)" fill="url(#salesGradBig)" strokeWidth={2.5} />[39m
[31m+             </AreaChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </ChartExpandModal>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {expandedChart === "heatmap" && ([39m
[31m+         <ChartExpandModal title="Mapa de Calor de Ventas" onClose={() => setExpandedChart(null)}>[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-auto h-full flex items-center justify-center">[39m
[31m+             <div className="min-w-175">[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex gap-1 mb-2 ml-14">[39m
[31m+                 {Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, h) => ([39m
[31m+                   <div key={h} className="flex-1 text-center text-[10px] text-gray-400">{h}h</div>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               {["Lun", "Mar", "Mie", "Jue", "Vie", "Sab", "Dom"].map((day, di) => ([39m
[31m+                 <div key={day} className="flex gap-1 items-center mb-1">[39m
[31m+                   <span className="w-12 text-xs text-gray-500 text-right pr-2 shrink-0 font-medium">{day}</span>[39m
[31m+                   {Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, h) => {[39m
[31m+                     const val = heatmapData[di]?.[h] ?? 0;[39m
[31m+                     const intensity = heatmapMax > 0 ? val / heatmapMax : 0;[39m
[31m+                     return ([39m
[31m+                       <div key={h} className="flex-1 h-8 rounded transition-colors" style={{ backgroundColor: intensity > 0 ? `rgba(45,106,79,${Math.max(intensity, 0.08)})` : "rgba(107,114,128,0.06)" }} title={`${day} ${h}:00 — S/ ${val.toFixed(0)}`} />[39m
[31m+                     );[39m
[31m+                   })}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </ChartExpandModal>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {expandedChart === "forecast" && ([39m
[31m+         <ChartExpandModal title="Pronostico 7 dias" onClose={() => setExpandedChart(null)}>[39m
[31m+           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={500}>[39m
[31m+             <LineChart data={forecastData}>[39m
[31m+               <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(107,114,128,0.12)" />[39m
[31m+               <XAxis dataKey="dia" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />[39m
[31m+               <YAxis tickFormatter={formatSolesShort} tick={{ fontSize: 13 }} />[39m

[31m+               <Legend formatter={(v: string) => v === "real" ? "Ventas reales" : "Prediccion"} />[39m
[31m+               <Line type="monotone" dataKey="real" stroke="var(--color-primary)" strokeWidth={3} dot={{ r: 4, fill: "var(--color-primary)" }} connectNulls={false} />[39m
[31m+               <Line type="monotone" dataKey="prediccion" stroke="#9b5de5" strokeWidth={2.5} strokeDasharray="6 3" dot={{ r: 4, fill: "#9b5de5" }} connectNulls={false} />[39m
[31m+             </LineChart>[39m
[31m+           </ResponsiveContainer>[39m
[31m+         </ChartExpandModal>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Mejora 11: Drill-down modal para ventas por hora */}[39m
[31m+       {drillHour !== null && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" onClick={() => setDrillHour(null)}>[39m
[31m+           <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 max-w-lg w-full max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">[39m
[31m+               <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-gray-900">Ventas de las {drillHour}:00</h3>[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={() => setDrillHour(null)} className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-full transition-colors"><X className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" /></button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             {(() => {[39m
[31m+               const hourSales = filteredSales.filter(s => {[39m
[31m+                 const d = new Date(String(s.createdAt ?? ""));[39m
[31m+                 return !isNaN(d.getTime()) && d.getHours() === drillHour;[39m
[31m+               });[39m
[31m+               if (hourSales.length === 0) return <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-4">Sin ventas en esta hora</p>;[39m
[31m+               const totalHour = hourSales.reduce((s: number, v) => s + (Number(v.total) || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 p-3 bg-primary/5 rounded-xl">[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{hourSales.length} ventas</p>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-primary">Total: S/ {totalHour.toFixed(2)}</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                     {hourSales.slice(0, 20).map((s, i) => ([39m
[31m+                       <div key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between py-2 px-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl text-xs">[39m
[31m+                         <div>[39m
[31m+                           <p className="font-medium text-gray-700">{new Date(String(s.createdAt ?? "")).toLocaleTimeString("es-PE", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}</p>[39m
[31m+                           <p className="text-gray-400 text-[10px]">{String(s.payment ?? s.paymentMethod ?? "efectivo")}</p>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                         <p className="font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">S/ {(Number(s.total) || 0).toFixed(2)}</p>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     ))}[39m
[31m+                     {hourSales.length > 20 && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center">y {hourSales.length - 20} mas...</p>}[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             })()}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ void SalesDashboard;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Tabs reordenados en flujo lógico del día ──────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+ const TABS = [[39m
[31m+   { id: "pos"               as const, label: "Vender",            shortLabel: "POS",     hint: "Punto de venta",      icon: ShoppingCart,  desc: "Busca productos, cobra y genera comprobantes" },[39m
[31m+   { id: "turnos"            as const, label: "Turnos",            shortLabel: "Turnos",  hint: "Control de personal", icon: Clock,         desc: "Abre y cierra turnos de trabajo del equipo" },[39m
[31m+   { id: "caja-registradora" as const, label: "Caja Registradora", shortLabel: "Caja",    hint: "Gestión de efectivo", icon: Wallet,        desc: "Movimientos de efectivo, retiros e ingresos" },[39m
[31m+   { id: "cuentas-cobrar"    as const, label: "Me deben",          shortLabel: "Fiados",  hint: "Créditos a clientes", icon: HandCoins,     desc: "Créditos otorgados, cobros y seguimiento" },[39m
[31m+   { id: "arqueo"            as const, label: "Cuadrar Caja",      shortLabel: "Cuadre",      hint: "Cierre del día",      icon: Scale,         desc: "Conteo de billetes y cierre del día" },[39m
[31m+   { id: "comisiones"        as const, label: "Comisiones",        shortLabel: "Comisiones",  hint: "Cálculo comisiones",  icon: Users,         desc: "Calcula comisiones de vendedores" },[39m
[31m+ ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Índices tras los cuales insertar separador visual (entre grupos lógicos)[39m
[31m+ const _SEPARATOR_AFTER_INDICES = [1, 3, 4]; // Después de Dashboard (idx 1), Turnos (idx 3), Caja Registradora (idx 4)[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ type TabId = typeof TABS[number]["id"];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function normalizeVentasCajaTab(savedTab: string | null): TabId {[39m
[31m+   if (savedTab === "resumen" || savedTab === "pedidos" || savedTab === "dashboard") {[39m
[31m+     return "pos";[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return TABS.some(tab => tab.id === savedTab) ? (savedTab as TabId) : TABS[0].id;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Shift Close Modal Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface ShiftSummary {[39m
[31m+   totalVendido: number;[39m
[31m+   numVentas: number;[39m
[31m+   efectivo: number;[39m
[31m+   yape: number;[39m
[31m+   plin: number;[39m
[31m+   tarjeta: number;[39m
[31m+   fiado: number;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function ShiftCloseModal({[39m
[31m+   onClose,[39m
[31m+   onConfirm,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   onClose: () => void;[39m
[31m+   onConfirm: () => void;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const [summary, setSummary] = useState<ShiftSummary | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchSummary = useCallback(async () => {[39m
[31m+     setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+     setError(null);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch("/api/sales?today=1");[39m
[31m+       if (!res.ok) throw new Error("No se pudo cargar las ventas");[39m
[31m+       const sales = await res.json();[39m
[31m+       const arr = Array.isArray(sales) ? sales : [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const data: ShiftSummary = {[39m
[31m+         totalVendido: 0,[39m
[31m+         numVentas: arr.length,[39m
[31m+         efectivo: 0,[39m
[31m+         yape: 0,[39m
[31m+         plin: 0,[39m
[31m+         tarjeta: 0,[39m
[31m+         fiado: 0,[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       for (const sale of arr) {[39m
[31m+         const total = typeof sale.total === "number" ? sale.total : 0;[39m
[31m+         data.totalVendido += total;[39m
[31m+         const pm = (sale.payment ?? "").toLowerCase();[39m
[31m+         if (pm.includes("efectivo")) data.efectivo += total;[39m
[31m+         else if (pm.includes("yape")) data.yape += total;[39m
[31m+         else if (pm.includes("plin")) data.plin += total;[39m
[31m+         else if (pm.includes("tarjeta")) data.tarjeta += total;[39m
[31m+         else if (pm.includes("fiado")) data.fiado += total;[39m
[31m+         else data.efectivo += total; // default bucket[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       setSummary(data);[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setError("Error al cargar el resumen de ventas");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     void fetchSummary();[39m
[31m+   }, [fetchSummary]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleConfirm = async () => {[39m
[31m+     setConfirming(true);[39m
[31m+     // Attempt to close shift via API (best-effort)[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       await fetch("/api/cash-registers/close-shift", { method: "POST" });[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       // ignore — shift close is optional[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setConfirming(false);[39m
[31m+     onConfirm();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fmt = (n: number) => `S/${n.toFixed(2)}`;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">[39m
[31m+       <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+         {/* Header */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="bg-primary px-6 py-4">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-white">Cerrar Turno</h3>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm text-white/80">Resumen del dia antes de cerrar</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Body */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="p-6 space-y-5">[39m
[31m+           {loading ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-8">[39m
[31m+               <div className="h-8 w-8 border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ) : error ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl p-4 text-center">[39m
[31m+               {error}[39m
[31m+               <button onClick={fetchSummary} className="block mx-auto mt-2 text-xs font-bold underline">Reintentar</button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ) : summary ? ([39m
[31m+             <>[39m
[31m+               {/* Big total */}[39m
[31m+               <div className="text-center pb-2 border-b border-gray-100">[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Total vendido en el turno</p>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-4xl font-black text-primary tracking-tight">{fmt(summary.totalVendido)}</p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2 bg-primary/10 text-primary px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">[39m
[31m+                   <History className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+                   {summary.numVentas} {summary.numVentas === 1 ? "operación" : "operaciones"} de venta[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {/* Payment breakdown - Premium Grid */}[39m
[31m+               <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-xs font-extrabold text-gray-400 pl-1">Desglose de ingresos</p>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-3">[39m
[31m+                   <div className="col-span-2 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between group relative overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="absolute -right-4 -top-4 h-16 w-16 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-xl group-hover:bg-emerald-500/20 transition-all" />[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-3 relative z-10">[39m
[31m+                       <div className="h-10 w-10 bg-emerald-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">[39m
[31m+                         <Banknote className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600" />[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                       <div>[39m
[31m+                         <p className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600/80">Efectivo (Caja)</p>[39m
[31m+                         <p className="text-lg font-black text-emerald-700">{fmt(summary.efectivo)}</p>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-100 rounded-xl p-3 sm:p-4">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">[39m
[31m+                       <div className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-purple-500" />[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs font-bold text-purple-700/80">Yape</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-purple-700">{fmt(summary.yape)}</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-xl p-3 sm:p-4">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">[39m
[31m+                       <div className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700/80">Plin</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-emerald-700">{fmt(summary.plin)}</p>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-xl p-3 sm:p-4">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">[39m
[31m+                       <CreditCard className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-500" />[39m

[31m+ // ── Main Module ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export default function POSCajaModule() {[39m
[31m+   const [sub, setSub] = useState<TabId>(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === "undefined") return TABS[0].id;[39m
[31m+     return normalizeVentasCajaTab(localStorage.getItem(`admin-last-tab-${MODULE_ID}`));[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(`admin-last-tab-${MODULE_ID}`, sub); }, [sub]);[39m
[31m+   const [showShiftClose, setShowShiftClose] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const { pendingCount, isOnline: _isOnline } = usePOSOffline();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Estado de turno abierto ──────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+   const [turnoAbierto, setTurnoAbierto] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Fetch turno activo al montar y al cambiar de tab (por si se abrió/cerró)[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     fetch("/api/turnos/activo")[39m
[31m+       .then(res => res.ok ? res.json() : null)[39m
[31m+       .then(data => { if (!cancelled && data) setTurnoAbierto(!!data.turnoActivo); })[39m
[31m+       .catch(() => { /* non-critical */ });[39m
[31m+     return () => { cancelled = true; };[39m
[31m+   }, [sub]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleOpenCloseModal = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (pendingCount > 0) {[39m
[31m+       alert(`Tienes ${pendingCount} ventas pendientes de sincronizar en modo Offline.\nPor favor, conecta a internet y pulsa "Sincronizar ahora" en la barra azul antes de cerrar el turno. De lo contrario esas ventas no se reflejarán en el corte.`);[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setShowShiftClose(true);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleShiftClosed = () => {[39m
[31m+     setShowShiftClose(false);[39m
[31m+     setTurnoAbierto(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       <OfflineIndicator />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <AdminModuleHeader[39m
[31m+         title="Ventas & Caja"[39m
[31m+         description="Punto de venta, turnos y cobranza"[39m
[31m+         icon={ShoppingCart}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         {/* Badge de turno */}[39m
[31m+         <span className={cn([39m
[31m+           "px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold hidden sm:inline-flex",[39m
[31m+           turnoAbierto ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700" : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-500"[39m
[31m+         )}>[39m
[31m+           {turnoAbierto ? "Turno abierto" : "Sin turno"}[39m
[31m+         </span>[39m
[31m+         {/* Botón Cerrar/Abrir Turno contextual (desktop) */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="hidden sm:block">[39m
[31m+           {turnoAbierto ? ([39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={handleOpenCloseModal}[39m
[31m+               className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-red-600 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 border border-red-200 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Cerrar Turno[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => setSub("turnos")}[39m
[31m+               className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-white bg-primary hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Abrir Turno[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </AdminModuleHeader>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <AdminTabBar[39m
[31m+         tabs={TABS.map(t => ({[39m
[31m+           id: t.id,[39m
[31m+           label: t.label,[39m
[31m+           shortLabel: t.shortLabel,[39m
[31m+           icon: t.icon,[39m
[31m+         }))}[39m
[31m+         activeTab={sub}[39m
[31m+         onTabChange={(id) => setSub(id as TabId)}[39m
[31m+         moduleId="pos-caja"[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── Mobile Cerrar/Abrir Turno button — fixed at bottom ───────── */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="sm:hidden fixed bottom-16 right-4 z-40">[39m
[31m+         {turnoAbierto ? ([39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={handleOpenCloseModal}[39m
[31m+             className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-white/70 animate-pulse" />[39m
[31m+             Cerrar Turno[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <button[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => setSub("turnos")}[39m
[31m+             className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-primary hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             Abrir Turno[39m
[31m+           </button>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ── CAMBIO 7: Renderizado de contenido por tab ───────────────── */}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "pos"               && <POSView />}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "turnos"            && <TurnosModule />}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "caja-registradora" && <CashRegisterTab />}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "cuentas-cobrar"    && <FiadosModule />}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "arqueo"            && <CashAuditTab onNavigateToTurnos={() => setSub("caja-registradora")} />}[39m
[31m+       {sub === "comisiones"        && <CommissionCalculator />}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Shift close modal */}[39m
[31m+       {showShiftClose && ([39m
[31m+         <ShiftCloseModal[39m
[31m+           onClose={() => setShowShiftClose(false)}[39m
[31m+           onConfirm={handleShiftClosed}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       </AdminTabBar>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   { id: "historial", label: "Historial", icon: BarChart3 },[39m
[31m+   { id: "salud", label: "Salud del Sistema", icon: HeartPulse },[39m
[31m+   { id: "navegador", label: "Mi Navegador", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+   { id: "almacenamiento", label: "Almacenamiento", icon: HardDrive },[39m
[31m+ ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Browser Info Tab ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function BrowserInfoTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [info, _setInfo] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === "undefined") return {} as Record<string, string>;[39m
[31m+     const nav = navigator as Navigator & Record<string, unknown>;[39m
[31m+     const conn = (nav as Record<string, unknown>).connection || (nav as Record<string, unknown>).mozConnection || (nav as Record<string, unknown>).webkitConnection;[39m
[31m+     const connObj = conn as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;[39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       "Navegador": (nav.userAgent?.split(" ").pop()) || "Desconocido",[39m
[31m+       "Plataforma": nav.platform || "Desconocido",[39m
[31m+       "Idioma": nav.language || "es",[39m
[31m+       "Pantalla": `${screen.width} × ${screen.height} px`,[39m
[31m+       "Ventana actual": `${window.innerWidth} × ${window.innerHeight} px`,[39m
[31m+       "Pixeles por punto": `${window.devicePixelRatio}x`,[39m
[31m+       "Modo oscuro del sistema": window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches ? "Sí" : "No",[39m
[31m+       "Conexión": (connObj?.effectiveType as string)?.toUpperCase() || "Desconocida",[39m
[31m+       "Velocidad estimada": connObj?.downlink ? `${connObj.downlink} Mbps` : "No disponible",[39m
[31m+       "Ahorro de datos": connObj?.saveData ? "Activado" : "Desactivado",[39m
[31m+       "Memoria RAM": (nav as Record<string, unknown>).deviceMemory ? `~${(nav as Record<string, unknown>).deviceMemory} GB` : "No disponible",[39m
[31m+       "Núcleos CPU": nav.hardwareConcurrency ? `${nav.hardwareConcurrency} núcleos` : "No disponible",[39m
[31m+       "Cookies habilitadas": nav.cookieEnabled ? "Sí" : "No",[39m
[31m+       "Modo seguro (HTTPS)": location.protocol === "https:" ? "Sí" : "No",[39m
[31m+       "Service Worker": "serviceWorker" in nav ? "Disponible" : "No disponible",[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const entries = Object.entries(info);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Connection quality card */}[39m
[31m+       <ConnectionQualityCard />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Browser details */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+         <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">[39m
[31m+           <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+             <Monitor className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+             Información del Navegador[39m
[31m+           </h3>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">[39m
[31m+             Datos técnicos de tu dispositivo — como la ficha técnica de tu computadora[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">[39m
[31m+           {entries.map(([key, value]) => ([39m
[31m+             <div key={key} className="px-4 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-500">{key}</span>[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-900 text-right">{value}</span>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Connection Quality Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function ConnectionQualityCard() {[39m
[31m+   const [quality, setQuality] = useState<{[39m
[31m+     type: string;[39m
[31m+     speed: number;[39m
[31m+     rtt: number;[39m
[31m+     grade: "buena" | "regular" | "lenta";[39m
[31m+   } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     const conn = (navigator as Navigator & Record<string, unknown>).connection as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;[39m
[31m+     if (!conn) return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const update = () => {[39m
[31m+       const speed = (conn.downlink as number) || 0;[39m
[31m+       const rtt = (conn.rtt as number) || 0;[39m
[31m+       const type = (conn.effectiveType as string) || "unknown";[39m
[31m+       const grade = speed >= 5 ? "buena" : speed >= 1.5 ? "regular" : "lenta";[39m
[31m+       setQuality({ type: type.toUpperCase(), speed, rtt, grade });[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     update();[39m
[31m+     const target = conn as unknown as EventTarget;[39m
[31m+     target.addEventListener("change", update);[39m
[31m+     return () => target.removeEventListener("change", update);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!quality) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 text-center">[39m
[31m+         <Wifi className="h-6 w-6 text-gray-400 mx-auto mb-2" />[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+           Tu navegador no permite medir la calidad de conexión[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const GRADE_STYLES = {[39m
[31m+     buena: { color: "text-emerald-600", bg: "bg-emerald-50", dot: "bg-emerald-500" },[39m
[31m+     regular: { color: "text-amber-600", bg: "bg-amber-50", dot: "bg-amber-500" },[39m
[31m+     lenta: { color: "text-red-600", bg: "bg-red-50", dot: "bg-red-500" },[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+   const style = GRADE_STYLES[quality.grade];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className={cn("rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-4", style.bg)}>[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+           <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl bg-white flex items-center justify-center ">[39m
[31m+             <Wifi className={cn("h-5 w-5", style.color)} />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div>[39m
[31m+             <p className={cn("text-sm font-bold", style.color)}>[39m
[31m+               <span className={cn("inline-block w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full", style.dot)} /> Conexión {quality.grade}[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+               {quality.grade === "buena"[39m
[31m+                 ? "Tu internet va rápido — la tienda cargará bien"[39m
[31m+                 : quality.grade === "regular"[39m
[31m+                   ? "Tu internet va normal — puede tardar un poco"[39m
[31m+                   : "Tu internet va lento — las páginas tardarán en cargar"}[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="text-right">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">{quality.speed} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-500">Mbps</span></p>[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{quality.type} · {quality.rtt}ms</p>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Performance History Tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface PerfSnapshot {[39m
[31m+   date: string; // ISO date[39m
[31m+   lcp: number;[39m
[31m+   fid: number;[39m
[31m+   cls: number;[39m
[31m+   ttfb: number;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const PERF_HISTORY_KEY = "buleje-perf-history";[39m
[31m+ const MAX_SNAPSHOTS = 14;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function getHistory(): PerfSnapshot[] {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const raw = localStorage.getItem(PERF_HISTORY_KEY);[39m
[31m+     return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];[39m
[31m+   } catch { return []; }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function saveHistory(snapshots: PerfSnapshot[]) {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     localStorage.setItem(PERF_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(snapshots.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOTS)));[39m
[31m+   } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function PerformanceHistoryTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [history, setHistory] = useState<PerfSnapshot[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setHistory(getHistory());[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const recordNow = () => {[39m
[31m+     setRecording(true);[39m
[31m+     const snap: PerfSnapshot = {[39m
[31m+       date: new Date().toISOString(),[39m
[31m+       lcp: 0, fid: 0, cls: 0, ttfb: 0,[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // Read performance entries[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const entries = performance.getEntriesByType("navigation");[39m
[31m+       if (entries.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+         const nav = entries[0] as PerformanceNavigationTiming;[39m
[31m+         snap.ttfb = Math.round(nav.responseStart - nav.requestStart);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // LCP from PerformanceObserver (last recorded)[39m
[31m+       const lcpEntries = performance.getEntriesByType("largest-contentful-paint");[39m
[31m+       if (lcpEntries.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+         snap.lcp = Math.round(lcpEntries[lcpEntries.length - 1].startTime);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // CLS approximation[39m
[31m+       const layoutShifts = performance.getEntriesByType("layout-shift");[39m
[31m+       let clsValue = 0;[39m
[31m+       for (const entry of layoutShifts) {[39m
[31m+         if (!(entry as PerformanceEntry & { hadRecentInput?: boolean }).hadRecentInput) {[39m
[31m+           clsValue += (entry as PerformanceEntry & { value?: number }).value || 0;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       snap.cls = Math.round(clsValue * 1000) / 1000;[39m
[31m+     } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const next = [...history, snap];[39m
[31m+     saveHistory(next);[39m
[31m+     setHistory(next);[39m
[31m+     setRecording(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const clearHistory = () => {[39m
[31m+     try { localStorage.removeItem(PERF_HISTORY_KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+     setHistory([]);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getGrade = (metric: string, value: number): { color: string; label: string } => {[39m
[31m+     if (metric === "lcp") {[39m
[31m+       if (value <= 2500) return { color: "bg-emerald-500", label: "Bueno" };[39m
[31m+       if (value <= 4000) return { color: "bg-amber-500", label: "Medio" };[39m
[31m+       return { color: "bg-red-500", label: "Lento" };[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (metric === "ttfb") {[39m
[31m+       if (value <= 200) return { color: "bg-emerald-500", label: "Rápido" };[39m
[31m+       if (value <= 500) return { color: "bg-amber-500", label: "Normal" };[39m
[31m+       return { color: "bg-red-500", label: "Lento" };[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (metric === "cls") {[39m
[31m+       if (value <= 0.1) return { color: "bg-emerald-500", label: "Estable" };[39m
[31m+       if (value <= 0.25) return { color: "bg-amber-500", label: "Algo inestable" };[39m
[31m+       return { color: "bg-red-500", label: "Inestable" };[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return { color: "bg-gray-400", label: "-" };[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Find max for bar scaling[39m
[31m+   const maxLcp = Math.max(...history.map(h => h.lcp), 1);[39m
[31m+   const maxTtfb = Math.max(...history.map(h => h.ttfb), 1);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Record button */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">[39m
[31m+           <div>[39m
[31m+             <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               <BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+               Historial de Rendimiento[39m
[31m+             </h3>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">[39m
[31m+               Como un diario de salud de tu tienda — registra una medición cada vez que quieras ver cómo va[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+             {history.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+               <button[39m
[31m+                 onClick={clearHistory}[39m
[31m+                 className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                 title="Borrar historial"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+               </button>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={recordNow}[39m
[31m+               disabled={recording}[39m
[31m+               className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-primary text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-primary/90 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {recording ? "Midiendo..." : <><BarChart3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 inline -mt-0.5" /> Medir ahora</>}[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {history.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+           <div className="text-center py-8">[39m
[31m+             <BarChart3 className="h-8 w-8 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-2" />[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+               Aún no hay mediciones. Presiona &quot;Medir ahora&quot; para empezar.[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+             {/* LCP chart */}[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5">[39m
[31m+                 LCP — Tiempo de carga principal (ms)[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+               <div className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+                 {history.map((snap, i) => {[39m
[31m+                   const grade = getGrade("lcp", snap.lcp);[39m
[31m+                   const width = Math.max((snap.lcp / maxLcp) * 100, 5);[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 w-14 shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                         {new Date(snap.date).toLocaleDateString("es", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" })}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <div className="flex-1 h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+                         <div[39m
[31m+                           className={cn("h-full rounded-full transition-all", grade.color)}[39m
[31m+                           style={{ width: `${width}%` }}[39m
[31m+                         />[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-700 w-14 text-right">[39m
[31m+                         {snap.lcp}ms[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 })}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             {/* TTFB chart */}[39m
[31m+             <div>[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5">[39m
[31m+                 TTFB — Tiempo de respuesta del servidor (ms)[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+               <div className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+                 {history.map((snap, i) => {[39m
[31m+                   const grade = getGrade("ttfb", snap.ttfb);[39m
[31m+                   const width = Math.max((snap.ttfb / maxTtfb) * 100, 5);[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 w-14 shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                         {new Date(snap.date).toLocaleDateString("es", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" })}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <div className="flex-1 h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+                         <div[39m
[31m+                           className={cn("h-full rounded-full transition-all", grade.color)}[39m
[31m+                           style={{ width: `${width}%` }}[39m
[31m+                         />[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-700 w-14 text-right">[39m
[31m+                         {snap.ttfb}ms[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 })}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             {/* CLS summary */}[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100">[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 Última estabilidad visual (CLS):[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+               {history.length > 0 && (() => {[39m
[31m+                 const last = history[history.length - 1];[39m
[31m+                 const grade = getGrade("cls", last.cls);[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <span className={cn("text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-white", grade.color)}>[39m
[31m+                     {last.cls} — {grade.label}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               })()}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Storage Tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function StorageTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [storage, setStorage] = useState<{[39m
[31m+     quota: number;[39m
[31m+     usage: number;[39m
[31m+     caches: number;[39m
[31m+     localStorage: number;[39m
[31m+     sessionStorage: number;[39m
[31m+   } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     async function measure() {[39m
[31m+       const data = { quota: 0, usage: 0, caches: 0, localStorage: 0, sessionStorage: 0 };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Storage API[39m
[31m+       if (navigator.storage?.estimate) {[39m
[31m+         const est = await navigator.storage.estimate();[39m
[31m+         data.quota = est.quota || 0;[39m
[31m+         data.usage = est.usage || 0;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Cache API[39m
[31m+       if ("caches" in window) {[39m
[31m+         try {[39m
[31m+           const names = await caches.keys();[39m
[31m+           let total = 0;[39m
[31m+           for (const name of names) {[39m
[31m+             const cache = await caches.open(name);[39m
[31m+             const keys = await cache.keys();[39m
[31m+             total += keys.length;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           data.caches = total;[39m
[31m+         } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // localStorage size estimate[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         let lsSize = 0;[39m
[31m+         for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {[39m
[31m+           const key = localStorage.key(i);[39m
[31m+           if (key) lsSize += (localStorage.getItem(key)?.length || 0) * 2; // UTF-16[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         data.localStorage = lsSize;[39m
[31m+       } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // sessionStorage size estimate[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         let ssSize = 0;[39m
[31m+         for (let i = 0; i < sessionStorage.length; i++) {[39m
[31m+           const key = sessionStorage.key(i);[39m
[31m+           if (key) ssSize += (sessionStorage.getItem(key)?.length || 0) * 2;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         data.sessionStorage = ssSize;[39m
[31m+       } catch { /* ignore */ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       setStorage(data);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     measure();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {[39m
[31m+     if (bytes === 0) return "0 B";[39m
[31m+     const k = 1024;[39m
[31m+     const sizes = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];[39m
[31m+     const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));[39m
[31m+     return `${parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1))} ${sizes[i]}`;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!storage) {[39m
[31m+     return <S />;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const usagePct = storage.quota > 0 ? (storage.usage / storage.quota) * 100 : 0;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Storage overview */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4">[39m
[31m+         <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">[39m
[31m+           <HardDrive className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+           Espacio utilizado[39m
[31m+         </h3>[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-3">[39m
[31m+           Como el espacio en tu celular — muestra cuánto está usando la tienda en tu navegador[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Progress bar */}[39m
[31m+         <div className="relative h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden mb-2">[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className={cn([39m
[31m+               "absolute inset-y-0 left-0 rounded-full transition-all duration-500",[39m
[31m+               usagePct < 50 ? "bg-emerald-500" : usagePct < 80 ? "bg-amber-500" : "bg-red-500"[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             style={{ width: `${Math.min(usagePct, 100)}%` }}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">[39m
[31m+             {formatBytes(storage.usage)} usado[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+             de {formatBytes(storage.quota)} disponible[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Detail cards */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+         <StorageCard[39m
[31m+           icon={HardDrive}[39m
[31m+           label="Datos guardados"[39m
[31m+           value={formatBytes(storage.localStorage)}[39m
[31m+           description="Configuración, preferencias y datos temporales"[39m
[31m+           color="text-emerald-500"[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+         <StorageCard[39m
[31m+           icon={MemoryStick}[39m
[31m+           label="Sesión actual"[39m
[31m+           value={formatBytes(storage.sessionStorage)}[39m
[31m+           description="Datos del momento — se borran al cerrar"[39m
[31m+           color="text-purple-500"[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+         <StorageCard[39m
[31m+           icon={Globe}[39m
[31m+           label="Caché del sitio"[39m
[31m+           value={`${storage.caches} archivos`}[39m
[31m+           description="Archivos guardados para cargar más rápido"[39m
[31m+           color="text-emerald-500"[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ // ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface Customer {[39m
[31m+   id: string | number;[39m
[31m+   name: string;[39m
[31m+   phone?: string;[39m
[31m+   totalSpent?: number;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface Recommendation {[39m
[31m+   productId: string | number;[39m
[31m+   name: string;[39m
[31m+   price: number;[39m
[31m+   reason?: string;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface SaleItem {[39m
[31m+   name?: string;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface SaleRecord {[39m
[31m+   items?: SaleItem[];[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface CrossPair {[39m
[31m+   a: string;[39m
[31m+   b: string;[39m
[31m+   count: number;[39m
[31m+   confidence: number;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function fmtPrice(n: number) {[39m
[31m+   return `S/${n.toLocaleString("es-PE", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}`;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function buildWhatsAppText(customerName: string, recs: Recommendation[]): string {[39m
[31m+   const lista = recs[39m
[31m+     .slice(0, 5)[39m
[31m+     .map((r) => `- ${r.name} (${fmtPrice(r.price)})`)[39m
[31m+     .join("\n");[39m
[31m+   return `Hola ${customerName}! Te recomendamos:\n${lista}\nVisitanos en Buleje. 🛒`;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function computeCrossPairs(sales: SaleRecord[]): CrossPair[] {[39m
[31m+   const coMap: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+   const totalSales = sales.length || 1;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   for (const sale of sales) {[39m
[31m+     const items = sale.items ?? [];[39m
[31m+     const names = items.map((i) => (i.name ?? "").trim()).filter(Boolean);[39m
[31m+     if (names.length < 2) continue;[39m
[31m+     for (let a = 0; a < names.length; a++) {[39m
[31m+       for (let b = a + 1; b < names.length; b++) {[39m
[31m+         const key = [names[a], names[b]].sort().join("|||");[39m
[31m+         coMap[key] = (coMap[key] ?? 0) + 1;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return Object.entries(coMap)[39m
[31m+     .sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1])[39m
[31m+     .slice(0, 10)[39m
[31m+     .map(([key, count]) => {[39m
[31m+       const [a, b] = key.split("|||");[39m
[31m+       return { a, b, count, confidence: Math.round((count / totalSales) * 100) };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Sub-tab: Para clientes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m

[31m+ function TabClientes() {[39m

[31m+   const [results, setResults] = useState<Customer[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loadingSearch, setLoadingSearch] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Customer | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [recs, setRecs] = useState<Recommendation[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loadingRecs, setLoadingRecs] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const debounceRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSearchChange = useCallback((val: string) => {[39m
[31m+     setSearch(val);[39m
[31m+     setSelected(null);[39m
[31m+     setRecs([]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);[39m
[31m+     if (val.trim().length < 2) {[39m
[31m+       setResults([]);[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     debounceRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {[39m
[31m+       setLoadingSearch(true);[39m
[31m+       try {[39m

[31m+         if (res.ok) {[39m

[31m+           setResults(data.customers ?? data ?? []);[39m
[31m+         }[39m

[31m+[39m
