[31m+     setResults([]);[39m
[31m+     setSearch(customer.name);[39m
[31m+     setLoadingRecs(true);[39m
[31m+     setRecs([]);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const phone = customer.phone ?? "";[39m
[31m+       const res = await fetch(`/api/recommendations?phone=${encodeURIComponent(phone)}&limit=8`);[39m
[31m+       if (res.ok) {[39m
[31m+         const data = await res.json();[39m
[31m+         setRecs(data.recommendations ?? data ?? []);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       setRecs([]);[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setLoadingRecs(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleWhatsApp = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!selected?.phone || recs.length === 0) return;[39m
[31m+     const texto = buildWhatsAppText(selected.name, recs);[39m
[31m+     const phone = selected.phone.replace(/\D/g, "");[39m
[31m+     window.open(`https://wa.me/${phone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(texto)}`, "_blank");[39m
[31m+   }, [selected, recs]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-6">[39m
[31m+       {/* Buscador */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative">[39m
[31m+         <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none" />[39m
[31m+         <input[39m
[31m+           type="text"[39m
[31m+           value={search}[39m
[31m+           onChange={(e) => handleSearchChange(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+           placeholder="Buscar cliente por nombre o teléfono..."[39m
[31m+           className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-sm text-gray-900 placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/40"[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+         {loadingSearch && ([39m
[31m+           <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2">[39m
[31m+             <div className="h-4 w-4 border-2 border-primary border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Resultados de búsqueda */}[39m
[31m+       {results.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+         <ul className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white divide-y divide-gray-100  overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+           {results.map((c) => ([39m
[31m+             <li key={String(c.id)}>[39m
[31m+               <button[39m
[31m+                 onClick={() => handleSelectCustomer(c)}[39m
[31m+                 className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 hover:bg-primary/5 transition-colors text-left min-h-[44px]"[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 <div>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">{c.name}</p>[39m
[31m+                   {c.phone && ([39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{c.phone}</p>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 {c.totalSpent !== undefined && ([39m
[31m+                   <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary shrink-0">[39m

[31m+                   </span>[39m

[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           {loadingRecs ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m
[31m+               {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <div key={i} className="h-20 rounded-xl bg-gray-100 animate-pulse" />[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ) : recs.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-200 p-8 text-center">[39m
[31m+               <Lightbulb className="h-8 w-8 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-2" />[39m
[31m+               <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 No hay recomendaciones disponibles para este cliente.[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">[39m

[31m+                 <div[39m
[31m+                   key={String(r.productId)}[39m
[31m+                   className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 space-y-1"[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 leading-tight">[39m
[31m+                     {r.name}[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m

[31m+                     {fmtPrice(r.price)}[39m

[31m+                     <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{r.reason}</p>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m

[31m+       {/* Estado vacío inicial */}[39m
[31m+       {!selected && results.length === 0 && search.trim().length < 2 && ([39m
[31m+         <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-200 p-10 text-center">[39m
[31m+           <Users className="h-10 w-10 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-3" />[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+             Busca un cliente para ver sus recomendaciones personalizadas[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m

[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // ── Sub-tab: Cross-sell ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function TabCrossSell() {[39m
[31m+   const [pairs, setPairs] = useState<CrossPair[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [error, setError] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     async function load() {[39m
[31m+       setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+       setError(false);[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const res = await fetch("/api/sales?limit=200");[39m
[31m+         if (!res.ok) throw new Error("error");[39m
[31m+         const data: SaleRecord[] = await res.json();[39m
[31m+         if (!cancelled) setPairs(computeCrossPairs(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []));[39m
[31m+       } catch {[39m
[31m+         if (!cancelled) setError(true);[39m
[31m+       } finally {[39m
[31m+         if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     void load();[39m
[31m+     return () => { cancelled = true; };[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleCreateCombo = useCallback((pair: CrossPair) => {[39m
[31m+     window.location.href = `/admin/bundles?a=${encodeURIComponent(pair.a)}&b=${encodeURIComponent(pair.b)}`;[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+         {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <div key={i} className="h-12 rounded-xl bg-gray-100 animate-pulse" />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (error) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-6 text-center">[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-red-600 font-semibold">[39m
[31m+           No se pudieron cargar las ventas para analizar.[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (pairs.length === 0) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-200 p-10 text-center">[39m
[31m+         <GitMerge className="h-10 w-10 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-3" />[39m
[31m+         <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 font-semibold">[39m
[31m+           No hay suficientes ventas con múltiples productos para analizar.[39m
[31m+         </p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+         Top 10 pares de productos vendidos juntos con mayor frecuencia — últimas 200 ventas.[39m
[31m+       </p>[39m
[31m+       <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-gray-200">[39m
[31m+         <table className="w-full text-sm">[39m
[31m+           <thead>[39m
[31m+             <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">[39m
[31m+               <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 Producto A[39m
[31m+               </th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 Producto B[39m
[31m+               </th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="text-right px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 Juntos[39m
[31m+               </th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="text-right px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">[39m
[31m+                 Confianza[39m
[31m+               </th>[39m
[31m+               <th className="px-4 py-2.5" />[39m
[31m+             </tr>[39m
[31m+           </thead>[39m
[31m+           <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 bg-white">[39m
[31m+             {pairs.map((pair, idx) => ([39m
[31m+               <tr key={idx} className="hover:bg-primary/5 transition-colors">[39m
